-- Older runs may still have deferred dropdown cleanup waiting on this rerun.
-- Make their unregister method safe before destroying the previous shell.
do
    pcall(function()
        local environment = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or _G
        local previous = environment.__SyllinseReptillianDropdownLibrary
        if type(previous) == "table" then
            previous.UnregisterOpenFrame = function(self, frame)
                if type(self.OpenFrames) == "table" then self.OpenFrames[frame] = nil end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local existing = playerGui:FindFirstChild("SyllinseUI")
if existing then
    existing:Destroy()
end

-- Use a consistent native FontFace family. Explicit weights avoid mismatched
-- legacy enum-font substitutions that can change spacing and letter shapes.
local FONT_REGULAR = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular)
local FONT_MEDIUM = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium)
local FONT_SEMIBOLD = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold)
local FONT_BOLD = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)

-- THEME SETUP
-- Edit the entries below. Copy one to add a theme and give it a new Id.
-- Keep Id stable for saved profiles. Set Name to change the dropdown label.
-- Media accepts direct HTTPS JPEG, PNG, GIF, MP4 and WebM file URLs.
-- For direct playback, set Media to an uploaded Roblox video URI (rbxassetid://...).
-- For animation, optionally set Photo to a still JPEG or PNG fallback.
-- 0 is opaque and 1 is invisible for all transparency fields.
-- For uploaded Roblox sprite sheets, add Sprite inside a preset:
-- Sprite = { Pages = { "rbxassetid://123456789" }, FrameCount = 16 },
-- Defaults are 4 columns x 4 rows, 256 x 160 pixels per cell and 12 FPS.
-- Optional overrides: Columns, Rows, CellWidth, CellHeight and Fps.
-- Sprite takes priority over Media. Photo can be a still fallback.
-- For an existing sprite sheet, Media can be "GIF_URL | PNG_SHEET_URL".
-- The GIF supplies exact frame delays. The PNG supplies the original pixels.
-- Use hq://NAME for media prepared with Prepare-SyllinseBackground.ps1.
-- saved://ID entries are maintained by the Saved backgrounds dropdown.
-- Direct media URLs continue using the hosted converter.

Theme.Style = Theme.Setup.Style
for _, preset in ipairs(Theme.Setup.Presets) do
    if preset.Id == "Original" then
        Theme.Root = preset.Root
        Theme.Surface = preset.Surface
        Theme.Text = preset.Text
        Theme.Accent = preset.Accent
        Theme.IconActive = preset.Accent
        Theme.TabIcons = preset.TabIcons
        Theme.ActiveTabIcon = preset.ActiveTabIcon
        Theme.Stroke = preset.Border or Theme.Surface:Lerp(Theme.Accent, Theme.Style.BorderBlend)
        Theme.Divider = preset.Divider or Theme.Stroke
        break
    end
end

-- Keep the original subtle header fill, derived from the secondary color.
local function getPanelHeaderColor()
    return Theme.Surface:Lerp(Theme.Text, 0.035)
end

-- Opaque text glyphs look brighter than tinted icon artwork at the same RGB value.
-- Blend terminology text tints back toward the charcoal root so they visually match the icons,
-- including when the user changes the active accent color.
-- ================================================================
-- MANUAL UI SIZE + CORNER SETTINGS
-- Edit these values directly, then execute the ENTIRE script again.
-- Switch Width/Height/ThumbSize control the visible switch dimensions.
-- Corner values are pixel radii; set 0 for square corners.
-- The script prints a confirmation when the first real switch receives these values.
-- ================================================================

local function getSwitchLabelFontFace()
    local weight = string.lower(tostring(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.LabelFontWeight or "Medium"))
    if weight == "regular" then
        return FONT_REGULAR
    elseif weight == "semibold" or weight == "semi-bold" then
        return FONT_SEMIBOLD
    elseif weight == "bold" then
        return FONT_BOLD
    end
    return FONT_MEDIUM
end

local function setManualCorner(instance, radius)
    if not instance or not instance:IsA("GuiObject") then return nil end
    local corner = instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
    if not corner then
        corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.Parent = instance
    end
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, math.max(0, tonumber(radius) or 0))
    return corner
end

local function terminologyTint(iconTint, strength)
    local base = Theme.Root
    return Color3.new(
        base.R + (iconTint.R - base.R) * strength,
        base.G + (iconTint.G - base.G) * strength,
        base.B + (iconTint.B - base.B) * strength
    )
end

local function addCorner(instance, radius)
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
    corner.Parent = instance
    return corner
end

local function addStroke(instance, color, transparency, thickness)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = color or Theme.Stroke
    stroke.Transparency = transparency or 0
    stroke.Thickness = thickness or 1
    stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    stroke.Parent = instance
    return stroke
end

local function isPopupInteractionBlocked(object)
    return Lumen and Lumen._SyllinsePopupBlocksInteraction
        and Lumen._SyllinsePopupBlocksInteraction(object) or false
end

local function makeFrame(name, parent, size, position, color, transparency)
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = name
    frame.Size = size
    frame.Position = position
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = color or Theme.Surface
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = transparency or 0
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = parent
    return frame
end

local function makeText(name, parent, text, size, position, fontSize, color, font, alignment)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = name
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    label.Text = text
    label.Size = size
    label.Position = position
    label.FontFace = font or FONT_REGULAR
    label.TextScaled = false
    label.RichText = false
    label.AutoLocalize = false
    label.TextSize = fontSize or 14
    label.TextColor3 = color or Theme.Text
    label.TextXAlignment = alignment or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    label.Parent = parent
    return label
end

local function tween(instance, duration, properties)
    local animation = TweenService:Create(
        instance,
        TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        properties
    )
    animation:Play()
    return animation
end

-- Screen GUI
local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "SyllinseUI"
gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
gui.DisplayOrder = 30
gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
gui.Parent = playerGui

-- MacLib-style mobile keybind support, without its window or widget styling:
-- this overlay is only populated on touch devices and remains hidden until enabled.
local previousActionGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("SyllinseMobileActions")
if previousActionGui then previousActionGui:Destroy() end
local mobileActionGui
if touchInputEnabled then
    mobileActionGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    mobileActionGui.Name = "SyllinseMobileActions"
    mobileActionGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    mobileActionGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    mobileActionGui.DisplayOrder = gui.DisplayOrder + 2
    mobileActionGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    mobileActionGui.Parent = playerGui
end

local baseWindowSize = UDim2.fromOffset(970, 670)
local root = makeFrame(
    "Window",
    gui,
    baseWindowSize,
    UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
    Theme.Root
)
root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
root.ClipsDescendants = true
setManualCorner(root, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Window)
local shellStroke = addStroke(root, Theme.Stroke, 1, 1) -- Base border beneath Pure Black's animated outer highlight.

local uiScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
uiScale.Name = "ResponsiveScale"
uiScale.Parent = root

-- One root UIScale handles responsive sizing and the subtle, centered open/close motion.
local responsiveScaleValue = 1
-- Use a normal 100% maximum. updateScale() automatically reduces it only when
-- the actual safe viewport cannot fit the current shell.
local windowSizePercent = 100
local windowSizeMultiplier = 1.0
local compactTouchLayout = false
local mobileSafeOffsetY = 0
-- Once manually dragged, preserve the shell position across close/open cycles.
local windowHasCustomPosition = false
local layoutResponsiveShell
local windowMotionFactor = Instance.new("NumberValue")
windowMotionFactor.Name = "WindowMotionFactor"
windowMotionFactor.Value = 0.90
windowMotionFactor.Changed:Connect(function(value)
    -- Keep the open/close motion layered on top of the current responsive size.
    uiScale.Scale = responsiveScaleValue * value
end)
root.Visible = false -- shown by the initial Syllinse-branded opening transition near the end

-- Scale the visible Syllinse/Lumen shell with one root UIScale, matching Lumen's
-- own container-level scaling approach. Keep nativeGeometryScale for popups that
-- render in their own ScreenGui and therefore sit outside this root transform.
local nativeGeometryScale = 1
local nativeScaledFactors = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local nativeTextBaseSizes = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function scaleUDim(value, ratio)
    return UDim.new(value.Scale, value.Offset * ratio)
end

local function scaleUDim2(value, ratio)
    return UDim2.new(
        value.X.Scale, value.X.Offset * ratio,
        value.Y.Scale, value.Y.Offset * ratio
    )
end

local function applyNativeScaleToInstance(instance, targetScale, preserveScreenPosition)
    local previousScale = nativeScaledFactors[instance] or 1
    if math.abs(previousScale - targetScale) < 0.0001 then
        return
    end
    local ratio = targetScale / previousScale

    if instance:IsA("GuiObject") then
        if not preserveScreenPosition then
            instance.Position = scaleUDim2(instance.Position, ratio)
        end
        instance.Size = scaleUDim2(instance.Size, ratio)
        if instance:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
            instance.CanvasSize = scaleUDim2(instance.CanvasSize, ratio)
            instance.CanvasPosition = instance.CanvasPosition * ratio
            -- Keep the scrollbar thin; scaling it made it visually heavy.
        end
    end

    if instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton") or instance:IsA("TextBox") then
        if nativeTextBaseSizes[instance] == nil then
            nativeTextBaseSizes[instance] = instance.TextSize
        end
        instance.TextSize = math.max(1, math.floor(nativeTextBaseSizes[instance] * targetScale + 0.5))
    elseif instance:IsA("UICorner") then
        instance.CornerRadius = scaleUDim(instance.CornerRadius, ratio)
    elseif instance:IsA("UIStroke") then
        instance.Thickness = math.max(0.5, instance.Thickness * ratio)
    elseif instance:IsA("UIPadding") then
        instance.PaddingLeft = scaleUDim(instance.PaddingLeft, ratio)
        instance.PaddingRight = scaleUDim(instance.PaddingRight, ratio)
        instance.PaddingTop = scaleUDim(instance.PaddingTop, ratio)
        instance.PaddingBottom = scaleUDim(instance.PaddingBottom, ratio)
    elseif instance:IsA("UIListLayout") or instance:IsA("UIPageLayout") then
        instance.Padding = scaleUDim(instance.Padding, ratio)
    elseif instance:IsA("UIGridLayout") then
        instance.CellSize = scaleUDim2(instance.CellSize, ratio)
        instance.CellPadding = scaleUDim2(instance.CellPadding, ratio)
    elseif instance:IsA("UISizeConstraint") then
        instance.MinSize = instance.MinSize * ratio
        instance.MaxSize = instance.MaxSize * ratio
    end

    nativeScaledFactors[instance] = targetScale
end

local function setNativeGeometryScale(targetScale)
    -- The shell itself owns the scale transform. Do not rewrite every descendant's
    -- offsets/sizes: Lumen uses UIListLayout and AutomaticSize to place its controls.
    targetScale = math.clamp(targetScale, 0.001, 1.5)
    nativeGeometryScale = targetScale -- retained for separately-rendered Lumen popups
    uiScale.Scale = targetScale * windowMotionFactor.Value
end

local function refreshNativeResponsiveLayout()
    root.Size = baseWindowSize
    setNativeGeometryScale(responsiveScaleValue)
    if layoutResponsiveShell then
        layoutResponsiveShell()
    end
end

-- Scale the current shell uniformly into the viewport.
local function updateScale(preserveWindowPosition)
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize

    -- A manual size-slider edit must never rewrite root.Position. Keep its exact
    -- UDim2 value so the shell stays where the user left it while UIScale changes.
    -- Viewport/orientation updates may still recenter or clamp the shell normally.

    -- Read all four safe-area insets. The previous calculation only used the
    -- vertical values, which could leave the shell offset or clipped on devices
    -- with asymmetric cutouts / rounded display edges.
    local safeLeft, safeTop, safeRight, safeBottom = 0, 0, 0, 0
    local okInset, topLeft, bottomRight = pcall(function()
        return GuiService:GetGuiInset()
    end)
    if okInset then
        if typeof(topLeft) == "Vector2" then
            safeLeft = math.max(0, topLeft.X)
            safeTop = math.max(0, topLeft.Y)
        end
        if typeof(bottomRight) == "Vector2" then
            safeRight = math.max(0, bottomRight.X)
            safeBottom = math.max(0, bottomRight.Y)
        end
    end

    local touchDevice = touchInputEnabled
    -- Use the maximum safe display area. Keep only a 4px mobile edge gap so the
    -- shell can grow as large as possible without touching/crossing the screen edge.
    local horizontalMargin = touchDevice and 4 or 18
    local verticalMargin = touchDevice and 4 or 18
    local availableWidth = math.max(1,
        viewport.X - safeLeft - safeRight - horizontalMargin * 2)
    local availableHeight = math.max(1,
        viewport.Y - safeTop - safeBottom - verticalMargin * 2)

    -- Find the largest proportional scale that fits in both dimensions:
    -- no stretching and no top/bottom/side cropping.
    local fittedScale = math.min(availableWidth / baseWindowSize.X.Offset, availableHeight / baseWindowSize.Y.Offset)
    -- The UI scale slider now maps directly to the actual scale:
    -- 100 means the original authored size, with no hidden enlargement factor.
    local requestedScale = math.min(windowSizeMultiplier, 1.0)
    responsiveScaleValue = math.max(0.001, math.min(fittedScale, requestedScale))
    refreshNativeResponsiveLayout()

    -- Only viewport/orientation updates are allowed to reposition the shell.
    -- Editing UI scale leaves root.Position byte-for-byte unchanged.
    if not preserveWindowPosition then
        mobileSafeOffsetY = math.floor((safeTop - safeBottom) / 2)
        local safeOffsetX = math.floor((safeLeft - safeRight) / 2)
        if not windowHasCustomPosition then
            root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
                viewport.X * 0.5 + safeOffsetX,
                viewport.Y * 0.5 + mobileSafeOffsetY
            )
        end
    end

    uiScale.Scale = responsiveScaleValue * windowMotionFactor.Value

    -- Preserve the existing touch-friendly switch target without changing the
    -- dimensions of any other control or panel.
    local switchCompensationScale = responsiveScaleValue < 1
        and (1 / math.max(responsiveScaleValue, 0.25)) or 1
    for _, switchTrack in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
        if switchTrack:IsA("GuiButton") and switchTrack.Name == "Toggle"
            and switchTrack:GetAttribute("SyllinseBaseSwitchWidth") then
            local switchScale = switchTrack:FindFirstChild("SyllinseSwitchScaleCompensation")
            if switchScale and switchScale:IsA("UIScale") then
                switchScale.Scale = switchCompensationScale
            end

            local switchRow = switchTrack.Parent
            while switchRow and switchRow ~= root do
                if switchRow.Name == "LabelFrame" then
                    local baseHeight = switchRow:GetAttribute("SyllinseToggleBaseRowHeight")
                    if type(baseHeight) == "number" and switchRow.Size.Y.Scale == 0 then
                        local requestedLabelSize = tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.LabelTextSize)
                            or (SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.Height - 2)
                        local minimumPhysicalHeight = math.max(
                            SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.RowMinHeight,
                            SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.Height + SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.RowPadding,
                            requestedLabelSize + SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.RowPadding
                        )
                        local targetHeight = math.max(
                            baseHeight, minimumPhysicalHeight / math.max(responsiveScaleValue, 0.25))
                        switchRow.Size = UDim2.new(
                            switchRow.Size.X.Scale, switchRow.Size.X.Offset,
                            0, math.ceil(targetHeight)
                        )
                        switchRow.ClipsDescendants = false
                    end
                    break
                end
                switchRow = switchRow.Parent
            end
        end
    end

    -- Keep a manually dragged shell fully inside the safe rectangle after rotation
    -- or viewport changes. Use the actual rendered size (including UIScale).
    if windowHasCustomPosition and not preserveWindowPosition then
        task.defer(function()
            if not root.Parent then return end
            local activeCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local viewportNow = activeCamera and activeCamera.ViewportSize or gui.AbsoluteSize
            local currentSize = root.AbsoluteSize
            local margin = touchDevice and 4 or 18
            local minX = safeLeft + currentSize.X / 2 + margin
            local maxX = viewportNow.X - safeRight - currentSize.X / 2 - margin
            local minY = safeTop + currentSize.Y / 2 + margin
            local maxY = viewportNow.Y - safeBottom - currentSize.Y / 2 - margin
            local cx = viewportNow.X * root.Position.X.Scale + root.Position.X.Offset
            local cy = viewportNow.Y * root.Position.Y.Scale + root.Position.Y.Offset
            if maxX < minX then
                cx = (safeLeft + viewportNow.X - safeRight) / 2
            else
                cx = math.clamp(cx, minX, maxX)
            end
            if maxY < minY then
                cy = (safeTop + viewportNow.Y - safeBottom) / 2
            else
                cy = math.clamp(cy, minY, maxY)
            end
            root.Position = UDim2.new(
                root.Position.X.Scale,
                cx - viewportNow.X * root.Position.X.Scale,
                root.Position.Y.Scale,
                cy - viewportNow.Y * root.Position.Y.Scale
            )
        end)
    end

end

updateScale()
local function watchCurrentCamera(camera)
    if camera then
        camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateScale)
    end
end
watchCurrentCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
    watchCurrentCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
    task.defer(updateScale)
end)

-- Each icon has a vector fallback, hidden while the real Roblox asset preloads. The real icon
-- appears only after a successful fetch, so a generic fallback never flashes before the user's icon.
-- If Roblox rejects the image, clear the broken-image placeholder and reveal the vector fallback.
local ICON_KIND_BY_ID = {
    ["123721229450612"] = "Logo",
    ["97863600700870"] = "Combat",
    ["128100651128415"] = "Desync",
    ["76957546191785"] = "Visuals",
    ["130068885175166"] = "Misc",
    ["108550744861024"] = "Configs",
    ["76254758372811"] = "Settings",
}
-- Prepare white alpha masks of the original artwork so ImageColor3 can brighten it.
local tabIconAssets = { Entries = {} }
do
    local signature = "\137PNG\013\010\026\010"
    local function pngSize(bytes)
        assert(type(bytes) == "string" and #bytes >= 33 and #bytes <= 8 * 1024 * 1024
            and bytes:sub(1, 8) == signature and bytes:sub(13, 16) == "IHDR", "Icon is not a PNG")
        local function u32(offset)
            local a, b, c, d = bytes:byte(offset, offset + 3)
            return ((a * 256 + b) * 256 + c) * 256 + d
        end
        local width, height = u32(17), u32(21)
        assert(width > 0 and height > 0 and width <= 1024 and height <= 1024, "Icon dimensions are too large")
        return width, height
    end
    function tabIconAssets.WhiteMask(codec, bytes)
        pngSize(bytes)
        local image = codec.decode(buffer.fromstring(bytes))
        local pixels, maximum, transparent = image.pixels, 0, false
        for offset = 3, buffer.len(pixels) - 1, 4 do
            local alpha = buffer.readu8(pixels, offset)
            maximum = math.max(maximum, alpha)
            if alpha == 0 then transparent = true end
            if offset % 32768 == 3 then task.wait() end
        end
        -- Reject opaque thumbnails rather than turning their backgrounds into white squares.
        assert(transparent and maximum > 0, "Icon has no transparent silhouette")
        for offset = 0, buffer.len(pixels) - 4, 4 do
            local alpha = buffer.readu8(pixels, offset + 3)
            buffer.writeu32(pixels, offset, bit32.bor(0xFFFFFF,
                bit32.lshift(math.floor(alpha * 255 / maximum + 0.5), 24)))
            if offset % 32768 == 0 then task.wait() end
        end
        return buffer.tostring(codec.encode(pixels, { width = image.width; height = image.height; }))
    end
    local function fetch(url)
        local send = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
        if type(send) == "function" then
            local ok, response = pcall(send, { Url = url; Method = "GET"; Timeout = 12; })
            if ok and type(response) == "table" then
                local status = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status)
                if status and status >= 200 and status < 300 and type(response.Body) == "string" then
                    return response.Body
                end
            end
        end
        return game:HttpGet(url)
    end
    local function publish(entry)
        for _, target in ipairs(entry.Targets) do
            local image = target.Image
            if image.Parent then
                image:SetAttribute("SyllinseTintMask", true)
                image.Image = entry.Asset
                image.ImageTransparency = 0
                image.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Default
                image.Visible = true
                if target.Fallback and target.Fallback.Parent then target.Fallback.Visible = false end
            end
        end
    end
    local function build(id, codec)
        local ok, bytes = pcall(fetch, "https://assetdelivery.roblox.com/v1/asset/?id=" .. id)
        if ok then
            local made, mask = pcall(tabIconAssets.WhiteMask, codec, bytes)
            if made then return mask end
            -- A decal may refer to an underlying image asset instead of returning PNG bytes.
            if type(bytes) == "string" and #bytes < 64 * 1024 then
                local imageId = bytes:match("<url>[^<]-[?&]id=(%d+)[^<]*</url>")
                    or bytes:match("<url>rbxassetid://(%d+)</url>")
                if imageId and imageId ~= id then
                    local fetched, image = pcall(fetch, "https://assetdelivery.roblox.com/v1/asset/?id=" .. imageId)
                    if fetched then
                        made, mask = pcall(tabIconAssets.WhiteMask, codec, image)
                        if made then return mask end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local http = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local response = http:JSONDecode(fetch("https://thumbnails.roblox.com/v1/assets?assetIds="
            .. id .. "&size=420x420&format=Png&isCircular=false"))
        local thumbnail = type(response) == "table" and type(response.data) == "table" and response.data[1]
        local url = thumbnail and thumbnail.imageUrl
        assert(type(url) == "string" and url:match("^https://[%w%-%.]+%.rbxcdn%.com/"), "No transparent icon image is available")
        return tabIconAssets.WhiteMask(codec, fetch(url))
    end
    function tabIconAssets:Prepare(id, entry)
        if not self.Codec or entry.Started then return end
        entry.Started = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, failure = pcall(function()
                local register = type(getcustomasset) == "function" and getcustomasset or getsynasset
                assert(type(register) == "function" and type(writefile) == "function", "Local icon assets are unavailable")
                local folder, path = "Syllinse/Icons", "Syllinse/Icons/tint-v1-" .. id .. ".png"
                local cached = false
                if type(isfile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and isfile(path) then
                    local read, bytes = pcall(readfile, path)
                    cached = read and pcall(pngSize, bytes)
                end
                if not cached then
                    local mask = build(id, self.Codec)
                    if not gui.Parent then return end
                    for _, directory in ipairs({ "Syllinse", folder }) do
                        if type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder(directory) then makefolder(directory) end
                    end
                    writefile(path, mask)
                end
                if not gui.Parent then return end
                local asset = register(path)
                assert(type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "", "The icon cache could not be registered")
                local ready = false
                ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({ asset }, function(_, status)
                    if status == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then ready = true end
                end)
                assert(ready, "The recolorable icon did not preload")
                entry.Asset = asset
                publish(entry)
            end)
            if not ok and gui.Parent then
                warn("[Syllinse icons] Could not prepare " .. id .. ": " .. tostring(failure))
            end
        end)
    end
    function tabIconAssets:Register(id, image, fallback)
        local entry = self.Entries[id]
        if not entry then entry = { Targets = {} }; self.Entries[id] = entry end
        table.insert(entry.Targets, { Image = image; Fallback = fallback })
        if entry.Asset then publish(entry) else self:Prepare(id, entry) end
    end
    function tabIconAssets:Start(codec)
        self.Codec = codec
        for id, entry in pairs(self.Entries) do self:Prepare(id, entry) end
    end
end


local warnedAssetIds = {}

local function makeVectorFallback(name, parent, size, position, anchorPoint, zIndex, kind)
    size = size or 24
    local rootFallback = Instance.new("Frame")
    rootFallback.Name = name or "ImageFallback"
    rootFallback.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
    rootFallback.Position = position or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    rootFallback.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    rootFallback.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rootFallback.BorderSizePixel = 0
    rootFallback.ZIndex = zIndex or 2
    rootFallback.Parent = parent

    local color = Theme.Muted
    local function line(x1, y1, x2, y2, thickness)
        local scale = size / 24
        local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
        local piece = Instance.new("Frame")
        piece.Name = "Stroke"
        piece.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        piece.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(((x1 + x2) / 2) * scale, ((y1 + y2) / 2) * scale)
        piece.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * scale), math.max(1, thickness * scale))
        piece.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
        piece.BackgroundColor3 = color
        piece.BorderSizePixel = 0
        piece.ZIndex = rootFallback.ZIndex + 1
        piece.Parent = rootFallback
        addCorner(piece, math.max(1, math.floor(thickness * scale)))
        return piece
    end
    local function dot(x, y, diameter, fill)
        local scale = size / 24
        local c = Instance.new("Frame")
        c.Name = "Dot"
        c.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        c.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(diameter * scale, diameter * scale)
        c.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * scale, y * scale)
        c.BackgroundColor3 = fill or color
        c.BorderSizePixel = 0
        c.ZIndex = rootFallback.ZIndex + 2
        c.Parent = rootFallback
        addCorner(c, math.ceil(diameter * scale))
        return c
    end

    if kind == "Logo" then
        local letter = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        letter.Name = "LogoFallbackS"
        letter.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        letter.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        letter.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
        letter.FontFace = FONT_SEMIBOLD
        letter.Text = "S"
        letter.TextSize = size * 0.9
        letter.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        letter.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.45
        letter.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 33, 33)
        letter.ZIndex = rootFallback.ZIndex + 1
        letter.Parent = rootFallback
        local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(0, 238, 214)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.28, Color3.fromRGB(0, 177, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.52, Color3.fromRGB(105, 77, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, Color3.fromRGB(239, 54, 211)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 242, 55)),
        })
        gradient.Rotation = -28
        gradient.Parent = letter
        return rootFallback
    elseif kind == "Combat" then
        -- Two crossed blades with short hilts; neutral monochrome until the source image loads.
        line(5, 4, 19, 20, 2.1)
        line(19, 4, 5, 20, 2.1)
        line(4, 3, 8, 7, 2.6)
        line(16, 17, 20, 21, 2.6)
        line(16, 3, 20, 7, 2.6)
        line(4, 17, 8, 21, 2.6)
    elseif kind == "Desync" then
        line(5, 4, 19, 4, 1.8)
        line(5, 4, 5, 13, 1.8)
        line(19, 4, 19, 13, 1.8)
        line(5, 13, 8, 17, 1.8)
        line(19, 13, 16, 17, 1.8)
        line(8, 17, 12, 20, 1.8)
        line(16, 17, 12, 20, 1.8)
    elseif kind == "Visuals" then
        line(3, 12, 8, 7, 1.7)
        line(8, 7, 16, 7, 1.7)
        line(16, 7, 21, 12, 1.7)
        line(21, 12, 16, 17, 1.7)
        line(16, 17, 8, 17, 1.7)
        line(8, 17, 3, 12, 1.7)
        dot(12, 12, 5.2, color)
    elseif kind == "Misc" then
        line(7, 6, 20, 6, 1.8)
        line(7, 12, 20, 12, 1.8)
        line(7, 18, 20, 18, 1.8)
        dot(4, 6, 2.2, color)
        dot(4, 12, 2.2, color)
        dot(4, 18, 2.2, color)
    elseif kind == "Configs" then
        line(6, 3, 15, 3, 1.5)
        line(15, 3, 19, 7, 1.5)
        line(19, 7, 19, 21, 1.5)
        line(19, 21, 6, 21, 1.5)
        line(6, 21, 6, 3, 1.5)
        line(15, 3, 15, 7, 1.5)
        line(15, 7, 19, 7, 1.5)
        line(9, 11, 16, 11, 1.5)
        line(9, 14, 16, 14, 1.5)
        line(9, 17, 16, 17, 1.5)
    else
        -- Generic fallback for any other image-based Lumen feature entry.
        line(5, 7, 19, 7, 1.8)
        line(5, 12, 19, 12, 1.8)
        line(5, 17, 19, 17, 1.8)
    end
    return rootFallback
end

local function recolorFallback(fallback, active, activeColor, idleColor)
    if not fallback then return end
    local color = active and (activeColor or Theme.IconActive) or (idleColor or Theme.IconIdle)
    for _, descendant in ipairs(fallback:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("Frame") then
            descendant.BackgroundColor3 = color
            descendant.BackgroundTransparency = active and 0 or 0.04
        elseif descendant:IsA("TextLabel") then
            descendant.TextColor3 = color
            descendant.TextTransparency = 0
        elseif descendant:IsA("UIStroke") then
            descendant.Color = color
            descendant.Transparency = active and 0 or 0.04
        end
    end
end

local function setRobloxImage(imageLabel, assetUri, _thumbSize, fallback)
    if not imageLabel or not assetUri or assetUri == "" then
        return
    end
    if not fallback and imageLabel.Parent then
        fallback = imageLabel.Parent:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback")
    end

    local source = tostring(assetUri)
    local assetId = source:match("(%d+)")
    local contentUri
    if source:match("^%s*rbxassetid://") then
        contentUri = source:gsub("^%s+", "")
    elseif source:match("^%s*%d+%s*$") then
        contentUri = "rbxassetid://" .. source:match("%d+")
    else
        contentUri = source
    end

    if assetId then imageLabel:SetAttribute("SyllinseAssetId", assetId) end
    imageLabel.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    imageLabel.ImageTransparency = 0
    imageLabel.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    imageLabel.Visible = false
    if fallback then fallback.Visible = false end

    task.spawn(function()
        local function tryLoad(uri)
            if imageLabel:GetAttribute("SyllinseTintMask") then return true, "Tint mask ready", nil end
            local success = false
            local statusText = "no fetch callback status"
            imageLabel.Visible = false
            imageLabel.Image = uri
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({ imageLabel }, function(_contentId, fetchStatus)
                    statusText = tostring(fetchStatus)
                    if fetchStatus == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then
                        success = true
                    end
                end)
            end)
            if not ok then
                return false, statusText, err
            end
            return success, statusText, nil
        end

        local loaded, status, err = tryLoad(contentUri)
        if imageLabel:GetAttribute("SyllinseTintMask") then return end
        local usedThumbnail = false

        -- Only try a thumbnail if the direct image content failed. Keep the image hidden
        -- while loading so neither a broken-image placeholder nor a temporary generic icon flashes.
        if not loaded and assetId then
            imageLabel.Image = ""
            task.wait(0.1)
            local thumbUri = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. assetId .. "&w=420&h=420"
            local thumbLoaded, thumbStatus, thumbErr = tryLoad(thumbUri)
            if thumbLoaded then
                loaded = true
                usedThumbnail = true
            else
                status = thumbStatus or status
                err = thumbErr or err
            end
        end

        if not imageLabel.Parent or imageLabel:GetAttribute("SyllinseTintMask") then return end
        if loaded then
            imageLabel.Visible = true
            if fallback then fallback.Visible = false end
            if usedThumbnail then
                imageLabel:SetAttribute("SyllinseLoadedAsThumbnail", true)
            end
            return
        end

        imageLabel.Image = ""
        imageLabel.Visible = false
        imageLabel:SetAttribute("SyllinseImageLoadFailed", true)
        if fallback then
            -- A fallback can fail after the current tab was selected; style it from the
            -- current selection immediately instead of waiting for the next tab click.
            local parentName = imageLabel.Parent and imageLabel.Parent.Name or ""
            local selected = parentName == (activeName .. "Button")
                or parentName == ("HeaderIconHolder_" .. activeName)
            local isLogo = assetId == LOGO_IMAGE_ID:match("(%d+)")
            if not isLogo then
                recolorFallback(fallback, selected)
            end
            fallback.Visible = true
        end
        local warningId = assetId or contentUri
        if not warnedAssetIds[warningId] then
            warnedAssetIds[warningId] = true
            if err then
                warn("[Syllinse] Could not preload image " .. tostring(warningId) .. ": " .. tostring(err) .. ". Keeping the vector fallback visible.")
            else
                warn("[Syllinse] Roblox could not fetch image asset " .. tostring(warningId) ..
                    " (" .. tostring(status) .. "). Keeping the vector fallback visible. Check the content ID and experience permissions.")
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Create an image icon plus a drawn fallback. Broken Roblox image placeholders are never shown.
local function makeImageIcon(name, parent, assetUri, size, position, anchorPoint, zIndex, _color, fallbackSize, imageWidth, imageHeight)
    local id = tostring(assetUri or ""):match("(%d+)")
    local kind = ICON_KIND_BY_ID[id] or name or "Generic"
    local fallback = makeVectorFallback(
        "ImageFallback", parent, fallbackSize or size or 24, position or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0), zIndex or 2, kind
    )

    local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    image.Name = name or "Icon"
    image.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(imageWidth or size or 24, imageHeight or size or 24)
    image.Position = position or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    image.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    image.BorderSizePixel = 0
    image.Image = ""
    image.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    image.ImageTransparency = 0
    image.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    image.ZIndex = (zIndex or 2) + 1
    image.Visible = false
    image.Parent = parent
    setRobloxImage(image, assetUri, nil, fallback)
    image:SetAttribute("SyllinseFallbackName", fallback.Name)
    if ICON_KIND_BY_ID[id] and kind ~= "Logo" then tabIconAssets:Register(id, image, fallback) end
    return image
end

-- Keep a colorful S visible until Roblox confirms the real logo image can be loaded.
local logoFallback = makeVectorFallback(
    "ImageFallback", sidebar, 60, UDim2.fromOffset(8, 10), Vector2.new(0, 0), 2, "Logo"
)
-- If the execution environment supports local custom assets and the PNG is in its working
-- directory, prefer the supplied original logo PNG. Otherwise use the Roblox asset ID.
-- This is optional and never blocks the UI from opening.
local function findLocalLogoAsset()
    local customAsset = (type(getcustomasset) == "function" and getcustomasset)
        or (type(getsynasset) == "function" and getsynasset)
    if type(customAsset) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end
    local paths = { "Syllinse_Logo.png", "Syllinse/Syllinse_Logo.png", "assets/Syllinse_Logo.png" }
    for _, path in ipairs(paths) do
        local okFile, exists = pcall(isfile, path)
        if okFile and exists then
            local okAsset, content = pcall(customAsset, path)
            if okAsset and type(content) == "string" and content ~= "" then
                return content
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

logoImageSource = findLocalLogoAsset() or logoImageSource
setRobloxImage(logo, logoImageSource, nil, logoFallback)

-- Load the updated Syllinse logo asset ID and the updated icon asset IDs without converting
-- them to thumbnail URLs.
-- A small neutral vector gear for Settings because no Settings asset ID was supplied.
-- It is drawn from GUI primitives rather than a font glyph, avoiding missing-box icons.
local function makeGearIcon(parent, size, position, color, zIndex, anchorPoint)
    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.Name = "GearIcon"
    container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
    container.Position = position
    container.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.BorderSizePixel = 0
    container.ZIndex = zIndex or 3
    container.Parent = parent

    local parts = {}
    local center = size / 2
    local radius = size * 0.33
    local toothW = math.max(3, math.floor(size * 0.19))
    local toothH = math.max(4, math.floor(size * 0.28))
    for i = 0, 7 do
        local a = (math.pi * 2 * i / 8) - math.pi / 2
        local tooth = Instance.new("Frame")
        tooth.Name = "Tooth" .. tostring(i + 1)
        tooth.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        tooth.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(toothW, toothH)
        tooth.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(center + math.cos(a) * radius, center + math.sin(a) * radius)
        tooth.Rotation = i * 45
        tooth.BackgroundColor3 = color or Theme.Muted
        tooth.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tooth.ZIndex = container.ZIndex
        tooth.Parent = container
        addCorner(tooth, 1)
        table.insert(parts, tooth)
    end

    local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
    ring.Name = "GearRing"
    ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 0.56, size * 0.56)
    ring.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ring.ZIndex = container.ZIndex + 1
    ring.Parent = container
    addCorner(ring, size)
    local ringStroke = addStroke(ring, color or Theme.Muted, 0, 1.5)

    local centerDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    centerDot.Name = "GearCenter"
    centerDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    centerDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(3, size * 0.18), math.max(3, size * 0.18))
    centerDot.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    centerDot.BackgroundColor3 = color or Theme.Muted
    centerDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    centerDot.ZIndex = container.ZIndex + 2
    centerDot.Parent = container
    addCorner(centerDot, size)

    return { Container = container, Parts = parts, RingStroke = ringStroke, Center = centerDot }
end

local function setGearIconColor(gear, color)
    if not gear then return end
    for _, part in ipairs(gear.Parts) do
        part.BackgroundColor3 = color
    end
    gear.RingStroke.Color = color
    gear.Center.BackgroundColor3 = color
end

local function makeSearchGlyph(parent)
    -- Conventional magnifying glass. The handle's inner endpoint meets the outside
    -- of the lens at 45 degrees; the lens is drawn above the joint so no line crosses it.
    local handle = Instance.new("Frame")
    handle.Name = "SearchHandle"
    handle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    handle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(7.5, 1.5)
    handle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26.4, 28.4)
    handle.Rotation = 45
    handle.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelIcon
    handle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    handle.ZIndex = 7
    handle.Parent = parent
    addCorner(handle, 2)

    local circle = Instance.new("Frame")
    circle.Name = "SearchLens"
    circle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
    circle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13.5, 15.5)
    circle.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
    circle.BackgroundTransparency = 0 -- masks the handle beneath the lens interior
    circle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    circle.ZIndex = 8
    circle.Parent = parent
    addCorner(circle, 20)
    local lensStroke = addStroke(circle, Theme.PanelIcon, 0.02, 1.25)
    return circle, handle, lensStroke
end

local function updateHeaderIcon(name)
    if headerTitle then headerTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.HeaderTitleTint or Theme.HeaderIconTint end
    for tabName, entry in pairs(headerIcons) do
        local selected = (tabName == name)
        entry.Holder.Visible = selected
        if entry.Icon then
            -- Header artwork keeps its own tint for the reference contrast.
            entry.Icon.ImageColor3 = selected and Theme.HeaderIconTint or Theme.PanelIcon
            entry.Icon.ImageTransparency = 0
        end
        recolorFallback(
            entry.Holder:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback"),
            selected,
            Theme.HeaderIconTint,
            Theme.PanelIcon
        )
    end
end

local function setFallbackEmphasis(parent, active, hovered)
    local fallback = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback")
    recolorFallback(fallback, active, nil, hovered and Theme.IconHover or nil)
end

local function setActiveTab(name)
    activeName = name
    local found
    for _, item in ipairs(navItems) do
        if item.Name == name then
            found = item
        end
    end

    if headerTitle then
        headerTitle.Text = name
        -- Keep a small, consistent gap from the visible icon; set per category in navItems.
        headerTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(found and found.HeaderTitleX or 51, 19)
    end
    updateHeaderIcon(name)
    if headerGear and found then
        headerGear.Container.Visible = found.Gear == true
        setGearIconColor(headerGear, Theme.HeaderIconTint)
    end

    for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
        local selected = nav.Name == name
        local terminologyLabel = terminologyLabels[nav.Name]
        if terminologyLabel then
            -- Match the tab's current hover color while keeping labels independent.
            local hoveringThisTab = nav.Hovering == true
            tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, {
                -- Text uses a muted perceived-match tint because opaque glyphs look
                -- brighter than translucent icon artwork at the same RGB value.
                TextColor3 = selected and terminologyTint(Theme.IconActive, 0.76)
                    or (hoveringThisTab and terminologyTint(Theme.IconHover, 0.86) or Theme.IconIdle),
            })
        end
        -- The mockup has no selected tile or accent bar; only the active icon brightens.
        tween(nav.Button, 0.14, {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        })
        if nav.Icon then
            -- Selected stays bright; a hovered, unselected icon uses the same muted
            -- hover tint as its separate terminology label.
            tween(nav.Icon, 0.14, {
                ImageColor3 = selected and Theme.IconActive
                    or (nav.Hovering and Theme.IconHover or Theme.IconIdle),
                ImageTransparency = 0,
            })
            setFallbackEmphasis(nav.Button, selected, nav.Hovering)
        end
        if nav.Gear then
            setGearIconColor(nav.Gear, selected and Theme.IconActive or (nav.Hovering and Theme.IconHover or Theme.IconIdle))
        end
        -- The mockup has no bright side indicator or selected tile.
        nav.Outline.Transparency = 1
    end

    -- Lumen owns the real page/control frames. The page frame itself has been mounted
    -- over Syllinse's original pair of panels, so use Lumen's native Open/Close behavior.
    local lumenPage = lumenPages[name]
    if lumenPage and type(lumenPage.Open) == "function" then
        pcall(function() lumenPage:Open() end)
    end
    for tabName, page in pairs(lumenPages) do
        local hostsForPage = lumenHosts[tabName]
        local selected = tabName == name
        local usingMobileHost = compactTouchLayout and hostsForPage and hostsForPage.Mobile
        if page.Frame then
            page.Frame.Visible = selected and not usingMobileHost
        end
        if hostsForPage and hostsForPage.Shared then hostsForPage.Shared.Visible = selected end
        if hostsForPage and hostsForPage.Mobile then
            hostsForPage.Mobile.Visible = selected and compactTouchLayout
            if selected and compactTouchLayout then
                hostsForPage.Mobile.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            end
        end
    end
    local hosts = lumenHosts[name]
    if hosts then
        if hosts.Shared then hosts.Shared.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0) end
        if hosts.Left then hosts.Left.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0) end
        if hosts.Right then hosts.Right.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0) end
        if hosts.Mobile then hosts.Mobile.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0) end
    end
end

for itemIndex, item in ipairs(navItems) do
    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Name = item.Name .. "Button"
    -- Use a 55x55 touch target while keeping the icon artwork compact.
    local navTapSize = NAV_BUTTON_SIZE
    local navRowY = 2 + (itemIndex - 1) * NAV_ROW_HEIGHT
    button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(navTapSize, navTapSize)
    button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(NAV_BUTTON_X, navRowY)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.Text = ""
    button.ZIndex = 2
    button.Parent = navContainer
    setManualCorner(button, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.NavigationButton)

    local outline = addStroke(button, Theme.Stroke, 1, 1)
    -- Keep the tab button as an invisible click target: no outline or hover tile is rendered.
    outline.Enabled = false
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    -- Hover animation is deliberately limited to sidebar icons/labels (not Lumen controls).
    local hoverScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    hoverScale.Name = "TabHoverScale"
    hoverScale.Scale = 1
    hoverScale.Parent = button

    local icon
    local gearIcon
    if item.IconId then
        -- Larger fitted image artwork is centered inside the 55px tap target.
        icon = makeImageIcon(
            item.Name .. "Icon", button, item.IconId, item.IconSize or 34,
            UDim2.new(0.5, item.IconOffsetX or 0, 0.5, item.IconOffsetY or 0),
            Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), 3, nil, item.IconSize or 38
        )
        icon.ImageColor3 = Theme.Muted
    elseif item.Gear then
        gearIcon = makeGearIcon(
            button, 34, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Theme.Muted, 3, Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        )
    end

    local terminologyLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    terminologyLabel.Name = "FunctionTerminology"
    terminologyLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    terminologyLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    terminologyLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    -- This label is a sibling of the tab button, not a child of it. It therefore
    -- cannot enlarge the button's hitbox or inherit the icon's hover-scale animation.
    -- Optional labels sit directly below each tab without overlapping the next row.
    local terminologyLabelPositionY = navRowY + navTapSize - 1
    terminologyLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, terminologyLabelPositionY)
    terminologyLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(77, 13)
    terminologyLabel.FontFace = FONT_BOLD
    terminologyLabel.Text = item.Name
    -- A slightly larger native-size glyph is easier to read than shrinking 13px text.
    -- TextScaled remains off so Roblox does not auto-fit and resample the lettering.
    terminologyLabel.TextScaled = false
    terminologyLabel.TextSize = 11
    terminologyLabel.RichText = false
    terminologyLabel.AutoLocalize = false
    terminologyLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.IconIdle
    terminologyLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
    terminologyLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    terminologyLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    terminologyLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    terminologyLabel.ZIndex = 5
    terminologyLabel.Visible = showMenuFunctionTerminology
    terminologyLabel.TextTransparency = showMenuFunctionTerminology and 0 or 1
    terminologyLabel.Parent = navContainer
    terminologyLabel.Active = false
    terminologyLabel.Selectable = false
    terminologyLabels[item.Name] = terminologyLabel

    -- Keep text at its native scale so small glyphs stay crisp; the label's
    -- color tween below still provides hover feedback without resampling the text.

    -- The label is a sibling of the button, so it uses a separate visual tween.
    -- Match the icon's exact scale multiplier, duration, and easing; the label stays
    -- outside the button and never changes its clickable size or hitbox.

    local buttonPointerInside = false
    local labelPointerInside = false
    local navHoverState = false
    local navReference

    local function applyNavHover(isHovered)
        if isHovered and isPopupInteractionBlocked(button) then return end
        if navReference then
            navReference.Hovering = isHovered
        end
        if navHoverState ~= isHovered then
            navHoverState = isHovered
            -- Keep the icon's subtle 1.04x hover animation. Text stays at native scale
            -- so its letterforms remain crisp; the label color still animates with hover.
            tween(hoverScale, 0.16, { Scale = isHovered and 1.04 or 1 })
        end

        -- Never draw a tile or hitbox around a sidebar tab. Only the icon and its
        -- optional terminology label react to hover, while the transparent button
        -- continues to handle clicks/taps normally.
        button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        setFallbackEmphasis(button, item.Name == activeName, isHovered)
        if isHovered then
            if item.Name ~= activeName then
                if icon then tween(icon, 0.14, { ImageColor3 = Theme.IconHover }) end
                tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, { TextColor3 = terminologyTint(Theme.IconHover, 0.86) })
            else
                tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, { TextColor3 = terminologyTint(Theme.IconActive, 0.76) })
            end
        else
            if item.Name ~= activeName then
                if icon then tween(icon, 0.14, { ImageColor3 = Theme.IconIdle }) end
                tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, { TextColor3 = Theme.IconIdle })
            else
                tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, { TextColor3 = terminologyTint(Theme.IconActive, 0.76) })
            end
        end
    end

    local function deferHoverExit()
        -- MouseLeave fires as the pointer crosses the gap between the icon button
        -- and its label. Wait briefly so the independent label can claim the hover
        -- state without adding any pixels to the button's clickable hitbox.
        task.delay(0.08, function()
            if not buttonPointerInside and not labelPointerInside then
                applyNavHover(false)
            end
        end)
    end

    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        buttonPointerInside = true
        applyNavHover(true)
    end)
    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        buttonPointerInside = false
        deferHoverExit()
    end)
    terminologyLabel.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if showMenuFunctionTerminology and terminologyLabel.Visible then
            labelPointerInside = true
            applyNavHover(true)
        end
    end)
    terminologyLabel.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        labelPointerInside = false
        deferHoverExit()
    end)
    button.Activated:Connect(function()
        setActiveTab(item.Name)
        -- Keep a feature query active while browsing its matches across categories.
        -- Category-prefix navigation (such as "c") clears when the user manually switches away.
        if searchBox and currentSearchMode ~= "controls" then
            searchBox.Text = ""
        end
    end)

    navReference = {
        Name = item.Name,
        Button = button,
        Icon = icon,
        Gear = gearIcon,
        Outline = outline,
        BaseButtonWidth = navTapSize,
        BaseButtonHeight = navTapSize,
        BaseButtonX = NAV_BUTTON_X,
        BaseY = navRowY,
        TerminologyLabelY = terminologyLabelPositionY,
        BaseIconSize = item.IconSize or 34,
        Hovering = false,
    }
    table.insert(navButtons, navReference)
end

-- Size the invisible-scrollbar nav canvas from the actual tab count.
navContainer.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 4 + #navItems * NAV_ROW_HEIGHT)

    corner.BottomRightRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
    corner.TopRightRadius = UDim.new(0, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Header)
end

-- Replace the original X in-place with a cleaner MacLib-inspired four-way move glyph.
-- Keep the original button position/hitbox; use rounded UI-vector strokes so the icon stays crisp.
local dragButton = Instance.new("ImageButton")
dragButton.Name = "DragButton"
dragButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
dragButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 9)
-- Larger 48x48 hit target makes the drag handle easier to grab without enlarging the artwork.
dragButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 48)
dragButton.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover
dragButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
dragButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
dragButton.Image = ""
dragButton.AutoButtonColor = false
dragButton.Active = true
dragButton.Selectable = false
dragButton.ZIndex = 8
dragButton.Parent = header
addCorner(dragButton, 10)
local dragButtonStroke = addStroke(dragButton, Theme.Stroke, 1, 1)
 dragButtonStroke.Enabled = false -- Invisible hit area: no hover outline is ever rendered.
-- Recreate MacLib's four-way transform/move glyph with vector UI strokes.
-- This keeps the same icon style without scaling a low-resolution raster asset.
local dragGlyph = Instance.new("Frame")
dragGlyph.Name = "DragGlyph"
dragGlyph.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
dragGlyph.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
dragGlyph.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
dragGlyph.BackgroundTransparency = 1
dragGlyph.BorderSizePixel = 0
dragGlyph.ZIndex = 9
dragGlyph.Parent = dragButton

local dragGlyphStrokes = {}
do
    local function addDragStroke(name, x1, y1, x2, y2)
        local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
        local stroke = Instance.new("Frame")
        stroke.Name = name
        stroke.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        stroke.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((x1 + x2) / 2, (y1 + y2) / 2)
        stroke.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy), 2)
        stroke.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
        stroke.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Muted
        stroke.BorderSizePixel = 0
        stroke.ZIndex = 10
        local cap = Instance.new("UICorner")
        cap.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        cap.Parent = stroke
        stroke.Parent = dragGlyph
        table.insert(dragGlyphStrokes, stroke)
    end

    -- Cross-shaped shafts with four clean, symmetrical arrowheads (MacLib transform style).
    addDragStroke("HorizontalStem", 3, 13, 23, 13)
    addDragStroke("VerticalStem", 13, 3, 13, 23)
    addDragStroke("UpLeft", 9, 7, 13, 3)
    addDragStroke("UpRight", 13, 3, 17, 7)
    addDragStroke("DownLeft", 9, 19, 13, 23)
    addDragStroke("DownRight", 13, 23, 17, 19)
    addDragStroke("LeftTop", 7, 9, 3, 13)
    addDragStroke("LeftBottom", 3, 13, 7, 17)
    addDragStroke("RightTop", 19, 9, 23, 13)
    addDragStroke("RightBottom", 23, 13, 19, 17)
end

motionBody.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
motionBody.BackgroundTransparency = 1
motionBody.BorderSizePixel = 0
motionBody.GroupTransparency = 0
motionBody.ZIndex = 1
motionBody.Parent = root

local CLOSED_SURFACE = Theme.Root -- Keep the launcher surface matched to the shell theme.
local LAUNCHER_HEIGHT = 58

-- Launcher logo size is independent from the compact square button.

local LAUNCHER_WIDTH = 58
local LAUNCHER_SIZE = Vector2.new(LAUNCHER_WIDTH, LAUNCHER_HEIGHT)

-- Light bloom layer used behind the launcher logo; it is reparented under the button below.
local launcherGlow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

-- Scale the enlarged launcher to the available phone viewport. The desktop button
-- keeps its full 58px size, while smaller phones get a proportional reduction so
-- the launcher does not dominate the screen. UIScale also scales the logo and glow.
local launcherResponsiveScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
launcherResponsiveScale.Name = "ResponsiveLauncherScale"
launcherResponsiveScale.Scale = 1
launcherResponsiveScale.Parent = launcher

local function updateLauncherResponsiveScale()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or gui.AbsoluteSize
    local scale = 1

    if touchInputEnabled or viewport.X < 700 or viewport.Y < 500 then
        scale = math.clamp(math.min(viewport.X / 430, viewport.Y / 760, 1), 0.72, 1)
    end

    launcherResponsiveScale.Scale = scale
end

local launcherViewportConnection
local function watchLauncherCamera(camera)
    if launcherViewportConnection then
        launcherViewportConnection:Disconnect()
        launcherViewportConnection = nil
    end
    if camera then
        launcherViewportConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateLauncherResponsiveScale)
    end
end

updateLauncherResponsiveScale()
watchLauncherCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
    watchLauncherCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
    task.defer(updateLauncherResponsiveScale)
end)

local function syncLauncherGlow()
    if not launcher or not launcher.Parent then return end
    -- Keep the light centered behind the logo itself, not as a halo around the button.
    -- These are children of the launcher and sit above its background, but below the logo image.
    local center = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    launcherGlow.Position = center
    launcherGlowCyan.Position = center
    launcherGlowViolet.Position = center
end
launcher:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"):Connect(syncLauncherGlow)
launcher:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(syncLauncherGlow)
task.defer(syncLauncherGlow)

local windowIsOpen = false
local windowTransitionId = 0
-- Keep transition handles and the resting position together to limit top-level locals.
-- The entire shell is summoned from / recalled into the launcher with no fade or outline flash.
local windowTransitionTweens = { Scale = nil, Settle = nil, Position = nil, PositionSettle = nil, Rotation = nil }
local setWindowOpen

local function refreshLauncherVisibility()
    -- Keep the launcher visible and interactive in both states so it toggles the shell
    -- open/closed from the same saved position. It may cover only its own small hit area.
    local shouldShow = showLauncherButton
    launcher.Visible = shouldShow
    launcherGlow.Visible = shouldShow
    launcherGlowCyan.Visible = shouldShow
    launcherGlowViolet.Visible = shouldShow
    launcher.Interactable = shouldShow
    launcher.Active = shouldShow
    if shouldShow then
        syncLauncherGlow()
    end
end

-- Always animate the Syllinse window around the viewport center. The floating launcher
-- is independently draggable; its position must never determine where the main UI opens.
local function centeredWindowPosition()
    if touchInputEnabled then
        return UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, mobileSafeOffsetY)
    end
    return UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
end

setWindowOpen = function(open)
    open = open == true
    if open == windowIsOpen then return end

    -- Cancel any in-flight animation before reversing direction. The launcher is never
    -- used as a travel destination: the shell opens and closes in place, and the launcher
    -- stays anchored exactly where the user left it.
    for _, activeTween in pairs({
        windowTransitionTweens.Scale,
        windowTransitionTweens.Settle,
        windowTransitionTweens.Position,
        windowTransitionTweens.PositionSettle,
        windowTransitionTweens.Rotation,
    }) do
        if activeTween then
            pcall(function() activeTween:Cancel() end)
        end
    end
    windowTransitionTweens.Scale = nil
    windowTransitionTweens.Settle = nil
    windowTransitionTweens.Position = nil
    windowTransitionTweens.PositionSettle = nil
    windowTransitionTweens.Rotation = nil

    windowIsOpen = open
    windowTransitionId += 1
    local thisTransition = windowTransitionId

    local targetPosition
    if windowHasCustomPosition then
        targetPosition = root.Position
    else
        targetPosition = centeredWindowPosition()
    end

    -- Always keep the shell anchored at its final position. This intentionally removes
    -- the launcher-to-window travel that made the UI shoot upward and appear to hide.
    root.Position = targetPosition
    root.Rotation = 0

    if open then
        local wasVisible = root.Visible
        root.Size = baseWindowSize

        if not wasVisible then
            -- A compact, centered start gives a premium reveal without moving the window.
            windowMotionFactor.Value = 0.88
        end

        root.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
        root.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        root.ClipsDescendants = true
        root.Visible = true
        motionBody.GroupTransparency = 0
        motionBody.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        refreshLauncherVisibility()

        -- One-piece reveal: the entire opaque shell expands, then gently settles.
        local openInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.19, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        local settleInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.075, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        windowTransitionTweens.Scale = TweenService:Create(windowMotionFactor, openInfo, {
            -- Settle at exactly 100% of the selected window size; no hidden 104% overshoot.
            Value = 1,
        })
        windowTransitionTweens.Scale.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
            if playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then return end
            if thisTransition ~= windowTransitionId or not windowIsOpen then return end

            windowTransitionTweens.Settle = TweenService:Create(windowMotionFactor, settleInfo, {
                Value = 1,
            })
            windowTransitionTweens.Settle.Completed:Connect(function(settleState)
                if settleState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then return end
                if thisTransition ~= windowTransitionId or not windowIsOpen then return end
                windowTransitionTweens.Scale = nil
                windowTransitionTweens.Settle = nil
            end)
            windowTransitionTweens.Settle:Play()
        end)
        windowTransitionTweens.Scale:Play()
    else
        -- Close Reptillian dropdowns with the shell so detached option popups never remain onscreen.
        if ReptillianLibrary and ReptillianLibrary.OpenFrames then
            local openReptillianFrames = {}
            for frame in pairs(ReptillianLibrary.OpenFrames) do
                if frame and frame.IsOpen then table.insert(openReptillianFrames, frame) end
            end
            for _, frame in ipairs(openReptillianFrames) do
                pcall(function() frame:SetOpen(false) end)
            end
        end

        -- Preserve Lumen overlays so keybind lists, watermarks, and notifications continue working.
        if Lumen and Lumen.OpenPopup and type(Lumen.OpenPopup.Close) == "function" then
            pcall(function() Lumen.OpenPopup.Close() end)
        end
        if Lumen and Lumen._Instance then Lumen._Instance.Enabled = true end
        if Lumen and Lumen._Overlay then Lumen._Overlay.Enabled = true end

        -- Fast, centered close. No translation, no partial-content fade, and no outline flash.
        local closeInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        windowTransitionTweens.Scale = TweenService:Create(windowMotionFactor, closeInfo, {
            Value = 0.88,
        })
        windowTransitionTweens.Scale.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
            if playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then return end
            if thisTransition ~= windowTransitionId or windowIsOpen then return end

            root.Visible = false
            root.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
            root.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            root.Rotation = 0
            motionBody.GroupTransparency = 0
            motionBody.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            windowMotionFactor.Value = 1
            uiScale.Scale = responsiveScaleValue * windowMotionFactor.Value
            windowTransitionTweens.Scale = nil
            windowTransitionTweens.Settle = nil
            windowTransitionTweens.Position = nil
            windowTransitionTweens.PositionSettle = nil
            windowTransitionTweens.Rotation = nil
            refreshLauncherVisibility()
        end)
        windowTransitionTweens.Scale:Play()
    end
end

launcher.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
    launcher:SetAttribute("ButtonHovered", true)
    local buttonFill = launcher:GetAttribute("ButtonBackgroundColor") or CLOSED_SURFACE
    local buttonAccent = launcher:GetAttribute("ButtonAccentColor") or Theme.Accent
    local opacity = math.clamp(tonumber(launcher:GetAttribute("LauncherOpacity")) or 100, 0, 100) / 100
    tween(launcher, 0.14, {
        BackgroundColor3 = buttonFill:Lerp(buttonAccent, 0.07),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1 - opacity,
    })
    tween(launcherStroke, 0.14, { Transparency = 1 - opacity })
    tween(launcherGlow, 0.16, {
        ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.05 * opacity),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 56),
    })
    tween(launcherGlowCyan, 0.16, { ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.13 * opacity), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 60) })
    tween(launcherGlowViolet, 0.16, { ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.10 * opacity), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 58) })
end)
launcher.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    launcher:SetAttribute("ButtonHovered", false)
    local opacity = math.clamp(tonumber(launcher:GetAttribute("LauncherOpacity")) or 100, 0, 100) / 100
    tween(launcher, 0.18, {
        BackgroundColor3 = launcher:GetAttribute("ButtonBackgroundColor") or CLOSED_SURFACE,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1 - opacity,
    })
    tween(launcherStroke, 0.18, { Transparency = 1 - (0.92 * opacity) })
    tween(launcherGlow, 0.22, {
        ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.03 * opacity),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 54),
    })
    tween(launcherGlowCyan, 0.22, { ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.09 * opacity), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 58) })
    tween(launcherGlowViolet, 0.22, { ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.07 * opacity), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 56) })
end)

-- The launcher is draggable like the reference pill; a tap toggles open/closed, while a drag only repositions it.
local draggingLauncher = false
local launcherMoved = false
local launcherGrab = Vector2.zero
local launcherDragStart = Vector2.zero
launcher.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        -- InputObject.Position is a Vector3 in Roblox; convert screen X/Y to Vector2
        -- before comparing it with GuiObject.AbsolutePosition (a Vector2).
        local inputPosition = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        draggingLauncher = true
        launcherMoved = false
        launcherGrab = inputPosition - launcher.AbsolutePosition
        launcherDragStart = inputPosition
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if not draggingLauncher then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        return
    end

    local inputPosition = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
    if (inputPosition - launcherDragStart).Magnitude > 3 then
        launcherMoved = true
    end
    if not launcherMoved then return end

    local point = inputPosition - launcherGrab - gui.AbsolutePosition
    launcher.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    launcher.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
        math.clamp(point.X, 0, math.max(gui.AbsoluteSize.X - launcher.AbsoluteSize.X, 0)),
        math.clamp(point.Y, 0, math.max(gui.AbsoluteSize.Y - launcher.AbsoluteSize.Y, 0))
    )
    syncLauncherGlow()
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if not draggingLauncher then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        return
    end

    draggingLauncher = false
    if not launcherMoved then
        setWindowOpen(not windowIsOpen)
    end
end)

-- Keep the enlarged 48x48 drag hit area fully invisible.
-- Only the four-way glyph changes tint on hover; no tile, background, or outline appears.
dragButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
    if isPopupInteractionBlocked(dragButton) then return end
    for _, stroke in ipairs(dragGlyphStrokes) do
        tween(stroke, 0.14, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text })
    end
end)
dragButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    for _, stroke in ipairs(dragGlyphStrokes) do
        tween(stroke, 0.18, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Muted })
    end
end)

-- to the closest tab name, while other queries filter real controls inside the content columns.

-- Lumen's native section cards are the content panels; do not create the older pair
-- of Syllinse backdrop panels underneath them (that created duplicate, mismatched surfaces).
