-- Drag the shell only from the dedicated four-way icon.
-- Keep the entire shell inside the top/bottom and right edges, while allowing it to
-- move partly beyond the left edge as long as the top-right drag icon stays reachable.
local dragging = false
local dragInput
local dragStart
local startPosition

local function beginShellDrag(input)
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        return
    end
    dragging = true
    -- Store the active pointer mode in dragInput. Mouse drags follow global mouse-move
    -- events; touch drags follow only the touch that began on the drag handle.
    dragInput = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input or Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
    dragStart = input.Position
    startPosition = root.Position
    input.Changed:Connect(function()
        if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
            dragging = false
            dragInput = nil
        end
    end)
end

-- Only the dedicated four-way drag icon can move the Syllinse shell.
dragButton.InputBegan:Connect(beginShellDrag)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    local pointerMoved = dragging and (
        (dragInput == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
        or (dragInput ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and dragInput == input and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
    )
    if pointerMoved then
        local pointerDelta = input.Position - dragStart
        if pointerDelta.Magnitude > 3 then
            windowHasCustomPosition = true
        end

        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or gui.AbsoluteSize
        local shellSize = root.AbsoluteSize
        local dragSize = dragButton.AbsoluteSize
        local centerX = viewport.X * startPosition.X.Scale + startPosition.X.Offset + pointerDelta.X
        local centerY = viewport.Y * startPosition.Y.Scale + startPosition.Y.Offset + pointerDelta.Y

        -- The left edge can leave the screen, but the drag icon itself must remain visible.
        local minCenterX = 8 - shellSize.X / 2 + 6 + dragSize.X
        -- Keep the right edge of the shell on-screen so it cannot hide the drag icon.
        local maxCenterX = viewport.X - shellSize.X / 2 - 8
        if minCenterX > maxCenterX then
            maxCenterX = viewport.X - shellSize.X / 2 + 2
            minCenterX = math.min(minCenterX, maxCenterX)
        end
        centerX = math.clamp(centerX, minCenterX, maxCenterX)

        -- Top matches the right side: keep the complete shell inside the top margin.
        -- Bottom remains flexible, but the top-right drag handle must stay reachable.
        local dragOffsetY = dragButton.AbsolutePosition.Y - root.AbsolutePosition.Y
        local minCenterY = 8 + shellSize.Y / 2
        local maxCenterY = viewport.Y - 8 - dragOffsetY - dragSize.Y + shellSize.Y / 2
        if maxCenterY < minCenterY then
            centerY = minCenterY
        else
            centerY = math.clamp(centerY, minCenterY, maxCenterY)
        end

        root.Position = UDim2.new(
            startPosition.X.Scale,
            centerX - viewport.X * startPosition.X.Scale,
            startPosition.Y.Scale,
            centerY - viewport.Y * startPosition.Y.Scale
        )

        -- Rebase after each update. This prevents edge-clamping from feeling sticky:
        -- when the pointer reverses direction, the shell moves immediately.
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPosition = root.Position
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if (dragInput == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
        or (dragInput ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input == dragInput and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        dragging = false
        dragInput = nil
    end
end)


-- ============================================================================
-- LUMEN CONTROL BRIDGE
-- The Syllinse shell above (icon rail, title/search row, original panel geometry,
-- colors and drag behavior) stays intact. Lumen owns the actual sections, buttons,
-- toggles, sliders, dropdowns, keybinds, color pickers, popups and config handling.
-- Its own window chrome is hidden; Lumen's native page content is mounted into the
-- two existing Syllinse panels; the previous control backend is removed entirely.
-- ============================================================================

-- Clean up tagged Lumen GUI layers if this script is executed again.
-- Only instances created by this integration are removed.
local function clearPreviousSyllinseLumenLayers()
    -- Reuse the same lightweight Reptillian library instance lifecycle on re-execution.
    -- The source's Exit() is patched below not to touch the executor's generic Library global.
    local sharedEnvironment
    pcall(function()
        if type(getgenv) == "function" then sharedEnvironment = getgenv() end
    end)
    if type(sharedEnvironment) == "table" then
        local previousReptillian = sharedEnvironment.__SyllinseReptillianDropdownLibrary
        if previousReptillian and type(previousReptillian.Exit) == "function" then
            pcall(function() previousReptillian:Exit() end)
        end
        sharedEnvironment.__SyllinseReptillianDropdownLibrary = nil
    end

    local containers = { playerGui }
    local okCore, coreGui = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if okCore and coreGui then table.insert(containers, coreGui) end
    for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("ScreenGui") and (
                child:GetAttribute("SyllinseLumenBackend") == true
                or child:GetAttribute("SyllinseLumenOverlay") == true
                or child:GetAttribute("SyllinseReptillianDropdownBackend") == true
            ) then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
end
clearPreviousSyllinseLumenLayers()

local LUMEN_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/chromatiks/Lumen/main/Library.lua"
local function replaceLibraryText(source, needle, replacement)
    local first, last = string.find(source, needle, 1, true)
    if not first then
        return source, false
    end
    return string.sub(source, 1, first - 1) .. replacement .. string.sub(source, last + 1), true
end

local loadOk, LumenOrError = pcall(function()
    assert(type(loadstring) == "function", "loadstring is not available in this client environment")
    local librarySource = game:HttpGet(LUMEN_URL)

    -- Read edits immediately, including a Save click before the field loses focus.
    local didPatch
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        '\tBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()\n\t\tInput.Set(Box.Text)\n\tend)',
        '\tInput.GetText = function() return Box.Text end\n\tBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()\n\t\tInput.Value = Box.Text\n\tend)\n\tBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()\n\t\tInput.Set(Box.Text)\n\tend)'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not add live input text access to this Lumen version") end
    -- Forward-declare the searchable dropdown's Build before connecting its Text event.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        '\tlocal SearchBoxDrop = nil\n\tif Dropdown.Search then',
        '\tlocal SearchBoxDrop = nil\n\tlocal Build\n\tif Dropdown.Search then'
    )
    if not didPatch and not string.find(librarySource, '\tlocal Build\n\tif Dropdown.Search then', 1, true) then
        warn("[Syllinse] Lumen search compatibility patch did not match this library version")
    end
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        '\t\tSearchBoxDrop:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()\n\t\t\tBuild()\n\t\tend)\n\tend\n\n\tlocal function Build()',
        '\t\tSearchBoxDrop:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()\n\t\t\tif Build then Build() end\n\t\tend)\n\tend\n\n\tBuild = function()'
    )
    if not didPatch and not string.find(librarySource, "if Build then Build() end", 1, true) then
        warn("[Syllinse] Lumen dropdown Build compatibility patch did not match this library version")
    end

    -- Normalize dropdown options. This also prevents one malformed config/list update from
    -- feeding tables, callbacks, booleans, or Instances into Roblox TextButton.Name/Text.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        '}, propertyTable or {})\n\n\tlocal OptionList = Add("TextButton", { Parent = Library._Instance; Name = "Options";',
        '}, propertyTable or {})\n\tlocal CleanOptions = {}\n\tif type(Dropdown.Options) == "table" then\n\t\tfor _, Option in ipairs(Dropdown.Options) do\n\t\t\tif type(Option) == "string" or type(Option) == "number" then\n\t\t\t\ttable.insert(CleanOptions, tostring(Option))\n\t\t\tend\n\t\tend\n\tend\n\tDropdown.Options = CleanOptions\n\n\tlocal OptionList = Add("TextButton", { Parent = Library._Instance; Name = "Options";'
    )
    if not didPatch then
        warn("[Syllinse] Lumen initial dropdown sanitizing patch did not match this library version")
    end

    -- Make UpdateOptions accept both dot and colon call styles, and accept only text options.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Dropdown.UpdateOptions = function(NewOptions: { string })\n\t\tDropdown.Options = NewOptions',
        'Dropdown.UpdateOptions = function(NewOptions, LegacyOptions)\n\t\tif NewOptions == Dropdown then NewOptions = LegacyOptions end\n\t\tlocal CleanOptions = {}\n\t\tif type(NewOptions) == "table" then\n\t\t\tfor _, Option in ipairs(NewOptions) do\n\t\t\t\tif type(Option) == "string" or type(Option) == "number" then table.insert(CleanOptions, tostring(Option)) end\n\t\t\tend\n\t\tend\n\t\tNewOptions = CleanOptions\n\t\tDropdown.Options = NewOptions'
    )
    if not didPatch then
        warn("[Syllinse] Lumen UpdateOptions compatibility patch did not match this library version")
    end

    -- Make Input:Set safe even if any caller accidentally uses method-style colon syntax.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Input.Set = function(Value: string)\n\t\tInput.Value = Value\n\t\tBox.Text = Value',
        'Input.Set = function(Value, LegacyValue)\n\t\tif Value == Input then Value = LegacyValue end\n\t\tif type(Value) ~= "string" then Value = tostring(Value or "") end\n\t\tInput.Value = Value\n\t\tBox.Text = Value'
    )
    if not didPatch then
        warn("[Syllinse] Lumen Input:Set compatibility patch did not match this library version")
    end

    -- Disable the library's drag-and-drop section placement so panels cannot swap sides.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(librarySource, 'local DragThreshold = 6', 'local DragThreshold = math.huge')
    if not didPatch then
        warn("[Syllinse] Could not lock Lumen section placement for this library version")
    end

    -- Refine control typography and section-header proportions while keeping the header surface clean.
    -- Raise small native Lumen text sizes before the library is compiled.
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource, 'TextSize = 13;', 'TextSize = 15;')
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource, 'TextSize = 12;', 'TextSize = 14;')
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource, 'TextSize = 11;', 'TextSize = 13;')
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource, 'Size = UD2(1, 0, 0, 22);', 'Size = UD2(1, 0, 0, 25);')
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'local Header = Add("Frame", {\n\t\t\tParent = SectionFrame;\n\t\t\tName = "Header";\n\t\t\tBackgroundColor3 = RGB(20, 20, 21);\n\t\t\tBorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0);\n\t\t\tBorderSizePixel = 0;\n\t\t\tSize = UD2(1, 0, 0, 35);\n\t\t}) :: Frame',
        'local Header = Add("Frame", {\n\t\t\tParent = SectionFrame;\n\t\t\tName = "Header";\n\t\t\tBackgroundColor3 = RGB(20, 20, 21);\n\t\t\tBorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0);\n\t\t\tBorderSizePixel = 0;\n\t\t\tSize = UD2(1, 0, 0, 38);\n\t\t}) :: Frame'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not enlarge Lumen section headers in this library version") end

    -- Center the section title within the full header height; no extra divider is drawn beneath it.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Add("UIPadding", { Parent = Header; PaddingLeft = UD(0, 10); })',
        'Add("UIPadding", { Parent = Header; PaddingLeft = UD(0, 10); PaddingTop = UD(0, 0); PaddingBottom = UD(0, 0); })'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not vertically center Lumen section headers") end
    -- Make each section card's main surface explicitly opaque so the panel color fills its full body.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'local SectionFrame = Add("Frame", {\n\t\t\tParent = Column;\n\t\t\tName = "Section";\n\t\t\tAutomaticSize = AS.Y;\n\t\t\tBackgroundColor3 = RGB(15, 14, 15);\n\t\t\tBorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0);',
        'local SectionFrame = Add("Frame", {\n\t\t\tParent = Column;\n\t\t\tName = "Section";\n\t\t\tAutomaticSize = AS.Y;\n\t\t\tBackgroundColor3 = RGB(15, 14, 15);\n\t\t\tBackgroundTransparency = 0;\n\t\t\tBorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0);'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not enforce opaque Lumen section card backgrounds") end

    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'local Elements = Add("Frame", {\n\t\t\tParent = SectionFrame;\n\t\t\tName = "Elements";\n\t\t\tAutomaticSize = AS.Y;\n\t\t\tBackgroundColor3 = RGB(255, 255, 255);\n\t\t\tBackgroundTransparency = 1;\n\t\t\tBorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0);\n\t\t\tBorderSizePixel = 0;\n\t\t\tPosition = UFO(0, 35);\n\t\t\tSize = UFS(1, 0);\n\t\t}) :: Frame',
        'local Elements = Add("Frame", {\n\t\t\tParent = SectionFrame;\n\t\t\tName = "Elements";\n\t\t\tAutomaticSize = AS.Y;\n\t\t\tBackgroundColor3 = RGB(255, 255, 255);\n\t\t\tBackgroundTransparency = 1;\n\t\t\tBorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0);\n\t\t\tBorderSizePixel = 0;\n\t\t\tPosition = UFO(0, 38);\n\t\t\tSize = UFS(1, 0);\n\t\t}) :: Frame'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not align Lumen section content below its larger header") end

    -- Appearance manages the filled header band and its single-line separator.


    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Library.ThemeLink(SectionFrame, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface")\n\t\tLibrary.ThemeLink(SectionStroke, "Color", "SectionBorder")',
        'Library.ThemeLink(Header, "BackgroundColor3", "SyllinsePanelHeader")\n\t\tLibrary.ThemeLink(SectionFrame, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface")\n\t\tLibrary.ThemeLink(SectionStroke, "Color", "SectionBorder")'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not link Lumen section headers to the Syllinse theme") end
    librarySource = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Add("UIListLayout", { Parent = Elements; Padding = UD(0, 10); SortOrder = SO.LayoutOrder; })',
        'Add("UIListLayout", { Parent = Elements; Padding = UD(0, 13); SortOrder = SO.LayoutOrder; })'
    )

    -- Add a touch more space between section cards so the taller header/divider treatment does not feel cramped.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Add("UIListLayout", { Parent = Column; Padding = UD(0, 12); SortOrder = SO.LayoutOrder; })',
        'Add("UIListLayout", { Parent = Column; Padding = UD(0, 14); SortOrder = SO.LayoutOrder; })' 
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not increase spacing between Lumen section cards") end

    -- Give Lumen's native inactive toggle track a visible neutral charcoal instead of near-black.
    -- This source-level fallback applies even before the runtime state styling runs.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        [[local Button = Add("TextButton", { Parent = self.LeftContent; Name = "Toggle"; AutoButtonColor = false; BackgroundColor3 = RGB(20, 20, 21);]],
        [[local Button = Add("TextButton", { Parent = self.LeftContent; Name = "Toggle"; AutoButtonColor = false; BackgroundColor3 = RGB(78, 76, 76);]]
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not set initial Lumen toggle tracks to visible charcoal gray") end

    -- Turn the caption beside every switch into a transparent TextButton. It keeps
    -- the same text/layout but makes the caption a second native activation target.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Add("TextLabel", { LayoutOrder = 99; Parent = LeftContent; Name = "Label";',
        'Add("TextButton", { LayoutOrder = 99; Parent = LeftContent; Name = "Label"; AutoButtonColor = false;'
    )
    if not didPatch and not string.find(librarySource,
        'Add("TextButton", { LayoutOrder = 99; Parent = LeftContent; Name = "Label"; AutoButtonColor = false;', 1, true) then
        warn("[Syllinse] Could not make Lumen toggle captions clickable in this library version")
    end

    -- Keep Lumen's stock accent-gradient binding from overwriting the manually
    -- configured Syllinse ON color after a theme change.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Library.ThemeLink(ToggleGrad, "Gradient", "AccentDark", "Accent")',
        '-- Syllinse applies the switch ON palette from SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE at runtime.'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not detach the native toggle gradient theme binding") end

    -- Use Lumen's native action-button dimensions, hover and click behavior.

    -- Panel controls use a subdued gray palette instead of the library's hard-coded white.
    -- This affects widget captions, slider values, dropdown/input text, and placeholder text.
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource, 'TextColor3 = RGB%(255, 255, 255%)', 'TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text')
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource, 'or RGB%(255, 255, 255%)', 'or Library.Theme.Text')
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource, 'PlaceholderColor3 = RGB%(255, 255, 255%)', 'PlaceholderColor3 = Library.Theme.TextDim')
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Name = Option; AutoButtonColor = false; BackgroundColor3 = RGB(20, 20, 21); BackgroundTransparency = 1; BorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0); BorderSizePixel = 0; FontFace = FN("rbxassetid://12187365364", FW.SemiBold, FS.Normal); LayoutOrder = Index; Size = UD2(1, 0, 0, 22); Text = Option; TextColor3 = Library.Theme.Text; TextSize = 15; TextTransparency = 0.5; TextWrapped = true; TextXAlignment = TXA.Left; }) :: TextButton',
        'Name = Option; AutoButtonColor = false; BackgroundColor3 = RGB(20, 20, 21); BackgroundTransparency = 1; BorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0); BorderSizePixel = 0; FontFace = FN("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", FW.SemiBold, FS.Normal); LayoutOrder = Index; Size = UD2(1, 0, 0, 22); Text = Option; TextColor3 = RGB(224, 223, 223); TextSize = 15; TextTransparency = 0.05; TextWrapped = true; TextXAlignment = TXA.Left; }) :: TextButton'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not brighten Lumen dropdown popup options") end

    -- Tint section-heading icons and field/dropdown chevrons with the panel text color.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Image = ResolveIcon(Section.Icon);\n\t\t\tPosition = UFS(0, 0.5);',
        'Image = ResolveIcon(Section.Icon);\n\t\t\tImageColor3 = Library.Theme.TextDim;\n\t\t\tPosition = UFS(0, 0.5);'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not tint Lumen section icons") end


    -- Use consistent text-only headers for all Lumen section cards. Hiding the image at
    -- zero size and removing layout padding means titles line up with the header's left inset.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Add("ImageLabel", {\n\t\t\tParent = Header;\n\t\t\tName = "Icon";\n\t\t\tBackgroundColor3 = RGB(255, 255, 255);\n\t\t\tBackgroundTransparency = 1;\n\t\t\tBorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0);\n\t\t\tBorderSizePixel = 0;\n\t\t\tImage = ResolveIcon(Section.Icon);\n\t\t\tImageColor3 = Library.Theme.TextDim;\n\t\t\tPosition = UFS(0, 0.5);\n\t\t\tSize = UFO(16, 16);\n\t\t})\n\t\tAdd("UIListLayout", {\n\t\t\tParent = Header;\n\t\t\tFillDirection = FD.Horizontal;\n\t\t\tPadding = UD(0, 5);\n\t\t\tSortOrder = SO.LayoutOrder;\n\t\t\tVerticalAlignment = VFA.Center;\n\t\t})',
        'Add("ImageLabel", {\n\t\t\tParent = Header;\n\t\t\tName = "Icon";\n\t\t\tBackgroundTransparency = 1;\n\t\t\tBorderSizePixel = 0;\n\t\t\tImage = "";\n\t\t\tPosition = UFS(0, 0.5);\n\t\t\tSize = UFO(0, 0);\n\t\t\tVisible = false;\n\t\t})\n\t\tAdd("UIListLayout", {\n\t\t\tParent = Header;\n\t\t\tFillDirection = FD.Horizontal;\n\t\t\tPadding = UD(0, 0);\n\t\t\tSortOrder = SO.LayoutOrder;\n\t\t\tVerticalAlignment = VFA.Center;\n\t\t})'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not remove Lumen section header icons in this library version") end
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource,
        'Image = "rbxassetid://95865107607162"; ImageTransparency = 0.2;',
        'Image = "rbxassetid://95865107607162"; ImageColor3 = Library.Theme.TextDim; ImageTransparency = 0.2;')
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource,
        'Image = "rbxassetid://96386750273341"; ImageTransparency = 0.2;',
        'Image = "rbxassetid://96386750273341"; ImageColor3 = Library.Theme.TextDim; ImageTransparency = 0.2;')

    -- Give slider rails a restrained gray treatment and make the rounded slider thumb 14 px.
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource,
        'Name = "Button"; AutoButtonColor = false; BackgroundColor3 = RGB%(20, 20, 21%); BorderColor3 = RGB%(0, 0, 0%); BorderSizePixel = 0; FontFace = FN%("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", FW%.Regular, FS%.Normal%); Position = UFO%(0, 18%); Size = UD2%(1, 0, 0, 12%);',
        'Name = "Button"; AutoButtonColor = false; BackgroundColor3 = RGB(64, 62, 62); BorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0); BorderSizePixel = 0; FontFace = FN("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", FW.Regular, FS.Normal); Position = UFO(0, 18); Size = UD2(1, 0, 0, 12);')
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource,
        'Name = "Circle"; AnchorPoint = V2%(1, 0%.5%); BackgroundColor3 = RGB%(0, 0, 0%);',
        'Name = "Circle"; AnchorPoint = V2(1, 0.5); BackgroundColor3 = RGB(112, 110, 110);')
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'local Circle = Add("Frame", { Parent = Button; Name = "Circle"; AnchorPoint = V2(1, 0.5); BackgroundColor3 = RGB(112, 110, 110); BorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0); BorderSizePixel = 0; Position = UD2(1, -3, 0.5, 0); Size = UFO(8, 8); }) :: Frame',
        'local Circle = Add("Frame", { Parent = Button; Name = "Circle"; AnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0.5); BackgroundColor3 = RGB(220, 218, 218); BorderColor3 = RGB(0, 0, 0); BorderSizePixel = 0; Position = UD2(0, 8, 0.5, 0); Size = UFO(16, 16); }) :: Frame'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not enlarge and center Lumen slider handles") end
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Tween(Circle, { Position = UD2(Alpha, -3, 0.5, 0); BackgroundTransparency = Fade }, 0.08)',
        'Tween(Circle, { Position = UD2(Alpha, 8 - 16 * Alpha, 0.5, 0); BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, 0.08)'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not align Lumen slider handles with the active fill") end

    -- Stop the filled rail at the thumb's center. The old full-width Alpha sizing let
    -- the rounded right UICorner stick out from behind the thumb, most noticeably at 100%.
    -- Matching the fill endpoint to the thumb center keeps the rounded cap hidden underneath it.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Tween(Overlay, { Size = UD2(Alpha, 0, 1, 0); BackgroundTransparency = Fade }, 0.08)',
        'Tween(Overlay, { Size = UD2(Alpha, 8 - 16 * Alpha, 1, 0); BackgroundTransparency = Fade }, 0.08)'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not align the slider fill endpoint under its thumb") end

    -- Keep the slider thumb fully on the rail at both endpoints, and map clicks through
    -- the usable center-to-center travel so the rightmost thumb is still easy to grab.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'BindSlider(Button, function(Alpha)\n\t\tSlider.Set(Slider.Min + (Slider.Max - Slider.Min) * Alpha.X)\n\tend)',
        'BindSlider(Button, function(Alpha)\n\t\tlocal Width = Button.AbsoluteSize.X\n\t\tlocal Inset = math.min(8, Width * 0.5)\n\t\tlocal UsableWidth = math.max(Width - Inset * 2, 1)\n\t\tlocal AdjustedAlpha = MC((Alpha.X * Width - Inset) / UsableWidth, 0, 1)\n\t\tSlider.Set(Slider.Min + (Slider.Max - Slider.Min) * AdjustedAlpha)\n\tend)'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not apply Lumen slider endpoint hitbox fix") end

    -- Keep slider fills theme-linked, with separate roles for the original gray palette.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'local SliderGrad = Add("UIGradient", { Parent = Overlay; Color = CS{ CSK(0, Library.Theme.AccentDark), CSK(1, Library.Theme.Accent) }; Rotation = -90; })\n\tLibrary.ThemeLink(SliderGrad, "Gradient", "AccentDark", "Accent")',
        'local SliderGrad = Add("UIGradient", { Parent = Overlay; Color = CS{ CSK(0, Library.Theme.SliderFillBottom), CSK(1, Library.Theme.SliderFillTop) }; Rotation = -90; })\n\tLibrary.ThemeLink(SliderGrad, "Gradient", "SliderFillBottom", "SliderFillTop")'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not recolor Lumen slider fill; the upstream source may have changed") end


    -- Remove Lumen's logo markers from the watermark and keybind list while keeping
    -- their native layouts and the overlay's original widget lifecycle.
    local function replaceLibrarySpan(source, startMarker, endMarker, replacement)
        local first = string.find(source, startMarker, 1, true)
        if not first then return source, false end
        local last = string.find(source, endMarker, first + #startMarker, true)
        if not last then return source, false end
        return string.sub(source, 1, first - 1) .. replacement .. string.sub(source, last), true
    end

    local watermarkLogoBlock = [[local Logo = Add("Frame", {
        Parent = Frame;
        Name = "SyllinseWatermarkNoLogo";
        BackgroundTransparency = 1;
        BorderSizePixel = 0;
        Size = UFO(0, 0);
        LayoutOrder = 0;
        Visible = false;
        ZIndex = 121;
    })
]]
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibrarySpan(
        librarySource,
        'local Logo = Add("Frame", {',
        '\tlocal function Chip(Text: string, Order: number, Dim: boolean?): TextLabel',
        watermarkLogoBlock
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not remove Lumen watermark logo") end

    -- A zero-size invisible placeholder keeps the library's Accent reference valid, without rendering a logo.
    local keybindLogoBlock = [[local HeaderIcon = Add("Frame", {
        Parent = Header;
        Name = "SyllinseKeybindNoLogo";
        BackgroundTransparency = 1;
        BorderSizePixel = 0;
        Size = UFO(0, 0);
        Position = UFO(0, 0);
        Visible = false;
        ZIndex = 122;
    })
]]
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibrarySpan(
        librarySource,
        'local HeaderIcon = Add("Frame", {',
        '\tAdd("TextLabel", {\n\t\tParent = Header;',
        keybindLogoBlock
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not remove Lumen keybind-list logo") end

    -- With no header icon, start the label at the left edge of the keybind header.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Position = UFO(16, 0);\n\t\tSize = UD2(1, -16, 1, 0);\n\t\tFontFace = FN("rbxassetid://12187365364", FW.SemiBold, FS.Normal);\n\t\tText = "Keybind list";',
        'Position = UFO(0, 0);\n\t\tSize = UD2(1, 0, 1, 0);\n\t\tFontFace = FN("rbxassetid://12187365364", FW.SemiBold, FS.Normal);\n\t\tText = "Keybind list";'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not align the logo-free keybind-list title") end

    -- Position the watermark from the top-left so its drag bounds clamp correctly to the screen.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'Name = "Watermark";\n\t\tAnchorPoint = V2(0.5, 0);\n\t\tPosition = UD2(0.5, 0, 0, 12);',
        'Name = "Watermark";\n\t\tAnchorPoint = V2(0, 0);\n\t\tPosition = UD2(0, 18, 0, 12);'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not set the watermark's draggable anchor position") end

    -- Remove the cool tint from watermark stats so the overlay is consistently grayscale.
    librarySource = string.gsub(librarySource,
        'TextColor3 = Dim and RGB%(150, 154, 170%) or RGB%(235, 236, 240%);',
        'TextColor3 = Dim and RGB(139, 137, 137) or RGB(224, 223, 223);')

    -- Make Hold/Toggle selection use the UI's neutral charcoal palette instead of a blue/white fill.
    local keybindModeBlock = [[local function Mode(Button: TextButton, Gradient: UIGradient, Label: TextLabel, Active: boolean)
            Gradient.Enabled = false
            Tween(Button, {
                BackgroundColor3 = Active and RGB(64, 62, 62) or RGB(34, 33, 33),
            }, 0.15)
            Tween(Label, {
                TextColor3 = Active and RGB(224, 223, 223) or RGB(139, 137, 137),
                TextTransparency = Active and 0 or 0.12,
            }, 0.15)
        end]]
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibrarySpan(
        librarySource,
        'local function Mode(Button: TextButton, Gradient: UIGradient, Label: TextLabel, Active: boolean)',
        '\n\t\tMode(HoldButton, HoldGradient, HoldLabel, Held)',
        keybindModeBlock
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not recolor Hold/Toggle selection controls") end

    -- Watermark frames are recreated by SetWatermark, so bind dragging at the source creation site.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        '\tLibrary.Watermark.Frame = Frame\n\tLibrary.Watermark.Label = Title',
        '\tFrame.Active = true\n\tBindDrag(Frame, Frame, true)\n\tFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()\n\t\tlocal P = Frame.Position\n\t\tLibrary.Watermark.SavedPos = { X = P.X.Offset; Y = P.Y.Offset }\n\tend)\n\tif Library.Watermark.SavedPos then\n\t\tFrame.Position = ClampToScreen(Frame, UFO(Library.Watermark.SavedPos.X, Library.Watermark.SavedPos.Y))\n\telse\n\t\tFrame.Position = ClampToScreen(Frame, Frame.Position)\n\tend\n\tLibrary.Watermark.Frame = Frame\n\tLibrary.Watermark.Label = Title'
    )
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not attach drag handling to the watermark") end

    -- Make toggle notifications opt-in per feature. Each eligible feature row gets its own bell;
    -- the global Settings > Notifications toggle remains the master on/off switch.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(
        librarySource,
        'if not Silent and Library.NotifyToggles and Library.Notify then',
        'if not Silent and Library.NotifyToggles and Library.Notify and Toggle.NotifyEnabled == true then'
    )
    if not didPatch then
        warn("[Syllinse] Could not patch Lumen's per-feature notification gate")
    end

    -- Let Syllinse place native popups beneath their controls without a competing screen-space tween.
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(librarySource,
        'Tween(Popup, { Position = Target }, 0.25, ES.Quint)',
        'if not Library._SyllinsePositionControlPopup or not Library._SyllinsePositionControlPopup(Keybind) then Tween(Popup, { Position = Target }, 0.25, ES.Quint) end')
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not connect keybind popup positioning") end
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(librarySource,
        'Tween(ColorpickerFrame, { Position = Target }, 0.25, ES.Quint)',
        'if not Library._SyllinsePositionControlPopup or not Library._SyllinsePositionControlPopup(Colorpicker) then Tween(ColorpickerFrame, { Position = Target }, 0.25, ES.Quint) end')
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not connect color picker popup positioning") end
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(librarySource,
        'local function Mode(Button: TextButton, Gradient: UIGradient, Label: TextLabel, Active: boolean)',
        'local function Mode(Button: TextButton, Gradient: UIGradient, Label: TextLabel, Active: boolean)\n\t\t\tif Library._SyllinseStyleKeybindMode then Library._SyllinseStyleKeybindMode(Button, Gradient, Label, Active); return end')
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not connect keybind popup mode colors") end
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(librarySource,
        'local Root = Popup.Frame or Popup._Frame',
        'local Root = Popup._SyllinseViewport or Popup.Frame or Popup._Frame')
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not connect popup viewport click bounds") end
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(librarySource,
        'Handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)\n\t\tif Input.UserInputType',
        'Handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)\n\t\tif Object:GetAttribute("SyllinseManagedPopupDrag") then return end\n\t\tif Input.UserInputType')
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not route native popup dragging") end
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(librarySource,
        'Object.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)',
        'Object.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)\n\t\tif Library._SyllinsePopupBlocksInteraction and Library._SyllinsePopupBlocksInteraction(Object) then return end')
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not protect covered sliders from popup input") end
    librarySource, didPatch = replaceLibraryText(librarySource,
        'local function Update(Input: InputObject)\n\t\tlocal Origin = Object.AbsolutePosition',
        'local function Update(Input: InputObject)\n\t\tif Library._SyllinsePopupBlocksInteraction and Library._SyllinsePopupBlocksInteraction(Object) then return end\n\t\tlocal Origin = Object.AbsolutePosition')
    if not didPatch then warn("[Syllinse] Could not protect covered slider drags") end

    local compileLibrary = loadstring(librarySource)
    assert(type(compileLibrary) == "function", "Lumen source could not be compiled after compatibility patches")
    return compileLibrary()
end)
assert(loadOk and type(LumenOrError) == "table" and type(LumenOrError.Window) == "function",
    "Could not load Lumen UI library: " .. tostring(LumenOrError))
Lumen = LumenOrError
if Lumen._Instance then
    Lumen._Instance:SetAttribute("SyllinseLumenBackend", true)
    Lumen._Instance.IgnoreGuiInset = true
end

-- Match Lumen's actual widget styling to the warm charcoal Syllinse palette; native panel text stays muted gray.
Lumen.SetConfigFolder("Syllinse")
Lumen.MenuKey = nil -- RightControl belongs to the Syllinse shell, not the hidden Lumen window.
Lumen.NotifyToggles = false -- Uses the Syllinse-skinned Magik-style notification renderer.
Lumen.Theme.Accent = Theme.Accent
Lumen.Theme.AccentDark = Theme.Muted
Lumen.Theme.SliderFillBottom = Theme.SliderFillBottom
Lumen.Theme.SliderFillTop = Theme.SliderFillTop
Lumen.Theme.Background = Theme.Root
Lumen.Theme.SyllinsePanelHeader = getPanelHeaderColor()
Lumen.Theme.Surface = Theme.Surface
Lumen.Theme.SurfaceAlt = Theme.SurfaceHover
Lumen.Theme.Border = Theme.Stroke
Lumen.Theme.SectionBorder = Theme.Stroke
Lumen.Theme.Text = Theme.PanelText
Lumen.Theme.TextDim = Theme.PanelIcon
Lumen.SetWatermark("Syllinse Project", false)
Lumen.SetKeybindList(false)
if Lumen._Overlay then
    Lumen._Overlay:SetAttribute("SyllinseLumenOverlay", true)
    Lumen._Overlay.IgnoreGuiInset = true
end
Lumen.ApplyTheme()

local LumenWindow = Lumen:Window({
    Title = "Syllinse",
    Footer = "Syllinse UI",
})
assert(LumenWindow and LumenWindow.Canvas, "Lumen did not create its window")

-- Lumen's popups are on its own ScreenGui, so put them just above Syllinse's GUI.
-- Keep that ScreenGui enabled for dropdowns/keybind/colorpicker popups and notifications,
-- but hide the separate Lumen window chrome itself.
if Lumen._Instance then
    Lumen._Instance:SetAttribute("SyllinseLumenBackend", true)
    Lumen._Instance.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    Lumen._Instance.DisplayOrder = (gui.DisplayOrder or 30) + 1
    Lumen._Instance.Enabled = true
end
if Lumen._Overlay then
    Lumen._Overlay:SetAttribute("SyllinseLumenOverlay", true)
    Lumen._Overlay.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    Lumen._Overlay.DisplayOrder = (gui.DisplayOrder or 30) + 1
    Lumen._Overlay.Enabled = true
end
LumenWindow.Canvas.Visible = false

-- Lumen's option popups live in a separate ScreenGui. Apply native geometry and text
-- sizing there too, while preserving each popup's screen-space anchor position.
local dropdownPopupNames = {
    Options = true,
    DropdownPopup = true,
    DropdownList = true,
    DropdownOptions = true,
}

local function setPopupNativeScale(instance, targetScale)
    -- Popup root positions are already calculated from the control's screen-space
    -- anchor, so keep only the root position fixed and natively scale its size/content.
    applyNativeScaleToInstance(instance, targetScale, true)
    for _, descendant in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
        applyNativeScaleToInstance(descendant, targetScale)
    end
    if instance:GetAttribute("SyllinseNativePopupWatcher") ~= true then
        instance:SetAttribute("SyllinseNativePopupWatcher", true)
        instance.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            task.defer(function()
                if descendant and descendant:IsDescendantOf(instance)
                    and nativeScaledFactors[descendant] == nil then
                    applyNativeScaleToInstance(descendant, nativeGeometryScale)
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
end


local function syncDropdownPopupScale(instance)
    if not instance or not instance:IsA("GuiObject") then
        return
    end
    if not dropdownPopupNames[instance.Name] then
        return
    end
    if instance.Parent ~= Lumen._Instance and instance.Parent ~= Lumen._Overlay then
        return
    end

    local popupScale = instance:FindFirstChild("SyllinseDropdownScale")
    if not popupScale then
        popupScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        popupScale.Name = "SyllinseDropdownScale"
        popupScale.Parent = instance
    end
    popupScale.Scale = 1
    setPopupNativeScale(instance, responsiveScaleValue)
end


local function watchMobileDropdownPopups(screenLayer)
    if not screenLayer then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(screenLayer:GetChildren()) do
        syncDropdownPopupScale(child)
    end
    screenLayer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        task.defer(function()
            if child and child.Parent == screenLayer then
                syncDropdownPopupScale(child)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

watchMobileDropdownPopups(Lumen._Instance)
watchMobileDropdownPopups(Lumen._Overlay)

if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
    Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
        for _, layer in ipairs({ Lumen._Instance, Lumen._Overlay }) do
            if layer then
                for _, child in ipairs(layer:GetChildren()) do
                    syncDropdownPopupScale(child)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Config files in the source library include a legacy LeftAlt menu key by default.
-- Loading one must not bring the hidden Lumen window chrome back over Syllinse.
local loadConfigDataOriginal = Lumen.LoadConfigData
Lumen.LoadConfigData = function(data)
    if type(data) == "table" then
        data.MenuKey = nil
        if type(data.Flags) == "table" then
            data.Flags.ConfigName = nil
            data.Flags.SelectedConfig = nil
        end
    end
    local result = loadConfigDataOriginal(data)
    Lumen.MenuKey = nil
    return result
end

-- Edit these values to adjust dropdown options, search, and popup sizing.
-- Popup height fits the visible rows; OptionGap is only the space between rows.
local SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE = {
    OptionHeight = 22,        -- clickable option row height
    OptionGap = 12,           -- spacing between option rows
    OptionTextSize = 16,      -- option label text size
    ClosedTextSize = 16,      -- selected value text size when the dropdown is closed
    IndicatorSize = 22,       -- selection box size
    MaxPopupHeight = 200,     -- maximum dropdown popup height
    SearchTextSize = 17,      -- text size inside the search field (editable)
    SearchHeight = 26,        -- search field height in pixels (editable)
    SearchTop = 7,            -- distance from popup top to search field (editable)
    SearchGap = 8,            -- gap between search field and option list (editable)
    SearchBottomPadding = 10, -- padding below the option list (editable)
    SearchPlaceholder = "Search...", -- placeholder displayed in the search field
}

-- Match the closed control to one popup option slot without increasing panel content height.
-- Reptillian's original dropdown row reserves 51 px; retaining it avoids adding extra panel scroll.
local SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_FIELD_HEIGHT =
    SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.OptionHeight + SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.OptionGap
-- The closed selected-value text has its own editable size in the style table.
local SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_TEXT_SIZE =
    SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.ClosedTextSize
local SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_ICON_SIZE = math.max(16,
    math.floor(SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.IndicatorSize * 0.82 + 0.5))
local SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_ROW_HEIGHT = 51

-- Reptillian dropdown renderer only: no second Window/Page is created.
do
    local source = game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/BugBuster3000/sametlibs/refs/heads/main/Reptillian/Source")
    local patches = {
        { [[if getgenv().Library then

        getgenv().Library:Exit()

        getgenv().Library:Unload()

        source, patched = replaceLibraryText(source, patch[1], patch[2])
        assert(patched, "Reptillian source changed; refusing an incompatible load")
    end
    assert(not string.find(source, "getgenv().Library", 1, true), "Unsafe Reptillian global-library reference remains")

        "Reptillian Dropdown constructor was not found")
    ReptillianLibrary.UnregisterOpenFrame = function(self, frame)
        if type(self.OpenFrames) == "table" then self.OpenFrames[frame] = nil end
    end
