        and ReptillianLibrary.UnusedHolder and ReptillianLibrary.UnusedHolder.Instance,
        "Reptillian popup hosts were not initialized")
    ReptillianLibrary.Font, ReptillianLibrary.FontSize = FONT_REGULAR, 14
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownOptionTextSize = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.OptionTextSize
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownIndicatorSize = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.IndicatorSize
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownFieldHeight = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_FIELD_HEIGHT
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownFieldTextSize = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_TEXT_SIZE
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownFieldIconSize = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_ICON_SIZE
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownRowHeight = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_ROW_HEIGHT
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownSearchTextSize = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchTextSize
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownSearchHeight = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchHeight
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownSearchTop = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchTop
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownSearchListTop = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchTop
        + SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchHeight
        + SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchGap
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownSearchBottomPadding = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchBottomPadding
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownSearchHeaderHeight = ReptillianLibrary.DropdownSearchListTop
        + SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchBottomPadding
    ReptillianLibrary.DropdownSearchPlaceholder = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.SearchPlaceholder
    local colors = {
        Background = Theme.Root, Inline = Theme.Surface, Tab = getPanelHeaderColor(),
        Text = Theme.PanelText, ["Inactive Text"] = Theme.Muted, Accent = Theme.Accent,
        Outline = Theme.Stroke, ["Hovered Element"] = Theme.SurfaceHover,
    }
    for name, color in pairs(colors) do ReptillianLibrary:ChangeTheme(name, color) end
    for _, holder in ipairs({ ReptillianLibrary.Holder, ReptillianLibrary.NotifHolder, ReptillianLibrary.UnusedHolder }) do
        local layer = holder and holder.Instance
        if layer then
            layer:SetAttribute("SyllinseReptillianDropdownBackend", true)
            layer.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            layer.DisplayOrder = (gui.DisplayOrder or 30) + 2
        end
    end
    if ReptillianLibrary.Holder and ReptillianLibrary.Holder.Instance then
        ReptillianLibrary.Holder.Instance.Name = "SyllinseReptillianDropdownLayer"
    end
    pcall(function() getgenv().__SyllinseReptillianDropdownLibrary = ReptillianLibrary end)
end

local SyllinseRuntime = { reptillianDropdowns = {} }

-- Native keybind and color picker popups share the shell's palette and icon anchors.
SyllinseRuntime.nativeControlPopups = {}

do
    local shield = Instance.new("TextButton")
    shield.Name = "SyllinsePopupInputShield"
    shield.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    shield.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    shield.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    shield.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shield.AutoButtonColor = false
    shield.Text = ""
    shield.Active = true
    shield.Selectable = false
    shield.ZIndex = 99
    shield.Visible = false
    shield.Parent = motionBody
    SyllinseRuntime.nativePopupShield = shield
    shield.Activated:Connect(function()
        local entry = SyllinseRuntime.activeNativeControlPopup
        if entry and type(entry.Control.Close) == "function" then entry.Control.Close() end
    end)
end

Lumen._SyllinsePopupBlocksInteraction = function(object)
    local entry = SyllinseRuntime.activeNativeControlPopup
    if not entry or not entry.Popup.Visible or not entry.Viewport.Visible then return false end
    local viewport = entry.Viewport
    return not object or (object ~= viewport and not object:IsDescendantOf(viewport))
end

SyllinseRuntime.focusNativeControlPopup = function(entry, focused)
    if focused then
        SyllinseRuntime.activeNativeControlPopup = entry
    elseif SyllinseRuntime.activeNativeControlPopup == entry then
        SyllinseRuntime.activeNativeControlPopup = nil
        for _, candidate in pairs(SyllinseRuntime.nativeControlPopups) do
            if candidate ~= entry and candidate.Popup.Visible and candidate.Viewport.Visible then
                SyllinseRuntime.activeNativeControlPopup = candidate
                break
            end
        end
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.nativePopupShield.Visible = SyllinseRuntime.activeNativeControlPopup ~= nil
end

-- The dropdown renderer also uses MouseEnter, which fires behind overlapping GUI.
if ReptillianLibrary and type(ReptillianLibrary.OnHover) == "function" then
    local nativeOnHover = ReptillianLibrary.OnHover
    ReptillianLibrary.OnHover = function(self, enter, leave)
        return nativeOnHover(self, function(...)
            if Lumen._SyllinsePopupBlocksInteraction(self.Instance) then return end
            if enter then return enter(...) end
        end, leave)
    end
end

Lumen._SyllinseStyleKeybindMode = function(button, gradient, label, active)
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.BackgroundColor3 = active and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Theme.SurfaceHover
    gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Theme.Surface:Lerp(Theme.Accent, 0.16), Theme.Surface:Lerp(Theme.Accent, 0.28))
    gradient.Enabled = active
    label.TextColor3 = active and Theme.PanelText or Theme.PanelSecondary
    label.TextTransparency = 0
end

SyllinseRuntime.styleNativeControlPopup = function(entry)
    local popup, control = entry.Popup, entry.Control
    if not popup.Parent then return end
    popup.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
    popup.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    local header = popup:FindFirstChild("Header")
    if header then header.BackgroundColor3 = getPanelHeaderColor() end
    for _, object in ipairs(popup:GetDescendants()) do
        if object:IsA("UIStroke") then
            -- The color cursor needs its white ring against the full color spectrum.
            if not object.Parent or object.Parent.Name ~= "SaturationCursor" then object.Color = Theme.Stroke end
        elseif object:IsA("TextLabel") or object:IsA("TextButton") or object:IsA("TextBox") then
            object.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelText
            object.TextTransparency = 0
            if object.Name == "KeyButton" then
                object.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
                object.AutoButtonColor = false
            end
        elseif object:IsA("ImageButton") and object.Name == "CloseButton" then
            object.ImageColor3 = Theme.PanelIcon
            object.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            object.AutoButtonColor = false
        end
    end
    if entry.Kind == "Keybind" then
        local page = popup:FindFirstChild("Page")
        local options = page and page:FindFirstChild("Options")
        for _, mode in ipairs({ "Hold", "Toggle" }) do
            local button = options and options:FindFirstChild(mode .. "Button")
            local gradient = button and button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
            local label = button and button:FindFirstChild("Label")
            if gradient and label then Lumen._SyllinseStyleKeybindMode(button, gradient, label, control.Type == mode) end
        end
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.bindNativeControlPopup = function(control, anchor, kind)
    local popup = control and control.Frame
    if not (popup and anchor and popup:IsA("GuiObject") and anchor:IsA("GuiObject")) then return end
    if SyllinseRuntime.nativeControlPopups[control] then return end
    local entry = { Control = control, Popup = popup, Anchor = anchor, Kind = kind }
    SyllinseRuntime.nativeControlPopups[control] = entry
    local connections, syncing, destroyed = {}, false, false
    local draggedPosition, stopDrag
    -- Share the shell's coordinate space and UIScale instead of a separate ScreenGui.
    -- A short viewport scrolls the popup content; it never flips above its icon.
    local viewport = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    viewport.Name = "SyllinseControlPopupViewport"
    viewport.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    viewport.BorderSizePixel = 0
    viewport.ClipsDescendants = true
    viewport.Active = true
    viewport.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    viewport.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    viewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    viewport.ScrollBarThickness = 0
    viewport.ZIndex = 100
    viewport.Visible = false
    viewport.Parent = motionBody
    popup.Parent = viewport
    popup.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    control._SyllinseViewport = viewport
    entry.Viewport = viewport

    local function close()
        if type(control.Close) == "function" then control.Close() else popup.Visible = false end
    end
    local function anchorShown()
        if not gui.Enabled or not root.Visible or not anchor.Parent then return false end
        local center = anchor.AbsolutePosition + anchor.AbsoluteSize / 2
        local ancestor = anchor
        while ancestor and ancestor ~= gui do
            if ancestor:IsA("GuiObject") then
                if not ancestor.Visible then return false end
                if ancestor.ClipsDescendants then
                    local position, size = ancestor.AbsolutePosition, ancestor.AbsoluteSize
                    if center.X < position.X or center.Y < position.Y
                        or center.X > position.X + size.X or center.Y > position.Y + size.Y then return false end
                end
            end
            ancestor = ancestor.Parent
        end
        return true
    end
    entry.Sync = function()
        if destroyed or syncing or not popup.Visible then return end
        if not anchorShown() then close(); return end
        syncing = true
        local shellScale = math.max(uiScale.Scale, 0.001)
        local origin, bounds = motionBody.AbsolutePosition, motionBody.AbsoluteSize / shellScale
        local size = popup.AbsoluteSize / shellScale
        local position = (anchor.AbsolutePosition - origin) / shellScale
        local anchorSize = anchor.AbsoluteSize / shellScale
        local width = math.min(size.X, math.max(1, bounds.X - 20))
        local x = math.clamp(draggedPosition and draggedPosition.X or position.X + anchorSize.X - width,
            10, math.max(10, bounds.X - 10 - width))
        local y = position.Y + anchorSize.Y + 6
        if draggedPosition then
            y = math.clamp(draggedPosition.Y, 10, math.max(10, bounds.Y - 10 - size.Y))
            draggedPosition = Vector2.new(x, y)
        end
        local height = math.min(size.Y, math.max(0, bounds.Y - 10 - y))
        viewport.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
        viewport.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)
        viewport.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(width, size.Y)
        viewport.ScrollBarThickness = height < size.Y and 3 or 0
        viewport.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Stroke
        popup.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        popup.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        syncing = false
    end
    local function connect(event, callback) table.insert(connections, event:Connect(callback)) end
    connect(popup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), function()
        if stopDrag then stopDrag() end
        draggedPosition = nil
        viewport.Visible = popup.Visible
        SyllinseRuntime.focusNativeControlPopup(entry, popup.Visible)
        if popup.Visible then
            viewport.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            SyllinseRuntime.styleNativeControlPopup(entry)
            entry.Sync()
            -- AutomaticSize on the color picker can settle after it becomes visible.
            task.defer(entry.Sync)
        end
    end)
    connect(popup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"), entry.Sync)
    -- Keep native open positioning from moving the content inside its viewport.
    connect(popup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"), entry.Sync)
    local header = popup:FindFirstChild("Header")
    if header then
        popup:SetAttribute("SyllinseManagedPopupDrag", true)
        connect(header.InputBegan, function(input)
            local inputType = input.UserInputType
            local isTouch = inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            if (not isTouch and inputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
                or stopDrag or SyllinseRuntime.activeNativeControlPopup ~= entry then return end

            local pointer = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
            local closeButton = header:FindFirstChild("CloseButton")
            if closeButton and closeButton.Visible then
                local offset = pointer - closeButton.AbsolutePosition
                local size = closeButton.AbsoluteSize
                if offset.X >= 0 and offset.Y >= 0 and offset.X <= size.X and offset.Y <= size.Y then return end
            end

            local grabOffset = pointer - viewport.AbsolutePosition
            local scrollEnabled = viewport.ScrollingEnabled
            viewport.ScrollingEnabled = false
            local move = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(changed)
                if isTouch then
                    if changed ~= input then return end
                elseif changed.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                    return
                end
                local position = Vector2.new(changed.Position.X, changed.Position.Y)
                draggedPosition = (position - grabOffset - motionBody.AbsolutePosition) / math.max(uiScale.Scale, 0.001)
                entry.Sync()
            end)
            local ended, focusLost
            stopDrag = function()
                move:Disconnect()
                ended:Disconnect()
                focusLost:Disconnect()
                viewport.ScrollingEnabled = scrollEnabled
                stopDrag = nil
            end
            ended = input.Changed:Connect(function()
                local state = input.UserInputState
                if stopDrag and (state == Enum.UserInputState.End or state == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel) then stopDrag() end
            end)
            focusLost = UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(stopDrag)
        end)
    end
    connect(viewport:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), function()
        if not viewport.Visible and popup.Visible then close() end
    end)
    connect(anchor:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"), entry.Sync)
    connect(anchor:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"), entry.Sync)
    connect(motionBody:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"), entry.Sync)
    connect(motionBody:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"), entry.Sync)
    connect(uiScale:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"), entry.Sync)
    connect(gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"), entry.Sync)
    local ancestor = anchor
    while ancestor and ancestor ~= gui do
        if ancestor:IsA("GuiObject") then connect(ancestor:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), entry.Sync) end
        ancestor = ancestor.Parent
    end
    local function destroy()
        if destroyed then return end
        destroyed = true
        if stopDrag then stopDrag() end
        if popup.Visible then close() end
        SyllinseRuntime.focusNativeControlPopup(entry, false)
        SyllinseRuntime.nativeControlPopups[control] = nil
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        control._SyllinseViewport = nil
        viewport:Destroy()
    end
    connect(anchor.Destroying, destroy)
    connect(popup.Destroying, destroy)
    SyllinseRuntime.styleNativeControlPopup(entry)
end

Lumen._SyllinsePositionControlPopup = function(control)
    local entry = SyllinseRuntime.nativeControlPopups[control]
    if not entry then return false end
    entry.Sync()
    return true
end

SyllinseRuntime.refreshNativeControlPopups = function()
    for _, entry in pairs(SyllinseRuntime.nativeControlPopups) do
        SyllinseRuntime.styleNativeControlPopup(entry)
        entry.Sync()
    end
end

do
    for _, kind in ipairs({ "Keybind", "Colorpicker" }) do
        local nativeConstructor = Lumen.SubElements[kind]
        Lumen.SubElements[kind] = function(owner, ...)
            local control = nativeConstructor(owner, ...)
            local anchor = kind == "Keybind" and control.Settings or control.Button
            SyllinseRuntime.bindNativeControlPopup(control, anchor, kind)
            return control
        end
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.syncReptillianTheme = function()
    if not ReptillianLibrary then return end
    local colors = {
        Background = Theme.Root, Inline = Theme.Surface, Tab = getPanelHeaderColor(),
        Text = Theme.PanelText, ["Inactive Text"] = Theme.Muted, Accent = Theme.Accent,
        Outline = Theme.Stroke, ["Hovered Element"] = Theme.SurfaceHover,
    }
    for name, color in pairs(colors) do ReptillianLibrary:ChangeTheme(name, color) end
    for _, dropdown in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.reptillianDropdowns) do
        local items = dropdown.Items
        if items.Text and items.Text.Instance then items.Text.Instance.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelText end
        if items.Value and items.Value.Instance then items.Value.Instance.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelValue end
    end
end

-- Shared field geometry: dropdowns and textboxes use the same closed-field settings.
SyllinseRuntime.textboxRows = {}
SyllinseRuntime.styleFieldBorder = function(field)
    local stroke = field:FindFirstChild("SyllinseFieldOutline")
    if not stroke then
        stroke = addStroke(field, Theme.Stroke, 0, 1)
        stroke.Name = "SyllinseFieldOutline"
        pcall(function() stroke.BorderStrokePosition = Enum.BorderStrokePosition.Inner end)
    end
    stroke.Color, stroke.Thickness, stroke.Transparency = Theme.Stroke, 1, 0
    setManualCorner(field, 4)
end
SyllinseRuntime.styleDropdownField = function(dropdown)
    local items = dropdown.Items
    if not items then return end
    local outline = items.RealDropdownOutline and items.RealDropdownOutline.Instance
    local field = items.RealDropdown and items.RealDropdown.Instance
    local row = items.Dropdown and items.Dropdown.Instance or (outline and outline.Parent)
    if row then row:SetAttribute("SyllinseManagedDropdown", true) end
    if outline and outline.Parent then
        -- The outline is separate from the control's opaque fill.
        outline.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        outline.BorderSizePixel = 0
        SyllinseRuntime.styleFieldBorder(outline)
    end
    if field and field.Parent then
        field.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
        field.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        field.BorderSizePixel = 0
        setManualCorner(field, 4)
    end
    local icon = items.Icon and items.Icon.Instance
    if icon then
        icon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        icon.Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0)
        icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_ICON_SIZE, SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_ICON_SIZE)
        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        icon.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Default
        icon.ImageColor3 = Theme.Muted
    end
    local popup = items.OptionHolder and items.OptionHolder.Instance
    if popup then
        popup.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
        popup.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        local stroke = popup:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        if stroke then stroke.Color = Theme.Stroke end
        popup:SetAttribute("SyllinseManagedDropdown", true)
    end
    local search = items.Search and items.Search.Instance
    if search then
        search.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelText
        search.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.Muted
    end
    if items.Text and items.Text.Instance then items.Text.Instance:SetAttribute("SyllinseFieldText", true) end
    if items.Value and items.Value.Instance then items.Value.Instance:SetAttribute("SyllinseFieldText", true) end
end
SyllinseRuntime.styleTextboxRow = function(row)
    local field = row:FindFirstChild("InputFrame")
    local box = field and field:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextBox", true)
    if not box then return end
    row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_ROW_HEIGHT)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    field.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    field.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    field.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_FIELD_HEIGHT)
    field.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    field.ClipsDescendants = true
    field.BorderSizePixel = 0
    field.BackgroundColor3 = field:GetAttribute("SyllinseFieldHovered") and Theme.SurfaceHover or Theme.Root
    field.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    SyllinseRuntime.styleFieldBorder(field)
    local padding = field:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
    if padding then
        padding.PaddingLeft, padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 0), UDim.new(0, 0)
    end
    local icon = field:FindFirstChild("Icon")
    if icon then
        icon.Visible = true
        icon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        icon.Position = UDim2.new(1, -9, 0.5, 0)
        icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(17, 17)
        icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        icon.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Default
        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        icon.ImageColor3 = Theme.PanelIcon or Theme.PanelValue
        icon.ImageTransparency = 0.2
    end
    -- Clip the native editor separately so long text cannot cover the pen or panel.
    -- Keep the actual Text value and native cursor/selection behavior intact.
    local viewport = field:FindFirstChild("SyllinseTextViewport")
    if not viewport then
        viewport = Instance.new("Frame")
        viewport.Name = "SyllinseTextViewport"
        viewport.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        viewport.BorderSizePixel = 0
        viewport.Active = false
        viewport.Parent = field
    end
    viewport.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    viewport.ClipsDescendants = true
    viewport.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 0)
    viewport.Size = UDim2.new(1, icon and -43 or -18, 1, 0)
    viewport.ZIndex = box.ZIndex
    box.Parent = viewport
    box.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    box.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    box.TextScaled = false
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    box.FontFace, box.TextSize = FONT_MEDIUM, SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_TEXT_SIZE
    box.TextColor3, box.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.PanelValue, Theme.PanelSecondary
    box.TextTransparency = 0
    box.TextWrapped, box.TextTruncate = false, Enum.TextTruncate.None
    box.TextXAlignment, box.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    box:SetAttribute("SyllinseFieldText", true)
    nativeTextBaseSizes[box] = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_TEXT_SIZE
    local title = row:FindFirstChild("Title")
    if title then
        title.FontFace, title.TextSize = FONT_MEDIUM, 14
        title.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelText
        title:SetAttribute("SyllinseFieldText", true)
        nativeTextBaseSizes[title] = 14
    end
    if not field:GetAttribute("SyllinseFieldHoverBound") then
        field:SetAttribute("SyllinseFieldHoverBound", true)
        field.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if isPopupInteractionBlocked(field) then return end
            field:SetAttribute("SyllinseFieldHovered", true)
            tween(field, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover })
        end)
        field.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            field:SetAttribute("SyllinseFieldHovered", false)
            tween(field, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root })
        end)
    end
end
SyllinseRuntime.applyFieldStyles = function()
    for i = #SyllinseRuntime.textboxRows, 1, -1 do
        local row = SyllinseRuntime.textboxRows[i]
        if row.Parent then SyllinseRuntime.styleTextboxRow(row) else table.remove(SyllinseRuntime.textboxRows, i) end
    end
    for _, dropdown in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.reptillianDropdowns) do SyllinseRuntime.styleDropdownField(dropdown) end
end
do
    local nativeInput = Lumen.Elements.Input
    Lumen.Elements.Input = function(section, options)
        local before = {}
        for _, child in ipairs(section.Content:GetChildren()) do before[child] = true end
        local input = nativeInput(section, options)
        for _, child in ipairs(section.Content:GetChildren()) do
            if not before[child] and child.Name == "TextBox" and child:FindFirstChild("InputFrame") then
                table.insert(SyllinseRuntime.textboxRows, child)
                SyllinseRuntime.styleTextboxRow(child)
                break
            end
        end
        return input
    end
end

-- Mount the Reptillian renderer into the existing Lumen section, and bridge flags/search/config APIs.
SyllinseRuntime.bindDropdownPopup = function(dropdown)
    local items = dropdown.Items
    local anchor = items.RealDropdown.Instance
    local field = items.RealDropdownOutline.Instance
    local popup = items.OptionHolder.Instance
    local originalOpen, originalTween = dropdown.SetOpen, items.OptionHolder.Tween
    local openFrames = ReptillianLibrary.OpenFrames
    local closing, destroyed, syncing
    local connections, raised, heldScrollers = {}, {}, {}
    local scrollLocks = SyllinseRuntime.dropdownScrollLocks
    if not scrollLocks then
        scrollLocks = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        SyllinseRuntime.dropdownScrollLocks = scrollLocks
    end
    local layers = SyllinseRuntime.dropdownPopupLayers
    if not layers then
        layers = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        SyllinseRuntime.dropdownPopupLayers = layers
    end
    local function connect(signal, callback)
        table.insert(connections, signal:Connect(callback))
    end
    local function releaseScroll()
        for _, frame in ipairs(heldScrollers) do
            local lock = scrollLocks[frame]
            if lock then
                lock.Count -= 1
                if lock.Count == 0 then
                    if frame.Parent then frame.ScrollingEnabled = lock.Enabled end
                    scrollLocks[frame] = nil
                end
            end
        end
        table.clear(heldScrollers)
    end
    local function captureScroll()
        if destroyed or closing or not dropdown.IsOpen or not popup.Visible or #heldScrollers > 0 then return end
        if UserInputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        -- Stop native wheel chaining at the list's ends without changing its own scrolling.
        local parent = field.Parent
        while parent and parent ~= root do
            if parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") and (parent.ScrollingEnabled or scrollLocks[parent]) then
                local lock = scrollLocks[parent]
                if not lock then
                    lock = { Count = 0, Enabled = parent.ScrollingEnabled }
                    scrollLocks[parent] = lock
                end
                lock.Count += 1
                table.insert(heldScrollers, parent)
                parent.ScrollingEnabled = false
                parent:ResetScrollVelocity()
            end
            parent = parent.Parent
        end
    end
    local function releaseLayers()
        for _, frame in ipairs(raised) do
            local layer = layers[frame]
            if layer then
                layer.Count -= 1
                if layer.Count == 0 then
                    if frame.Parent then
                        frame.ClipsDescendants, frame.ZIndex = layer.Clip, layer.ZIndex
                    end
                    layers[frame] = nil
                end
            end
        end
        table.clear(raised)
    end
    local function raiseLayers()
        if #raised > 0 then return end
        local parent = field
        while parent and parent ~= root do
            if parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") and (parent.ScrollingEnabled or scrollLocks[parent]) then
                -- This is the stationary page boundary: never disable its clipping.
                parent.ClipsDescendants = true
                break
            end
            if parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
                local layer = layers[parent]
                if not layer then
                    layer = { Count = 0, Clip = parent.ClipsDescendants, ZIndex = parent.ZIndex }
                    layers[parent] = layer
                end
                layer.Count += 1
                table.insert(raised, parent)
                parent.ClipsDescendants = false
                parent.ZIndex = math.max(parent.ZIndex, 20)
            end
            parent = parent.Parent
        end
    end
    local closePopup
    local function anchorShown()
        if not gui.Enabled or not field.Parent then return false end
        local parent = field
        while parent and parent ~= gui do
            if parent:IsA("GuiObject") and not parent.Visible then return false end
            parent = parent.Parent
        end
        return true
    end
    local function fittedHeight()
        local style = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE
        local count = 0
        for _, option in pairs(dropdown.Options) do
            if option.SearchVisible ~= false then count += 1 end
        end
        local headerHeight = style.SearchTop + style.SearchHeight + style.SearchGap + style.SearchBottomPadding
        local rowsHeight = count * style.OptionHeight + math.max(0, count - 1) * style.OptionGap
        return math.clamp(headerHeight + rowsHeight, headerHeight, math.max(headerHeight, dropdown.MaxSize or style.MaxPopupHeight))
    end
    local function syncPopup()
        if syncing or destroyed then return end
        if not dropdown.IsOpen then
            releaseScroll()
            if not popup.Visible or popup.Parent ~= field then releaseLayers() end
            return
        end
        if closing then popup.Visible = false; return end
        if not anchorShown() then closePopup(); return end
        syncing = true
        raiseLayers()
        -- Same parent coordinates as the closed field: no screen offsets, cached
        -- positions, scroll compensation or independent position animation.
        popup.Parent = field
        popup.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        popup.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 6)
        popup.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, fittedHeight())
        popup.ClipsDescendants = true
        syncing = false
    end
    closePopup = function()
        if destroyed or closing then return end
        closing = true
        releaseScroll()
        popup.Visible = false
        dropdown:SetOpen(false)
        if dropdown.IsOpen then
            -- A menu/page can hide during the renderer's opening fade.
            task.delay(ReptillianLibrary.Animation.Time + 0.02, function()
                closing = false
                if not destroyed and dropdown.IsOpen then closePopup() end
            end)
        else
            closing = false
            releaseLayers()
        end
    end
    items.OptionHolder.Tween = function(self, properties, ...)
        local adjusted = table.clone(properties)
        adjusted.Position = nil
        if properties.Size then
            adjusted.Size = nil
        end
        local result
        if next(adjusted) then result = originalTween(self, adjusted, ...) end
        if properties.Position or properties.Size then syncPopup() end
        return result
    end
    dropdown.SetOpen = function(self, state)
        local result = originalOpen(self, state)
        syncPopup()
        return result
    end
    connect(popup.MouseEnter, captureScroll)
    connect(popup.MouseLeave, releaseScroll)
    connect(popup.InputChanged, function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then captureScroll() end
    end)
    connect(UserInputService.LastInputTypeChanged, function(kind)
        if kind == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then releaseScroll() end
    end)
    connect(popup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"), syncPopup)
    connect(popup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"), syncPopup)
    connect(popup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"), function()
        if syncing then return end
        syncPopup()
    end)
    connect(popup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), syncPopup)
    connect(gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"), syncPopup)
    local parent = field.Parent
    while parent and parent ~= gui do
        if parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
            connect(parent:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), syncPopup)
        end
        parent = parent.Parent
    end
    anchor.Destroying:Connect(function()
        destroyed = true
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        releaseScroll()
        releaseLayers()
        dropdown.IsOpen = false
        -- Exit() clears the library's internal upvalue before deferred Destroying
        -- callbacks run. Remove our entry without calling back into that library.
        if type(openFrames) == "table" then openFrames[dropdown] = nil end
        popup.Visible = false
        popup:Destroy()
    end)
end


SyllinseRuntime.addReptillianDropdown = function(section, options)
    options = options or {}
    assert(section and section.Content and typeof(section.Content) == "Instance", "Missing Lumen section content")
    local choices = {}
    for _, value in ipairs(options.Options or {}) do
        if type(value) == "string" or type(value) == "number" then table.insert(choices, tostring(value)) end
    end
    local multi, flag = options.Multi == true, options.Flag
    local callback = type(options.Callback) == "function" and options.Callback or function() end
    local default = options.Value
    if multi then default = type(default) == "table" and table.clone(default) or {} else default = tostring(default or "") end

    local dropdown = ReptillianLibrary:Dropdown({
        Name = tostring(options.Name or "Dropdown"), Items = choices, Default = default,
        Multi = multi, Flag = flag, MaxSize = SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.MaxPopupHeight,
        Parent = { Instance = section.Content }, Callback = callback,
    })
    local items = dropdown.Items
    local row = items.Dropdown.Instance
    row.Name = "ReptillianDropdownFrame"
    for _, pair in ipairs({ {"Text", "Title"}, {"RealDropdownOutline", "RealDropdownOutline"},
        {"RealDropdown", "RealDropdown"}, {"Value", "Value"}, {"Icon", "Icon"},
        {"OptionHolder", "ReptillianDropdownPopup"}, {"Search", "Search"}, {"Holder", "Holder"} }) do
        if items[pair[1]] and items[pair[1]].Instance then items[pair[1]].Instance.Name = pair[2] end
    end
    items.Text.Instance.FontFace, items.Text.Instance.TextSize = FONT_MEDIUM, 14
    items.Text.Instance.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelText
    items.Value.Instance.FontFace, items.Value.Instance.TextSize = FONT_MEDIUM, SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_CLOSED_TEXT_SIZE
    items.Value.Instance.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelValue

    local nativeSet, nativeRefresh = dropdown.Set, dropdown.Refresh
    local setDropdown
    local function syncFlag(value)
        if type(flag) ~= "string" or flag == "" then return end
        Lumen.Flags[flag] = Lumen.Flags[flag] or {}
        Lumen.Flags[flag].Value = type(value) == "table" and table.clone(value) or value
        Lumen.Flags[flag].Set = setDropdown
    end
    setDropdown = function(selfOrValue, maybeValue)
        local value = selfOrValue == dropdown and maybeValue or selfOrValue
        if multi then
            local selected = {}
            for _, item in ipairs(type(value) == "table" and value or {}) do
                item = tostring(item)
                if dropdown.Options[item] and not table.find(selected, item) then table.insert(selected, item) end
            end
            for _, option in pairs(dropdown.Options) do
                if option.IsSelected then option.IsSelected = false; option:ToggleState("Inactive") end
            end
            return nativeSet(dropdown, selected)
        end
        value = tostring(value or "")
        if value == "" or not dropdown.Options[value] then
            dropdown.Value, items.Value.Instance.Text = "", "..."
            if type(flag) == "string" and flag ~= "" then ReptillianLibrary.Flags[flag] = "" end
            syncFlag("")
            callback("")
            return true
        end
        return nativeSet(dropdown, value)
    end
    dropdown.Set = setDropdown
    dropdown.Callback = function(value) syncFlag(value); callback(value) end
    dropdown.UpdateOptions = function(selfOrOptions, maybeOptions)
        local nextOptions = selfOrOptions == dropdown and maybeOptions or selfOrOptions
        local clean = {}
        for _, value in ipairs(type(nextOptions) == "table" and nextOptions or {}) do
            if type(value) == "string" or type(value) == "number" then table.insert(clean, tostring(value)) end
        end
        local previous = type(dropdown.Value) == "table" and table.clone(dropdown.Value) or dropdown.Value
        nativeRefresh(dropdown, clean)
        if multi then
            local kept = {}
            for _, value in ipairs(type(previous) == "table" and previous or {}) do
                if dropdown.Options[tostring(value)] then table.insert(kept, tostring(value)) end
            end
            setDropdown(kept)
        elseif type(previous) == "string" and dropdown.Options[previous] then
            setDropdown(previous)
        else
            setDropdown(clean[1] or "")
        end
    end
    if multi then syncFlag(dropdown.Value)
    elseif default == "" or not dropdown.Options[default] then setDropdown("")
    else syncFlag(dropdown.Value) end

    table.insert(Lumen.Searchable, { Frame = row, Text = tostring(options.Name or "Dropdown"), Section = section })
    local function closeWhenHidden()
        if dropdown.IsOpen then dropdown:SetOpen(false) end
    end
    row:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function() if not row.Visible then closeWhenHidden() end end)
    if section.Frame then section.Frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function() if not section.Frame.Visible then closeWhenHidden() end end) end
    if section.PageFrame then section.PageFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function() if not section.PageFrame.Visible then closeWhenHidden() end end) end
    table.insert(SyllinseRuntime.reptillianDropdowns, dropdown)
    SyllinseRuntime.styleDropdownField(dropdown)
    SyllinseRuntime.bindDropdownPopup(dropdown)
    local nativeOpen = dropdown.SetOpen
    dropdown.SetOpen = function(self, state)
        SyllinseRuntime.styleDropdownField(dropdown)
        return nativeOpen(self, state)
    end
    return dropdown
end

-- Capture Lumen GUI visibility only when a settings gear is pressed. This avoids
-- guessing popup names (Lumen builds have used several different names).
local function snapshotLumenPopupVisibility()
    local snapshot = {}
    local layers = {}
    if Lumen._Instance then table.insert(layers, Lumen._Instance) end
    if Lumen._Overlay then table.insert(layers, Lumen._Overlay) end
    if root then table.insert(layers, root) end
    for _, layer in ipairs(layers) do
        local layerState = {}
        for _, object in ipairs(layer:GetDescendants()) do
            if object:IsA("GuiObject") then
                layerState[object] = object.Visible
            end
        end
        snapshot[layer] = layerState
    end
    return snapshot
end

local function watchLumenPopupOpenedBySettingsIcon(icon, before)
    if not before then return end
    task.delay(0.12, function()
        if SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon ~= icon then return end

        local popupRoot, popupLayer, largestArea
        local layers = {}
        if Lumen._Instance then table.insert(layers, Lumen._Instance) end
        if Lumen._Overlay then table.insert(layers, Lumen._Overlay) end
        if root then table.insert(layers, root) end
        for _, layer in ipairs(layers) do
            local previous = before[layer]
            if previous then
                for _, object in ipairs(layer:GetDescendants()) do
                    if object:IsA("GuiObject") and object.Visible and previous[object] ~= true then
                        -- Promote the changed object to the highest newly-visible
                        -- ancestor, stopping below any ancestor that was already visible.
                        local candidate = object
                        local parent = object.Parent
                        while parent and parent ~= layer and parent:IsA("GuiObject") do
                            if previous[parent] == true then break end
                            if parent.Visible then candidate = parent end
                            parent = parent.Parent
                        end

                        local size = candidate.AbsoluteSize
                        local area = size.X * size.Y
                        -- Ignore tiny hover tooltips; a real picker/keybind panel is larger.
                        if area >= 3200 and (not largestArea or area > largestArea) then
                            popupRoot, popupLayer, largestArea = candidate, layer, area
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if not popupRoot then return end

        -- Watch the detected popup and its ancestors. Lumen may hide either one
        -- when the user closes the popup, so either transition resets the gear.
        local watched = {}
        local object = popupRoot
        while object and object ~= popupLayer do
            if object:IsA("GuiObject") and not watched[object] then
                watched[object] = true
                local watchedObject = object
                watchedObject:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
                    if not watchedObject.Visible and SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon == icon then
                        SyllinseRuntime.clearActiveSettingsIconSelection()
                    end
                end)
                watchedObject.Destroying:Connect(function()
                    if SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon == icon then
                        SyllinseRuntime.clearActiveSettingsIconSelection()
                    end
                end)
            end
            object = object.Parent
        end
    end)
end

-- 0% makes the launcher visuals fully transparent; its hit area remains active so
-- the user can still tap/drag the open-close button while recording on mobile.
-- 100% restores the original appearance. Use a table method to avoid another
-- top-level local register in this already-large script.
SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.ApplyLauncherOpacity = function(value)
    local opacity = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 100, 0, 100) / 100
    SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.LauncherOpacity = math.floor(opacity * 100 + 0.5)
    launcher:SetAttribute("LauncherOpacity", SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.LauncherOpacity)

    launcher.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - opacity
    launcherStroke.Transparency = 1 - (0.92 * opacity)
    launcherLogo.ImageTransparency = 1 - opacity
    launcherGlow.ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.03 * opacity)
    launcherGlowCyan.ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.09 * opacity)
    launcherGlowViolet.ImageTransparency = 1 - (0.07 * opacity)
    if SyllinseRuntime.syncLauncherMediaOpacity then SyllinseRuntime.syncLauncherMediaOpacity() end
end
SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.ApplyLauncherOpacity(100)

-- Magik Society-style toast notifications, rebuilt with the current Syllinse palette.
-- The layout keeps the source UI's soft shadow, optional icon,
-- close button, slide animation and duration progress bar without its purple accent.
SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost = nil
SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout = nil
SyllinseRuntime.notificationPosition = "Top Right"
SyllinseRuntime.notificationOrder = 0
SyllinseRuntime.activeNotificationDismissals = {}
SyllinseRuntime.MAX_SYLLINSE_NOTIFICATIONS = 4
SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_SHADOW_ASSET = "rbxassetid://6014261993"
SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_GLOW_ASSET = "rbxassetid://8992230677"
SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_TITLE_COLORS = {
    Success = Theme.Text,
    Info = Theme.PanelTitle,
    Warning = Theme.PanelText,
    Error = Theme.Text,
}

SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationWidth = function()
    if showLauncherButton then
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local viewportWidth = camera and camera.ViewportSize.X or 390
        -- Keep mobile toasts compact and within the available viewport width.
        return math.floor(math.clamp(viewportWidth * 0.49, 178, 228))
    end
    return 248
end

SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationInset = function()
    return showLauncherButton and 10 or 16
end

SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationHost = function()
    if SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost and SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.Parent then
        return SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost
    end

    local parent = (Lumen and Lumen._Instance and Lumen._Instance:IsA("ScreenGui")) and Lumen._Instance or gui
    local inset = SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationInset()
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost = Instance.new("Frame")
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.Name = "SyllinseNotifications"
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.Position = UDim2.new(1, -inset, 0, inset)
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.Size = UDim2.new(0, SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationWidth(), 1, -(inset * 2))
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.BorderSizePixel = 0
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.ZIndex = 80
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.Parent = parent

    SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout.Name = "ToastLayout"
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout.Parent = SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost

    return SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost
end

SyllinseRuntime.setNotificationPosition = function(position)
    if type(position) ~= "string" then return false end
    if not table.find({ "Top Left", "Top Right", "Bottom Left", "Bottom Right" }, position) then
        return false
    end

    SyllinseRuntime.notificationPosition = position
    local host = SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationHost()
    local left = position == "Top Left" or position == "Bottom Left"
    local bottom = position == "Bottom Left" or position == "Bottom Right"
    local inset = SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationInset()
    host.Size = UDim2.new(0, SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationWidth(), 1, -(inset * 2))
    host.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(left and 0 or 1, bottom and 1 or 0)
    host.Position = UDim2.new(left and 0 or 1, left and inset or -inset, bottom and 1 or 0, bottom and -inset or inset)
    if SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout then
        SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout.HorizontalAlignment = left and Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
        SyllinseRuntime.notificationLayout.VerticalAlignment = bottom and Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom or Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    end
    return true
end

SyllinseRuntime.syncNotificationHostToViewport = function()
    if not SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost or not SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.Parent then return end
    local inset = SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationInset()
    local left = SyllinseRuntime.notificationPosition == "Top Left" or SyllinseRuntime.notificationPosition == "Bottom Left"
    local bottom = SyllinseRuntime.notificationPosition == "Bottom Left" or SyllinseRuntime.notificationPosition == "Bottom Right"
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.Size = UDim2.new(0, SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationWidth(), 1, -(inset * 2))
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(left and 0 or 1, bottom and 1 or 0)
    SyllinseRuntime.notificationHost.Position = UDim2.new(left and 0 or 1, left and inset or -inset, bottom and 1 or 0, bottom and -inset or inset)
end

SyllinseRuntime.makeToastGlow = function(parent)
    local bloom = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    bloom.Name = "ToastGlow"
    bloom.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    bloom.Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, -8)
    bloom.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(showLauncherButton and 145 or 180, showLauncherButton and 62 or 76)
    bloom.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bloom.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bloom.Image = SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_GLOW_ASSET
    bloom.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    bloom.ImageTransparency = 0.94
    bloom.ZIndex = 82
    bloom.Parent = parent

    local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.Text),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.PanelIcon),
    })
    gradient.Rotation = 90
    gradient.Parent = bloom
    return bloom
end

SyllinseRuntime.showNotification = function(options)
    options = type(options) == "table" and options or {}
    local duration = math.clamp(tonumber(options.Duration) or 3.5, 0.5, 20)
    local title = tostring(options.Title or "Notification")
    local bodyText = tostring(options.Content or options.Text or options.Message or "")
    local titleColor = SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_TITLE_COLORS[options.Type] or Theme.PanelTitle
    local extraWidth = math.clamp(tonumber(options.ExtraWidth) or 0, 0, 48)
    local extraPadding = math.clamp(tonumber(options.ExtraVerticalPadding) or 0, 0, 10)

    SyllinseRuntime.notificationOrder += 1
    local host = SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationHost()
    local fromLeft = SyllinseRuntime.notificationPosition == "Top Left" or SyllinseRuntime.notificationPosition == "Bottom Left"
    local slideDistance = (host.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and host.AbsoluteSize.X or SyllinseRuntime.getNotificationWidth()) + extraWidth + 18
    local enterX = fromLeft and -slideDistance or slideDistance

    local outer = Instance.new("Frame")
    outer.Name = "ToastOuter"
    outer.Size = UDim2.new(1, extraWidth, 0, 0)
    outer.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    outer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    outer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    outer.LayoutOrder = SyllinseRuntime.notificationOrder
    outer.ZIndex = 80
    outer.Parent = host

    local slide = Instance.new("Frame")
    slide.Name = "ToastSlide"
    slide.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(enterX, 0)
    slide.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    slide.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    slide.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    slide.BorderSizePixel = 0
    slide.ZIndex = 80
    slide.Parent = outer

    local shadow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    shadow.Name = "ToastShadow"
    shadow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-14, -14)
    shadow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 28, 1, 28)
    shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    shadow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shadow.Image = SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_SHADOW_ASSET
    shadow.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    shadow.ImageTransparency = 1
    shadow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
    shadow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(49, 49, 450, 450)
    shadow.ZIndex = 79
    shadow.Parent = slide

    local toast = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
    toast.Name = "ToastCard"
    toast.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    toast.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    toast.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
    toast.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
    toast.GroupTransparency = 1
    toast.ZIndex = 81
    toast.Parent = slide
    setManualCorner(toast, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Toast)
    -- Use the selected stroke color without animating the outline.
    addStroke(toast, Theme.Stroke, 0, 1)

    SyllinseRuntime.makeToastGlow(toast)

    local inner = Instance.new("Frame")
    inner.Name = "ToastContent"
    inner.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    inner.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    inner.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    inner.BorderSizePixel = 0
    inner.ZIndex = 84
    inner.Parent = toast

    local contentPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    local toastSidePadding = showLauncherButton and 10 or 11
    contentPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, toastSidePadding)
    contentPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, toastSidePadding)
    contentPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, (showLauncherButton and 8 or 9) + extraPadding)
    contentPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, (showLauncherButton and 13 or 15) + extraPadding)
    contentPadding.Parent = inner

    local titleOffset = 3
    if options.Icon then
        local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        icon.Name = "ToastIcon"
        icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 1)
        icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(showLauncherButton and 12 or 13, showLauncherButton and 12 or 13)
        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        icon.BorderSizePixel = 0
        icon.Image = tostring(options.Icon)
        icon.ImageColor3 = titleColor
        icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        icon.ZIndex = 85
        icon.Parent = inner
        titleOffset = 21
    end

    local toastTitleSize = showLauncherButton and 14 or 15
    local toastTitleHeight = showLauncherButton and 18 or 19
    local closeReservedWidth = showLauncherButton and 34 or 27
    local titleLabel = makeText(
        "ToastTitle", inner, title,
        UDim2.new(1, -closeReservedWidth - titleOffset, 0, toastTitleHeight),
        UDim2.fromOffset(titleOffset, 0), toastTitleSize, titleColor, FONT_MEDIUM
    )
    titleLabel.ZIndex = 85

    local closeButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    closeButton.Name = "ToastClose"
    closeButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    closeButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, 2, 0, showLauncherButton and -5 or -3)
    closeButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(showLauncherButton and 28 or 22, showLauncherButton and 28 or 22)
    closeButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    closeButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    closeButton.Text = "×"
    closeButton.TextColor3 = Theme.Muted
    closeButton.TextSize = showLauncherButton and 19 or 17
    closeButton.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
    closeButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    closeButton.ZIndex = 85
    closeButton.Parent = inner
    closeButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        tween(closeButton, 0.15, { TextColor3 = Theme.Text })
    end)
    closeButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        tween(closeButton, 0.2, { TextColor3 = Theme.Muted })
    end)

    local contentLabel
    if bodyText ~= "" then
        local toastBodySize = showLauncherButton and 12 or 13
        local toastBodyTop = showLauncherButton and 20 or 21
        contentLabel = makeText(
            "ToastMessage", inner, bodyText,
            UDim2.new(1, -12 - titleOffset, 0, 0),
            UDim2.fromOffset(titleOffset, toastBodyTop), toastBodySize, Theme.PanelText, FONT_REGULAR
        )
        contentLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        contentLabel.TextWrapped = true
        contentLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
        contentLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        contentLabel.ZIndex = 85
    end

    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Name = "ToastProgressTrack"
    track.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    track.Position = UDim2.new(0, toastSidePadding, 1, -5)
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(toastSidePadding * 2), 0, 2)
    track.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Stroke
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.ZIndex = 85
    track.Parent = toast
    setManualCorner(track, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Progress)

    local progress = Instance.new("Frame")
    progress.Name = "ToastProgress"
    progress.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    progress.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderHead
    progress.BorderSizePixel = 0
    progress.ZIndex = 86
    progress.Parent = track
    setManualCorner(progress, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Progress)

    local progressTween
    local progressIndeterminate = false
    local dismissed = false
    local dismiss
    dismiss = function()
        if dismissed then return end
        dismissed = true
        if progressTween then progressTween:Cancel(); progressTween = nil end
        for i, entry in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.activeNotificationDismissals) do
            if entry == dismiss then
                table.remove(SyllinseRuntime.activeNotificationDismissals, i)
                break
            end
        end
        if not outer.Parent then return end
        tween(slide, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(enterX, 0) })
        tween(toast, 0.2, { GroupTransparency = 1 })
        tween(shadow, 0.2, { ImageTransparency = 1 })
        task.delay(0.24, function()
            if outer.Parent then outer:Destroy() end
        end)
    end

    closeButton.Activated:Connect(dismiss)
    table.insert(SyllinseRuntime.activeNotificationDismissals, dismiss)
    while #SyllinseRuntime.activeNotificationDismissals > SyllinseRuntime.MAX_SYLLINSE_NOTIFICATIONS do
        local oldest = table.remove(SyllinseRuntime.activeNotificationDismissals, 1)
        if oldest then oldest() end
    end

    task.defer(function()
        if not dismissed and outer.Parent then
            tween(slide, 0.5, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) })
            tween(toast, 0.3, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
            tween(shadow, 0.4, { ImageTransparency = 0.62 })
            if not options.Persistent then
                TweenService:Create(progress, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                    Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
                }):Play()
            end
        end
    end)

    if not options.Persistent then task.delay(duration, dismiss) else track.Visible = false end
    return {
        Dismiss = dismiss;
        Update = function(message, fraction, indeterminate)
            if dismissed or not outer.Parent then return end
            if contentLabel and contentLabel.Text ~= tostring(message) then contentLabel.Text = tostring(message) end
            track.Visible = indeterminate == true or fraction ~= nil
            if indeterminate then
                if not progressIndeterminate then
                    if progressTween then progressTween:Cancel() end
                    progressIndeterminate = true
                    track.ClipsDescendants = true
                    progress.Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)
                    progress.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.28, 1)
                    progressTween = TweenService:Create(progress, TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, false), {
                        Position = UDim2.fromScale(-0.28, 0),
                    })
                    progressTween:Play()
                end
                return
            end
            if progressIndeterminate then
                if progressTween then progressTween:Cancel(); progressTween = nil end
                progressIndeterminate = false
                progress.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
                if options.DrainFromFull then progress.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) end
            end
            if fraction then
                local size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(fraction, 0, 1), 1)
                if options.ProgressTweenTime then
                    if progressTween then progressTween:Cancel() end
                    progressTween = TweenService:Create(progress, TweenInfo.new(options.ProgressTweenTime, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Size = size })
                    progressTween:Play()
                else
                    progress.Size = size
                end
            end
        end;
    }
end

Lumen.Notify = SyllinseRuntime.showNotification
Lumen.NotifyToggles = false

if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
    Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(SyllinseRuntime.syncNotificationHostToViewport)
end
Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(SyllinseRuntime.syncNotificationHostToViewport)
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.syncNotificationHostToViewport()
end)

SyllinseRuntime.notify = function(title, message, kind, duration)
    if SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.Notifications == false then return end
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        SyllinseRuntime.showNotification({
            Title = tostring(title or "Syllinse"),
            Content = tostring(message or ""),
            Type = kind or "Info",
            Duration = duration or 2.5,
        })
    end)
    if not ok then warn("[Syllinse] Notification error: " .. tostring(err)) end
end

-- Per-feature notification opt-ins. The Settings-page Notifications switch is the
-- global master; these individual bells decide which feature toggles may toast.
SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings = {}
SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.FeatureNotifications = SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings
-- Only controls that also expose a keybind/settings gear get an inline bell.
-- Visual-only toggles and settings without a keybind control intentionally have no bell.
SyllinseRuntime.FEATURE_NOTIFICATION_FLAGS = {
    AimbotEnabled = true,
    SilentAim = true,
    TriggerEnabled = true,
    AAEnabled = true,
    Fly = true,
}

-- Lumen's compact label rows are 14 px tall by default. When a touch control grows,
-- expand its owning row as well so larger controls never collide with the next row.
Lumen._SyllinseExpandMobileControlRow = function(control, targetHeight)
    if not (touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled) or not control then return end
    local ancestor = control.Parent
    while ancestor and ancestor ~= root do
        if ancestor.Name == "LabelFrame" then
            local size = ancestor.Size
            if size.Y.Scale == 0 and size.Y.Offset < targetHeight then
                ancestor.Size = UDim2.new(size.X.Scale, size.X.Offset, 0, targetHeight)
            end
            ancestor.ClipsDescendants = false
            -- Keep both halves centered in the taller row rather than pinning icons to the top.
            local leftContent = ancestor:FindFirstChild("LeftContent")
            if leftContent then
                leftContent.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
                leftContent.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
            end
            local rightContent = ancestor:FindFirstChild("RightContent")
            if rightContent then
                rightContent.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
                rightContent.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
            end
            return ancestor
        end
        ancestor = ancestor.Parent
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.attachFeatureNotificationBell = function(label, toggleControl, featureFlag)
    if not label or not label.RightContent or not toggleControl or not featureFlag then return end
    local rightContent = label.RightContent
    if not rightContent.Parent then return end

    -- The bell belongs only beside a real keybind/settings gear. Some toggles do not
    -- have a keybind control, and must not get an orphan notification icon.
    local settingsIcon = rightContent:FindFirstChild("Settings")
    if not settingsIcon or not settingsIcon:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
    if (touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled) then
        -- Keep a generous touch row for the native Lumen keybind/settings icon.
        Lumen._SyllinseExpandMobileControlRow(settingsIcon, 40)
    end

    local existingBell = rightContent:FindFirstChild("FeatureNotificationBell_" .. featureFlag)
    if existingBell then
        if (touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled) then
            Lumen._SyllinseExpandMobileControlRow(existingBell, 40)
        end
        return existingBell
    end

    if SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == nil then
        SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] = false
    end
    toggleControl.NotifyEnabled = SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true

    local bell = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    bell.Name = "FeatureNotificationBell_" .. featureFlag
    -- Use the original notification artwork, enlarged directly with the ImageButton.
    -- The responsive styling pass sets the final desktop/mobile dimensions.
    local bellTouchFirst = touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
    local bellSize = bellTouchFirst and SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.NotificationBell.TouchSize
        or SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.NotificationBell.DesktopSize
    bell.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(bellSize, bellSize)
    bell.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bell.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover
    bell.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bell.AutoButtonColor = false
    bell.Selectable = false
    bell.Image = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.NotificationBell.Image
    bell.ImageColor3 = SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] and Theme.Text or Theme.Muted
    bell.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    bell.LayoutOrder = 1
    bell.ZIndex = 8
    bell.Parent = rightContent
    Lumen._SyllinseExpandMobileControlRow(bell, 40)

    -- Keep the bell immediately LEFT of the keybind/settings gear. Other inline
    -- controls (such as a color picker) stay after the bell/gear pair.
    for _, sibling in ipairs(rightContent:GetChildren()) do
        if sibling:IsA("GuiObject") and sibling ~= bell then
            if sibling == settingsIcon then
                sibling.LayoutOrder = 2
            else
                sibling.LayoutOrder = 3 + math.max(0, tonumber(sibling.LayoutOrder) or 0)
            end
        end
    end
    bell.LayoutOrder = 1

    bell:SetAttribute("FeatureFlag", featureFlag)
    bell:SetAttribute("Tooltip", "Toggle notifications for " .. tostring(label.Text or featureFlag))
    addCorner(bell, (touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled) and 7 or 4)

    local activeDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    activeDot.Name = "NotificationEnabledDot"
    activeDot.Size = (touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled) and UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5) or UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4)
    activeDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    activeDot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 0)
    activeDot.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
    activeDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    activeDot.Visible = SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true
    activeDot.ZIndex = 9
    activeDot.Parent = bell
    setManualCorner(activeDot, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.StatusDot)

    local function refreshBell()
        local enabled = SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true
        toggleControl.NotifyEnabled = enabled
        SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.FeatureNotifications[featureFlag] = enabled
        bell.ImageColor3 = enabled and Theme.Text or Theme.Muted
        activeDot.Visible = enabled
    end

    bell.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if isPopupInteractionBlocked(bell) then return end
        -- Hover highlights the bell artwork without drawing a button box.
        bell.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bell.ImageColor3 = Theme.Text
    end)
    bell.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        bell.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bell.ImageColor3 = SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] and Theme.Text or Theme.Muted
    end)
    bell.Activated:Connect(function()
        SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] = not (SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true)
        refreshBell()
        -- Deliberately do not toast here: clicking the bell changes preference only.
    end)

    refreshBell()
    return bell
end

SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_POSITIONS = { "Top Left", "Top Right", "Bottom Left", "Bottom Right" }
SyllinseRuntime.applyNotificationPosition = function(position)
    if type(position) ~= "string" or not table.find(SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_POSITIONS, position) then
        return false
    end
    Lumen.NotifyPosition = position
    return SyllinseRuntime.setNotificationPosition(position)
end

SyllinseRuntime.setNotificationPosition(Lumen.NotifyPosition or "Top Right")

-- Mobile touch integrations adapted from MacLib while keeping Syllinse's existing visuals.
-- A vertical swipe through a scroll container should scroll, not accidentally toggle a row.
SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollGuards = {}
SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollDispatcherInstalled = false
SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputChangedConnection = nil
SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputEndedConnection = nil

SyllinseRuntime.setGuardedButtonInteractable = function(state, button, desiredState)
    if not button or not button.Parent or not button:IsA("GuiButton") then return end
    if desiredState then
        local original = state.OriginalInteractable[button]
        if original ~= nil then
            pcall(function() button.Interactable = original end)
        end
        state.OriginalInteractable[button] = nil
    else
        if state.OriginalInteractable[button] == nil then
            local ok, original = pcall(function() return button.Interactable end)
            if not ok then return end
            state.OriginalInteractable[button] = original
        end
        pcall(function() button.Interactable = false end)
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.setMobileScrollState = function(state, scrolling)
    if not state or state.Destroyed then return end
    state.Scrolling = scrolling == true
    pcall(function()
        state.Frame:SetAttribute("_mobileScrolling", state.Scrolling) -- MacLib-compatible marker
        state.Frame:SetAttribute("_syllinseMobileScrolling", state.Scrolling)
    end)
    if state.Scrolling then
        for _, descendant in ipairs(state.Frame:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("GuiButton") then
                SyllinseRuntime.setGuardedButtonInteractable(state, descendant, false)
            end
        end
    else
        for button, original in pairs(state.OriginalInteractable) do
            if button and button.Parent then
                pcall(function() button.Interactable = original end)
            end
        end
        table.clear(state.OriginalInteractable)
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.bindMobileScrollGuard = function(scrollFrame)
    if not touchInputEnabled or not scrollFrame or not scrollFrame:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then return end
    if scrollFrame:GetAttribute("_syllinseTouchGuardBound") then return end
    scrollFrame:SetAttribute("_syllinseTouchGuardBound", true)
    scrollFrame:SetAttribute("_mobileScrolling", false)
    scrollFrame:SetAttribute("_syllinseMobileScrolling", false)
    scrollFrame.Active = true

    local state = {
        Frame = scrollFrame,
        ActiveInput = nil,
        StartPosition = nil,
        Scrolling = false,
        OriginalInteractable = {},
        Destroyed = false,
    }
    table.insert(SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollGuards, state)

    scrollFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        state.ActiveInput = input
        state.StartPosition = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        if state.Scrolling then SyllinseRuntime.setMobileScrollState(state, false) end
    end)
    scrollFrame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch or not state.ActiveInput then return end
        local point = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        local delta = point - (state.StartPosition or point)
        if math.abs(delta.Y) > 8 and math.abs(delta.Y) > math.abs(delta.X) then
            SyllinseRuntime.setMobileScrollState(state, true)
        end
    end)
    scrollFrame.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        if state.Scrolling and descendant:IsA("GuiButton") then
            SyllinseRuntime.setGuardedButtonInteractable(state, descendant, false)
        end
    end)
    scrollFrame.Destroying:Connect(function()
        SyllinseRuntime.setMobileScrollState(state, false)
        state.Destroyed = true
        for index = #SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollGuards, 1, -1 do
            if SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollGuards[index] == state then
                table.remove(SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollGuards, index)
                break
            end
        end
        if #SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollGuards == 0 then
            if SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputChangedConnection then SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputChangedConnection:Disconnect() end
            if SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputEndedConnection then SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputEndedConnection:Disconnect() end
            SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputChangedConnection = nil
            SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputEndedConnection = nil
            SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollDispatcherInstalled = false
        end
    end)

    if not SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollDispatcherInstalled then
        SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollDispatcherInstalled = true
        SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputChangedConnection = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            local point = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
            for _, activeState in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollGuards) do
                if not activeState.Destroyed and activeState.ActiveInput == input then
                    local delta = point - (activeState.StartPosition or point)
                    if math.abs(delta.Y) > 8 and math.abs(delta.Y) > math.abs(delta.X) then
                        SyllinseRuntime.setMobileScrollState(activeState, true)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollInputEndedConnection = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            for _, activeState in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.mobileScrollGuards) do
                if activeState.ActiveInput
                    and (input == activeState.ActiveInput or activeState.ActiveInput.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
                    local endedInput = activeState.ActiveInput
                    task.delay(0.05, function()
                        if not activeState.Destroyed and activeState.ActiveInput == endedInput then
                            SyllinseRuntime.setMobileScrollState(activeState, false)
                            activeState.ActiveInput = nil
                            activeState.StartPosition = nil
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons = {}
SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtonsEnabled = false
SyllinseRuntime.actionCameraConnection = nil
SyllinseRuntime.actionCurrentCameraConnection = nil
SyllinseRuntime.clampMobileActionButtonsToViewport = function()
    task.defer(function()
        if not mobileActionGui or not mobileActionGui.Parent then return end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(390, 844)
        for _, entry in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons) do
            local button = entry.Button
            if button and button.Parent then
                local position = button.AbsolutePosition
                local size = button.AbsoluteSize
                local left = math.clamp(position.X, 4, math.max(4, viewport.X - size.X - 4))
                local top = math.clamp(position.Y, 4, math.max(4, viewport.Y - size.Y - 4))
                button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(left, top)
            end
        end
    end)
end

SyllinseRuntime.watchMobileActionCamera = function(camera)
    if SyllinseRuntime.actionCameraConnection then SyllinseRuntime.actionCameraConnection:Disconnect() end
    SyllinseRuntime.actionCameraConnection = nil
    if camera then
        SyllinseRuntime.actionCameraConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(SyllinseRuntime.clampMobileActionButtonsToViewport)
    end
end

if mobileActionGui then
    SyllinseRuntime.watchMobileActionCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
    SyllinseRuntime.actionCurrentCameraConnection = Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
        SyllinseRuntime.watchMobileActionCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
        SyllinseRuntime.clampMobileActionButtonsToViewport()
    end)
    mobileActionGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        for _, entry in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons) do
            if entry.InputChangedConnection then entry.InputChangedConnection:Disconnect() end
            if entry.InputEndedConnection then entry.InputEndedConnection:Disconnect() end
        end
        if SyllinseRuntime.actionCameraConnection then SyllinseRuntime.actionCameraConnection:Disconnect() end
        if SyllinseRuntime.actionCurrentCameraConnection then SyllinseRuntime.actionCurrentCameraConnection:Disconnect() end
        SyllinseRuntime.actionCameraConnection = nil
        SyllinseRuntime.actionCurrentCameraConnection = nil
    end)
end

SyllinseRuntime.refreshMobileActionButton = function(entry, state)
    if not entry then return end
    entry.Active = state == true
    local button = entry.Button
    if not button or not button.Parent then return end
    button.BackgroundColor3 = entry.Active and Theme.SurfaceHover or Theme.Surface
    button.BackgroundTransparency = entry.Active and 0.01 or 0.03
    button.TextColor3 = entry.Active and Theme.Text or Theme.PanelText
    entry.Stroke.Color = entry.Active and Theme.PanelTitle or Theme.Stroke
    entry.Stroke.Transparency = entry.Active and 0.12 or 0.08
end

SyllinseRuntime.syncMobileActionButtons = function(featureFlag, state)
    for _, entry in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons) do
        if entry.FeatureFlag == featureFlag then
            SyllinseRuntime.refreshMobileActionButton(entry, state)
        end
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.setMobileActionButtonsVisible = function(state)
    SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtonsEnabled = state == true
    SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.MobileActionButtons = SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtonsEnabled
    for _, entry in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons) do
        if entry.Button and entry.Button.Parent then
            entry.Button.Visible = SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtonsEnabled or entry.ManualVisible == true
        end
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.setMobileActionButtonVisibility = function(keybindFlag, state)
    for _, entry in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons) do
        if entry.KeybindFlag == keybindFlag then
            entry.ManualVisible = state == true
            if entry.Button and entry.Button.Parent then
                entry.Button.Visible = SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtonsEnabled or entry.ManualVisible
            end
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

-- Lumen's provided Toggle:Set implementation treats a false argument as a toggle
-- request. Normalize that edge case once per toggle so false defaults and config loads
-- remain accurate while retaining Lumen's native animation/callback/flag behavior.
SyllinseRuntime.addToggle = function(label, options)
    local opts = options or {}
    local requestedState = opts.State == true
    local featureFlag = type(opts.Flag) == "string" and opts.Flag or nil
    local hasFeatureNotifications = featureFlag ~= nil and SyllinseRuntime.FEATURE_NOTIFICATION_FLAGS[featureFlag] == true
    if hasFeatureNotifications then
