        if SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == nil then
            SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] = false
        end
        opts.NotifyEnabled = SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true
    else
        -- Do not let unrelated controls emit native toggle toasts.
        opts.NotifyEnabled = false
    end
    local control = label:Toggle(opts)

    -- Lumen returns a state table from Label:Toggle(); it does NOT set control.Frame.
    -- The native hierarchy is LabelFrame > LeftContent > Toggle. Attach the actual
    -- LabelFrame to the wrapper so the styling code can reliably find the real button.
    local toggleLabelFrame = label and label.LeftContent and label.LeftContent.Parent
    if control and toggleLabelFrame and toggleLabelFrame:IsA("GuiObject") then
        control.Frame = toggleLabelFrame
        control.Button = label.LeftContent:FindFirstChild("Toggle")
    end

    if hasFeatureNotifications then
        task.defer(function()
            SyllinseRuntime.attachFeatureNotificationBell(label, control, featureFlag)
        end)
    end
    local originalSet = control.Set
    local currentState = control.State == true
    local toggleCaption = label and label.LeftContent and label.LeftContent:FindFirstChild("Label")
    local toggleCaptionHovered = false

    local function refreshSyllinseToggleCaption(isOn)
        if not toggleCaption or not toggleCaption.Parent or not toggleCaption:IsA("TextButton") then return end
        local inactiveColor = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.LabelTextColor or Color3.fromRGB(190, 188, 188)
        local activeColor = Theme.Text
        if (SyllinseRuntime.activeAppearancePreset or "Original") == "Original" then
            inactiveColor, activeColor = Theme.PanelSecondary, Theme.PanelTitle
        end
        local hoverColor = Theme.PanelTitle or Theme.Text
        toggleCaption.TextColor3 = isOn and activeColor or (toggleCaptionHovered and hoverColor or inactiveColor)
        toggleCaption.TextTransparency = 0
    end

    if toggleCaption and toggleCaption:IsA("TextButton") then
        toggleCaption.AutoButtonColor = false
        toggleCaption.Selectable = false
        toggleCaption:SetAttribute("SyllinseClickableToggleCaption", true)
        -- Clicking the caption toggles the same control as clicking the switch track.
        -- Activated supports mouse and touch; the existing mobile scroll guard disables
        -- GuiButtons while a panel is actively being scrolled.
        toggleCaption.Activated:Connect(function()
            if control and type(control.Set) == "function" then
                control.Set()
            end
        end)
        toggleCaption.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if isPopupInteractionBlocked(toggleCaption) then return end
            toggleCaptionHovered = true
            refreshSyllinseToggleCaption(currentState)
        end)
        toggleCaption.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            toggleCaptionHovered = false
            refreshSyllinseToggleCaption(currentState)
        end)
    end

    -- Keep Lumen's native toggle behavior and geometry, but use a visible charcoal
    -- off track. Lumen's internal click handler tweens the track back to near-black,
    -- so the style is also re-applied after native activation/state animations.
    local function applySyllinseToggleStyle(isOn, preserveNativeAnimation, snapIndicator)
        local frame = control and control.Frame
        if not frame or not frame.Parent then return end
        local track
        for _, object in ipairs(frame:GetDescendants()) do
            if object.Name == "Toggle" and object:IsA("GuiButton") then
                track = object
                break
            end
        end
        if not track then
            if control and control.Frame and control.Frame:GetAttribute("SyllinseToggleLookupWarned") ~= true then
                control.Frame:SetAttribute("SyllinseToggleLookupWarned", true)
                warn("[Syllinse] Couldn't find Lumen's Toggle button under LabelFrame. The Lumen hierarchy may have changed.")
            end
            return
        end

        -- These are the live manual dimensions from SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.
        -- Remove any Lumen aspect/size constraint that would silently clamp the
        -- requested dimensions back to the library's small default switch size.
        local baseSwitchWidth = math.max(12, tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.Width) or 38)
        local baseSwitchHeight = math.max(12, tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.Height) or 19)
        track.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
        track.SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeXY
        for _, constraint in ipairs(track:GetChildren()) do
            if constraint:IsA("UIAspectRatioConstraint") then
                constraint:Destroy()
            elseif constraint:IsA("UISizeConstraint") then
                constraint.MinSize = Vector2.new(baseSwitchWidth, baseSwitchHeight)
                constraint.MaxSize = Vector2.new(baseSwitchWidth, baseSwitchHeight)
            end
        end
        if track.Parent and track.Parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
            track.Parent.ClipsDescendants = false
        end
        track:SetAttribute("SyllinseBaseSwitchWidth", baseSwitchWidth)
        track:SetAttribute("SyllinseBaseSwitchHeight", baseSwitchHeight)
        track.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(baseSwitchWidth, baseSwitchHeight)

        -- Lumen's LabelFrame uses a horizontal UIListLayout with a 10px default gap.
        -- Reduce that gap so the feature text sits naturally beside the compact switch.
        local leftContent = track.Parent
        if leftContent and leftContent:IsA("GuiObject") then
            leftContent.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
            local contentLayout = leftContent:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
            if contentLayout then
                contentLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, math.max(0, tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.LabelGap) or 5))
                contentLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
            end
        end
        local toggleRow = leftContent and leftContent.Parent
        if toggleRow and toggleRow.Name == "LabelFrame" then
            leftContent.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            leftContent.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
            local rightContent = toggleRow:FindFirstChild("RightContent")
            if rightContent and rightContent:IsA("GuiObject") then
                rightContent.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
                rightContent.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
            end
        end

        -- Keep feature captions optically smaller than section headings.
        -- Apply this to the actual text objects beside the Toggle, not the whole row.
        local labelTextSize = math.max(10, tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.LabelTextSize)
            or (baseSwitchHeight - 2))
        local labelTextColor = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.LabelTextColor
            or Color3.fromRGB(190, 188, 188)
        local activeLabelTextColor = Theme.Text
        if (SyllinseRuntime.activeAppearancePreset or "Original") == "Original" then
            labelTextColor, activeLabelTextColor = Theme.PanelSecondary, Theme.PanelTitle
        end
        if leftContent then
            for _, textObject in ipairs(leftContent:GetDescendants()) do
                local isText = textObject:IsA("TextLabel") or textObject:IsA("TextButton")
                    or textObject:IsA("TextBox")
                local belongsToSwitch = textObject == track or textObject:IsDescendantOf(track)
                if isText and not belongsToSwitch then
                    textObject:SetAttribute("SyllinseToggleLabel", true)
                    textObject.FontFace = getSwitchLabelFontFace()
                    textObject.TextSize = labelTextSize
                    if textObject == toggleCaption then
                        textObject.TextColor3 = isOn and activeLabelTextColor
                            or (toggleCaptionHovered and Theme.PanelTitle or labelTextColor)
                    else
                        textObject.TextColor3 = labelTextColor
                    end
                    textObject.TextTransparency = 0
                    textObject.TextScaled = false
                    textObject.AutoLocalize = false
                end
            end
        end

        local switchScale = track:FindFirstChild("SyllinseSwitchScaleCompensation")
        if not switchScale then
            switchScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            switchScale.Name = "SyllinseSwitchScaleCompensation"
            switchScale.Parent = track
        end
        switchScale.Scale = 1 / math.max(responsiveScaleValue, 0.25)

        -- Reserve a compact row for the visible switch. Touch devices receive a larger
        -- transparent interaction area through the mobile row-expansion helper.
        local switchRow = track.Parent
        while switchRow and switchRow ~= root do
            if switchRow.Name == "LabelFrame" then
                if switchRow:GetAttribute("SyllinseToggleBaseRowHeight") == nil then
                    switchRow:SetAttribute("SyllinseToggleBaseRowHeight", switchRow.Size.Y.Offset)
                end
                local baseHeight = switchRow:GetAttribute("SyllinseToggleBaseRowHeight") or switchRow.Size.Y.Offset
                local minimumPhysicalHeight = math.max(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.RowMinHeight, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.Height + SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.RowPadding)
                local targetHeight = math.max(
                    baseHeight, minimumPhysicalHeight / math.max(responsiveScaleValue, 0.25))
                if switchRow.Size.Y.Scale == 0 then
                    switchRow.Size = UDim2.new(switchRow.Size.X.Scale, switchRow.Size.X.Offset, 0, math.ceil(targetHeight))
                    switchRow.ClipsDescendants = false
                end
                break
            end
            switchRow = switchRow.Parent
        end

        local onTrackColor = Theme.ThemedControls and Theme.Surface:Lerp(Theme.Accent, 0.56)
            or SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.TrackOnColor or Color3.fromRGB(145, 143, 143)
        local offTrackColor = not Theme.ThemedControls and SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.TrackOffColor or Theme.ToggleOffTrack
        local onThumbColor = Theme.ThemedControls and Theme.Accent:Lerp(Theme.Text, 0.35)
            or SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbOnColor or Color3.fromRGB(226, 224, 224)
        local offThumbColor = not Theme.ThemedControls and SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbOffColor or Theme.ToggleOffThumb

        -- Keep the track itself as the OFF base. Lumen paints its ON state using
        -- the child named Overlay, so recolor that overlay too (not only the parent).
        track.BackgroundColor3 = offTrackColor
        track.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        track.AutoButtonColor = false
        track.ClipsDescendants = true

        local trackRadius = math.clamp(
            tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.TrackCornerRadius) or (baseSwitchHeight / 2),
            0,
            baseSwitchHeight / 2
        )
        setManualCorner(track, trackRadius)
        -- The visible ON fill is a separate Overlay with its own UICorner; match it
        -- to the track so changing TrackCornerRadius affects both states equally.
        local trackOverlay = track:FindFirstChild("Overlay")
        if trackOverlay and trackOverlay:IsA("GuiObject") then
            trackOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = onTrackColor
            -- During a state change, leave transparency to Lumen's native tween.
            -- Repainting it here would cut the fade short and make the switch feel abrupt.
            if not preserveNativeAnimation then
                trackOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = isOn and 0 or 1
            end
            setManualCorner(trackOverlay, trackRadius)
            local overlayGradient = trackOverlay:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
            if overlayGradient then
                -- Use a flat warm-grey fill. Disabling the old library gradient
                -- prevents the ON state from being darkened or tinted by a theme gradient.
                overlayGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(onTrackColor)
                overlayGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
                overlayGradient.Enabled = false
            end
        end

        -- Lumen calls the switch thumb "Indicator". Enlarge it on every device
        -- and keep the same inset/centering in both on and off states.
        do
            local indicator = track:FindFirstChild("Indicator", true)
            if indicator and indicator:IsA("GuiObject") then
                local thumbSize = math.max(8, tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbSize) or 13)
                local thumbInset = math.max(0, tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbInset) or 4)
                indicator.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                indicator.SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeXY
                for _, constraint in ipairs(indicator:GetChildren()) do
                    if constraint:IsA("UIAspectRatioConstraint") then
                        constraint:Destroy()
                    elseif constraint:IsA("UISizeConstraint") then
                        constraint.MinSize = Vector2.new(thumbSize, thumbSize)
                        constraint.MaxSize = Vector2.new(thumbSize, thumbSize)
                    end
                end
                indicator.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(thumbSize, thumbSize)
                -- State changes animate the thumb's color and movement together below.
                -- Initial styling can set the resting color and exact 3.5px inset directly.
                if not preserveNativeAnimation then
                    indicator.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and onThumbColor or offThumbColor
                end
                indicator.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                indicator.ClipsDescendants = true
                setManualCorner(
                    indicator,
                    math.clamp(tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbCornerRadius) or (thumbSize / 2), 0, thumbSize / 2)
                )
                if snapIndicator then
                    if isOn then
                        indicator.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
                        indicator.Position = UDim2.new(1, -thumbInset, 0.5, 0)
                    else
                        indicator.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
                        indicator.Position = UDim2.new(0, thumbInset, 0.5, 0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local trackStroke = track:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        if not trackStroke then
            trackStroke = addStroke(track, Theme.Stroke, 0.12, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.StrokeThickness)
        end
        -- ON is communicated by a lighter warm-grey fill only: never show an outline.
        -- Keep OFF with a very subtle charcoal edge for separation from the panel.
        for _, outline in ipairs(track:GetDescendants()) do
            if outline:IsA("UIStroke") then
                outline.Color = Theme.Stroke
                outline.Thickness = tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.StrokeThickness) or 1
                outline.Transparency = isOn and 1 or 0.12
            end
        end
        trackStroke.Color = Theme.Stroke
        trackStroke.Transparency = isOn and 1 or 0.12

        if not SyllinseRuntime.ManualSwitchStyleConfirmed then
            SyllinseRuntime.ManualSwitchStyleConfirmed = true
            print(string.format(
                "[Syllinse] Manual switch style active: %dx%d track, %dpx thumb.",
                baseSwitchWidth,
                baseSwitchHeight,
                tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbSize) or 13
            ))
        end


        for _, part in ipairs(track:GetDescendants()) do
            local partName = string.lower(part.Name or "")
            local isThumb = partName:find("circle", 1, true)
                or partName:find("knob", 1, true)
                or partName:find("handle", 1, true)
            if isThumb and part:IsA("GuiObject") then
                if part:IsA("ImageLabel") or part:IsA("ImageButton") then
                    part.ImageColor3 = isOn and onThumbColor or offThumbColor
                    part.ImageTransparency = 0
                else
                    part.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and onThumbColor or offThumbColor
                    part.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                end
                local thumbCorner = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
                local thumbRadius = math.clamp(
                    tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbCornerRadius) or 6.5,
                    0,
                    math.min(part.AbsoluteSize.X, part.AbsoluteSize.Y) / 2
                )
                if thumbCorner then
                    thumbCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, thumbRadius)
                elseif part:IsA("Frame") or part:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                    setManualCorner(part, thumbRadius)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    -- Re-apply after Lumen's own layout/theme passes too; those can recreate or
    -- restyle the native toggle after the first deferred call.
    SyllinseRuntime.toggleStyleRefreshers = SyllinseRuntime.toggleStyleRefreshers or {}
    table.insert(SyllinseRuntime.toggleStyleRefreshers, function()
        if not control or not control.Frame or not control.Frame.Parent then return end
        if type(control.State) == "boolean" then currentState = control.State end
        applySyllinseToggleStyle(currentState)
    end)
    if control.Frame and control.Frame:GetAttribute("SyllinseToggleRebuildWatcher") ~= true then
        control.Frame:SetAttribute("SyllinseToggleRebuildWatcher", true)
        control.Frame.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if descendant.Name == "Toggle" and descendant:IsA("GuiButton") then
                task.defer(function() applySyllinseToggleStyle(currentState) end)
                task.delay(0.12, function() applySyllinseToggleStyle(currentState) end)
            end
        end)
    end
    -- Snap only once to establish the initial resting position. After that, the
    -- switch is moved exclusively by the animated Set path below.
    task.defer(function() applySyllinseToggleStyle(currentState, false, true) end)
    task.delay(0.12, function() applySyllinseToggleStyle(currentState, false, false) end)
    task.delay(0.35, function() applySyllinseToggleStyle(currentState, false, false) end)

    local function animateSyllinseThumb(state)
        if not control or not control.Frame or not control.Frame.Parent then return end
        local track = control.Button
        if not track or not track.Parent then
            track = control.Frame:FindFirstChild("Toggle", true)
        end
        if not track then return end
        local indicator = track:FindFirstChild("Indicator", true)
        if not indicator or not indicator:IsA("GuiObject") then return end

        local thumbInset = math.max(0, tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbInset) or 3.5)
        local onThumbColor = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbOnColor or Color3.fromRGB(226, 224, 224)
        local offThumbColor = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Switch.ThumbOffColor or Theme.ToggleOffThumb
        local targetAnchor = state and Vector2.new(1, 0.5) or Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        local targetPosition = state
            and UDim2.new(1, -thumbInset, 0.5, 0)
            or UDim2.new(0, thumbInset, 0.5, 0)

        -- Lumen's native 0.1s animation is retained for the fill. This eased
        -- 0.19s thumb tween supersedes its position tween, avoiding the old snap.
        local motion = TweenService:Create(
            indicator,
            TweenInfo.new(0.19, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            {
                AnchorPoint = targetAnchor,
                Position = targetPosition,
                BackgroundColor3 = state and onThumbColor or offThumbColor,
            }
        )
        motion:Play()
    end

    control.Set = function(state, silent)
        if state == nil then state = not currentState end
        state = state == true
        if state == currentState then
            return currentState
        end
        currentState = state
        -- Make sure sizing/colors are correct before Lumen starts its own overlay fade.
        applySyllinseToggleStyle(state, true, false)
        originalSet(state, silent)
        animateSyllinseThumb(state)
        SyllinseRuntime.syncMobileActionButtons(featureFlag, currentState)
        if control.Flag and Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] then
            Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Value = currentState
            Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
        end
        if hasFeatureNotifications and not silent and SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true then
            SyllinseRuntime.notify(opts.Name or featureFlag, currentState and "Enabled" or "Disabled",
                currentState and "Success" or "Info", opts.NotificationDuration or 2.5)
        end
        return currentState
    end

    if control.Flag then
        Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] = Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] or {}
        Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
    end
    if currentState ~= requestedState then
        control.Set(requestedState, true)
    end
    return control
end

SyllinseRuntime.MOBILE_ACTION_LABELS = {
    AimbotKey = "AIM",
    SilentAimKey = "SILENT",
    SilentKey = "SILENT",
    TriggerKey = "TRIGGER",
    AAKey = "ANTI-AIM",
    FlyKey = "FLY",
}

SyllinseRuntime.createMobileActionButton = function(keybindFlag, featureFlag, callback, initialState)
    if not mobileActionGui or type(callback) ~= "function" then return nil end
    local index = #SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons + 1
    local label = SyllinseRuntime.MOBILE_ACTION_LABELS[keybindFlag] or tostring(keybindFlag or "ACTION")

    local viewportCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local viewportHeight = viewportCamera and viewportCamera.ViewportSize.Y or 844
    local buttonHeight = 34
    local stackTop = math.min(viewportHeight * 0.34, math.max(8, viewportHeight - (#SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons + 1) * 42 - 8))
    local labelWidths = { AIM = 64, SILENT = 78, TRIGGER = 84, ["ANTI-AIM"] = 92, FLY = 60 }
    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Name = "SyllinseMobileAction_" .. tostring(keybindFlag or index)
    button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, stackTop + (index - 1) * 42)
    button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(labelWidths[label] or 78, buttonHeight)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.03
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.FontFace = FONT_SEMIBOLD
    button.Text = label
    button.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelText
    button.TextSize = 12
    button.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    button.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    button.Visible = false
    button.Active = true
    button.Selectable = false
    button.ZIndex = 250
    button.Parent = mobileActionGui
    setManualCorner(button, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.KeybindButton)
    local stroke = addStroke(button, Theme.Stroke, 0.08, 1)

    local entry = {
        KeybindFlag = keybindFlag,
        FeatureFlag = featureFlag,
        Callback = callback,
        Button = button,
        Stroke = stroke,
        Active = initialState == true,
        ManualVisible = false,
        DragInput = nil,
    }
    table.insert(SyllinseRuntime.mobileActionButtons, entry)
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshMobileActionButton(entry, initialState)

    -- Same touch/mouse drag-and-release pattern as MacLib's mobile keybind API.
    -- A short tap toggles the linked feature; dragging only repositions the button.
    local gesture
    button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            return
        end
        gesture = {
            Input = input,
            StartPoint = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y),
            StartPosition = button.AbsolutePosition,
            Dragged = false,
        }
    end)

    -- Scope movement updates to this button, matching MacLib's per-keybind drag handler.
    entry.InputChangedConnection = button.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not gesture then return end
        local isTouchMove = gesture.Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        local isMouseMove = gesture.Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        if not isTouchMove and not isMouseMove then return end

        local point = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        local delta = point - gesture.StartPoint
        if delta.Magnitude > 8 then gesture.Dragged = true end
        if not gesture.Dragged then return end

        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(390, 844)
        local left = math.clamp(gesture.StartPosition.X + delta.X, 4, math.max(4, viewport.X - button.AbsoluteSize.X - 4))
        local top = math.clamp(gesture.StartPosition.Y + delta.Y, 4, math.max(4, viewport.Y - button.AbsoluteSize.Y - 4))
        button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(left, top)
    end)

    entry.InputEndedConnection = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if not gesture then return end
        local isTouchEnd = gesture.Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        local isMouseEnd = gesture.Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        if not isTouchEnd and not isMouseEnd then return end

        local completedGesture = gesture
        gesture = nil
        if not completedGesture.Dragged then
            local nextState = not entry.Active
            local ok, err = pcall(callback, nextState)
            if not ok then warn("[Syllinse] Mobile action failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
            -- If the callback is not linked to a toggle, still show the local action state.
            SyllinseRuntime.refreshMobileActionButton(entry, nextState)
        end
    end)

    return entry
end

SyllinseRuntime.attachKeybindSetting = function(label, options, settingName, linkedToggleControl)
    -- Lumen's Keybind callback reports the active state, but does not automatically
    -- toggle the feature's separate main switch. Link those two controls so a hotkey
    -- actually updates the same state as clicking the feature toggle.
    local keybindOptions = options or {}
    local originalCallback = keybindOptions.Callback
    if linkedToggleControl then
        keybindOptions.Callback = function(active)
            if type(originalCallback) == "function" then
                originalCallback(active)
            end
            if linkedToggleControl and type(linkedToggleControl.Set) == "function" then
                -- Use the explicit active state (not Set() with no argument), so
                -- Hold mode turns off on release and Toggle mode follows its key state.
                linkedToggleControl.Set(active)
            end
        end
    end

    local control = label:Keybind(keybindOptions)
    if settingName then
        SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings[settingName] = control.Key
        local originalSet = control.Set
        control.Set = function(key)
            originalSet(key)
            SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings[settingName] = key
            if control.Flag and Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] then
                Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Value = key
                Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
            end
        end
        if control.Flag and Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] then
            Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
        end
    end

    if touchInputEnabled and linkedToggleControl and type(keybindOptions.Callback) == "function" then
        local initialState = linkedToggleControl.State == true
        SyllinseRuntime.createMobileActionButton(
            settingName or keybindOptions.Flag,
            linkedToggleControl.Flag,
            keybindOptions.Callback,
            initialState
        )
    end

    -- Match MacLib's per-keybind visibility API while leaving Lumen's native keybind control intact.
    control.SetMobileButtonVisibility = function(selfOrState, state)
        if selfOrState == control then
            return SyllinseRuntime.setMobileActionButtonVisibility(settingName or keybindOptions.Flag, state)
        end
        return SyllinseRuntime.setMobileActionButtonVisibility(settingName or keybindOptions.Flag, selfOrState)
    end
    return control
end

-- Native wheel and touch scrolling share the same outer page viewport.
navContainer.ScrollingEnabled = true

SyllinseRuntime.stylePageScrollbar = function(scroll)
    local lock = SyllinseRuntime.dropdownScrollLocks and SyllinseRuntime.dropdownScrollLocks[scroll]
    scroll.ScrollingEnabled = not (lock and lock.Count > 0)
    scroll.ClipsDescendants = true
    scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 0
    scroll.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
    scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
    scroll.VerticalScrollBarPosition = Enum.VerticalScrollBarPosition.Right
end

SyllinseRuntime.makePage = function(name, icon)
    local page = LumenWindow:Page({ Icon = icon })
    local pageFrame = page.Frame
    local leftColumn = pageFrame and pageFrame:FindFirstChild("Left")
    local rightColumn = pageFrame and pageFrame:FindFirstChild("Right")
    assert(leftColumn and rightColumn, "Lumen page is missing its columns: " .. name)

    -- One viewport spans the content area to the shell's right edge.
    local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    scroll.Name = "SyllinsePageScroll_" .. name
    scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scroll.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    scroll.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SIDEBAR_WIDTH, PANEL_TOP)
    scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -SIDEBAR_WIDTH - 1, 1, -(PANEL_TOP + (footerBar.Visible and FOOTER_HEIGHT or 0)))
    scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
    scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    scroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    scroll.ScrollingEnabled = true
    SyllinseRuntime.stylePageScrollbar(scroll)
    scroll.Active = true
    scroll.ClipsDescendants = true
    scroll.Visible = false
    scroll.ZIndex = 1
    scroll.Parent = root
    SyllinseRuntime.bindMobileScrollGuard(scroll)

    -- Use viewport-local coordinates: native Page.Open resets the page origin to zero.
    -- These offsets preserve the previous full-window column positions and widths.
    local leftInset = 86 - SIDEBAR_WIDTH
    local rightInset = 41 - (SIDEBAR_WIDTH - 1) * 0.5
    local columnWidthOffset = (SIDEBAR_WIDTH + 1) * 0.5 - 48
    pageFrame.Name = "SyllinseLumenPage_" .. name
    pageFrame.Parent = scroll
    pageFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    pageFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    pageFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, PANEL_HEIGHT)
    pageFrame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    pageFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pageFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pageFrame.ClipsDescendants = false
    pageFrame.Visible = false
    if pageFrame:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
        pageFrame.ScrollingEnabled = false
        pageFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 0
        pageFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
    end
    for index, column in ipairs({ leftColumn, rightColumn }) do
        column.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        column.Position = index == 1 and UDim2.fromOffset(leftInset, 0) or UDim2.new(0.5, rightInset, 0, 0)
        column.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, columnWidthOffset, 0, PANEL_HEIGHT)
        column.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
        column.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
        column.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
        column.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        column.ScrollingEnabled = false
        column.ScrollBarThickness = 0
        column.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
        column.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        column.BorderSizePixel = 0
        column.ClipsDescendants = false
        column.Active = false
        column.ZIndex = 1
    end
    local function columnHeight(column, scale)
        local height = 0
        -- Relative physical bounds account for UIScale and cancel the outer scroll offset.
        for _, child in ipairs(column:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Visible then
                local bottom = (child.AbsolutePosition.Y - column.AbsolutePosition.Y + child.AbsoluteSize.Y) / scale
                height = math.max(height, bottom)
            end
        end
        local padding = column:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
        if padding then height = height + padding.PaddingBottom.Offset end
        return math.ceil(height + 8)
    end
    -- Keep native input and easing; map its travel to 80% for a gentle slowdown.
    -- Extend the native range proportionally so the last panel stays reachable.
    local scrollFactor = 0.8
    local function syncScrollOffset()
        local scale = math.max(uiScale.Scale, 0.001)
        pageFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, scroll.CanvasPosition.Y * (1 - scrollFactor) / scale)
    end
    scroll:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(syncScrollOffset)
    local function refreshScroll()
        if not scroll.Parent then return end
        local scale = math.max(uiScale.Scale, 0.001)
        local leftHeight = columnHeight(leftColumn, scale)
        local rightHeight = columnHeight(rightColumn, scale)
        local height = math.max(PANEL_HEIGHT, leftHeight, rightHeight)
        pageFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
        leftColumn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, columnWidthOffset, 0, height)
        rightColumn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, columnWidthOffset, 0, height)
        local canvasHeight = PANEL_HEIGHT + math.max(0, height - PANEL_HEIGHT) / scrollFactor
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, canvasHeight)
        local maxY = math.max(0, (canvasHeight - PANEL_HEIGHT) * scale)
        if scroll.CanvasPosition.Y > maxY then scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, maxY) end
        syncScrollOffset()
    end
    local queued = false
    local function scheduleRefresh()
        if queued then return end
        queued = true
        task.defer(function()
            queued = false
            refreshScroll()
        end)
    end
    for _, column in ipairs({ leftColumn, rightColumn }) do
        local layout = column:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
        if layout then layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(scheduleRefresh) end
        column.ChildAdded:Connect(scheduleRefresh)
        column.ChildRemoved:Connect(scheduleRefresh)
    end
    root:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(scheduleRefresh)
    scroll:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function() if scroll.Visible then scheduleRefresh() end end)
    lumenPages[name] = page
    lumenHosts[name] = { Left = leftColumn, Right = rightColumn, Shared = scroll, RefreshScroll = refreshScroll }
    pageFrame:SetAttribute("SyllinseTabName", name)
    scheduleRefresh()
    return page
end


SyllinseRuntime.pages = {
    Combat = SyllinseRuntime.makePage("Combat", "swords"),
    Desync = SyllinseRuntime.makePage("Desync", "activity"),
    Visuals = SyllinseRuntime.makePage("Visuals", "eye"),
    Configs = SyllinseRuntime.makePage("Configs", "save"),
    Misc = SyllinseRuntime.makePage("Misc", "wrench"),
    Settings = SyllinseRuntime.makePage("Settings", "settings"),
}

-- Bind MacLib-style scroll guards to the visible outer scroll containers in Lumen's
-- separate popup layers (for example, dropdown option lists). Avoid nested guards.
SyllinseRuntime.watchOuterMobileScrollFrames = function(layer)
    if not touchInputEnabled or not layer then return end
    local function consider(instance)
        if not instance:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then return end
        local parent = instance.Parent
        while parent and parent ~= layer do
            if parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then return end
            parent = parent.Parent
        end
        SyllinseRuntime.bindMobileScrollGuard(instance)
    end
    for _, descendant in ipairs(layer:GetDescendants()) do consider(descendant) end
    layer.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        if descendant:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then task.defer(consider, descendant) end
    end)
end
SyllinseRuntime.watchOuterMobileScrollFrames(Lumen._Instance)
SyllinseRuntime.watchOuterMobileScrollFrames(Lumen._Overlay)
SyllinseRuntime.watchOuterMobileScrollFrames(ReptillianLibrary.Holder.Instance)

-- Lay out the header and panels for the current shell before fitting it to the screen.
layoutResponsiveShell = function()
    if not root or not root.Parent then return end

    local canvasW, canvasH = baseWindowSize.X.Offset, baseWindowSize.Y.Offset
    local railWidth = SIDEBAR_WIDTH
    compactTouchLayout = false
    PANEL_TOP = 73
    FOOTER_HEIGHT = 34

    sidebar.Size = UDim2.new(0, railWidth, 1, 0)
    -- Stop at the footer's top edge, excluding the outer border pixels.
    sideDivider.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(railWidth - Theme.Style.DividerThickness, 1)
    local footerInset = footerBar.Visible and FOOTER_HEIGHT or 0
    sideDivider.Size = UDim2.new(0, Theme.Style.DividerThickness, 1, -(footerInset + (footerBar.Visible and 1 or 2)))
    header.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(railWidth, 0)
    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -railWidth, 0, PANEL_TOP)
    local headerWidth = canvasW - railWidth
    local showAvatar = headerWidth >= 500
    local searchWidth = math.clamp(headerWidth - (showAvatar and 244 or 200), 116, 242)
    local titleArea = headerWidth - searchWidth - (showAvatar and 112 or 54) - 10
    searchFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    searchFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 13)
    searchFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(searchWidth, 46)
    searchBox.PlaceholderText = searchWidth < 190 and "Search" or "Search Features"
    avatar.Visible = showAvatar
    avatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 42)
    avatar.Position = UDim2.new(1, -searchWidth - 112, 0, 15)
    dragHandle.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.min(470, titleArea), 1, 0)
    headerTitle.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.min(250, titleArea - 60), 0, 34)

    logo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 100)
    logo.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-7, -9)
    logoGlowCyan.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(102, 102)
    logoGlowCyan.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-13, -11)
    logoGlowViolet.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(98, 100)
    logoGlowViolet.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-10, -9)
    local logoFallback = sidebar:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback")
    if logoFallback then
        logoFallback.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 60)
        logoFallback.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 10)
    end

    -- Preserve the 55x55 touch targets after viewport/orientation changes.
    for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
        nav.Button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(NAV_BUTTON_SIZE, NAV_BUTTON_SIZE)
        nav.Button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(nav.BaseButtonX or NAV_BUTTON_X, nav.BaseY or 2)
    end
    if navContainer and navContainer.Parent then
        navContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -100 - footerInset)
        navContainer.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 4 + #navButtons * NAV_ROW_HEIGHT)
    end

    footerBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, FOOTER_HEIGHT)
    footerBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -FOOTER_HEIGHT)
    footerLeftText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -148, 1, 0)
    footerLeftText.TextSize = 14
    footerBrand.Size = UDim2.new(0, 132, 0, FOOTER_HEIGHT)
    footerBrand.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -FOOTER_HEIGHT)
    footerBrand.TextSize = 15
    PANEL_HEIGHT = canvasH - PANEL_TOP - (footerBar.Visible and FOOTER_HEIGHT or 0)

    for tabName, page in pairs(lumenPages) do
        local hosts = lumenHosts[tabName]
        local active = tabName == activeName
        if hosts and hosts.Shared then
            hosts.Shared.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(railWidth, PANEL_TOP)
            hosts.Shared.Size = UDim2.new(1, -railWidth - 1, 1, -(PANEL_TOP + (footerBar.Visible and FOOTER_HEIGHT or 0)))
            hosts.Shared.Visible = active
            SyllinseRuntime.stylePageScrollbar(hosts.Shared)
            if hosts.RefreshScroll then hosts.RefreshScroll() end
        end
        if page and page.Frame then page.Frame.Visible = active end
    end

end

-- Make each slider's displayed number directly editable without replacing its
-- native Lumen slider. The field is positioned over the existing value readout,
-- and routes numeric edits through the same native Set/Callback path as dragging.
SyllinseRuntime.addNumericSlider = function(section, options)
    local sliderOptions = table.clone(options or {})
    local userCallback = sliderOptions.Callback
    local currentValue = tonumber(sliderOptions.Value) or tonumber(sliderOptions.Min) or 0
    local minimum = tonumber(sliderOptions.Min) or 0
    local maximum = tonumber(sliderOptions.Max) or 100
    local increment = math.max(tonumber(sliderOptions.Increment) or 1, 0.000001)
    local suffix = type(sliderOptions.Suffix) == "string" and string.gsub(sliderOptions.Suffix, "^%s+", "") or ""
    local numericEditor
    local nativeValueLabel

    local function snapValue(value)
        value = tonumber(value)
        if not value then return currentValue end
        value = math.clamp(value, minimum, maximum)
        local steps = math.floor(((value - minimum) / increment) + 0.5)
        value = minimum + steps * increment
        return math.clamp(value, minimum, maximum)
    end

    local function formatNumber(value)
        local formatted = string.format("%.6f", tonumber(value) or currentValue)
        formatted = string.gsub(formatted, "0+$", "")
        formatted = string.gsub(formatted, "%.$", "")
        if formatted == "" or formatted == "-0" then formatted = "0" end
        return formatted
    end

    local function syncEditor(value)
        currentValue = tonumber(value) or currentValue
        if numericEditor and numericEditor.Parent and not numericEditor:IsFocused() then
            numericEditor.Text = formatNumber(currentValue)
        end
    end

    sliderOptions.Callback = function(value)
        currentValue = tonumber(value) or currentValue
        syncEditor(currentValue)
        if type(userCallback) == "function" then
            userCallback(value)
        end
    end

    local control = section:Slider(sliderOptions)
    local nativeSet = control and control.Set

    if type(nativeSet) == "function" then
        control.Set = function(value, ...)
            -- Most Lumen calls use dot-style Set(value); accept colon-style calls too.
            local extraArgs = table.pack(...)
            if value == control then
                value = extraArgs[1]
                local shifted = table.create(math.max(extraArgs.n - 1, 0))
                for i = 2, extraArgs.n do shifted[i - 1] = extraArgs[i] end
                extraArgs = table.pack(table.unpack(shifted))
            end
            local snapped = snapValue(value)
            local ok, result = pcall(nativeSet, snapped, table.unpack(extraArgs, 1, extraArgs.n))
            if not ok then
                -- Compatibility fallback for library revisions that expect self.
                ok, result = pcall(nativeSet, control, snapped, table.unpack(extraArgs, 1, extraArgs.n))
            end
            currentValue = tonumber(control.Value) or snapped
            syncEditor(currentValue)
            if control.Flag and Lumen and Lumen.Flags and Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] then
                Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
            end
            return ok and result or nil
        end
        if control.Flag and Lumen and Lumen.Flags and Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] then
            Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
        end
    end

    local function parseNumericPrefix(text)
        if type(text) ~= "string" then return nil end
        local valueText = string.match(text, "^%s*([+-]?%d+%.?%d*)")
        return valueText and tonumber(valueText) or nil
    end

    local function findValueLabel(frame)
        if not frame then return nil end
        local objects = {}
        if frame:IsA("TextLabel") or frame:IsA("TextButton") then
            table.insert(objects, frame)
        end
        for _, object in ipairs(frame:GetDescendants()) do
            if object:IsA("TextLabel") or object:IsA("TextButton") then
                table.insert(objects, object)
            end
        end
        local best, bestScore = nil, -math.huge
        for _, object in ipairs(objects) do
            local name = string.lower(object.Name or "")
            local number = parseNumericPrefix(object.Text)
            local score = -math.huge
            if number ~= nil then
                score = 10 - math.min(math.abs(number - currentValue), 1000) / 1000
                if math.abs(number - currentValue) < 0.0001 then score = score + 100 end
            end
            if name == "value" or name == "slidervalue" or name == "valuelabel" then
                score = math.max(score, 500)
            elseif string.find(name, "value", 1, true) or string.find(name, "number", 1, true) then
                score = math.max(score, 300)
            end
            if string.find(name, "title", 1, true) or name == "name" or name == "label" then
                score = score - 500
            end
            if score > bestScore then
                best, bestScore = object, score
            end
        end
        return bestScore > 0 and best or nil
    end

    -- Locate the actual Lumen SliderFrame from the owning section. Lumen's slider
    -- handle does not consistently expose Frame/Instance/Container, so relying on
    -- those fields can silently skip creation of the editor entirely.
    local function findSliderFrame()
        local sectionFrame = section and (section.Frame or section.Instance or section.Container)
        if not (sectionFrame and typeof(sectionFrame) == "Instance" and sectionFrame:IsA("GuiObject")) then
            return nil
        end

        local expectedTitle = string.lower(string.gsub(tostring(sliderOptions.Name or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1"))
        local candidates = {}
        local partialMatch = nil
        for _, object in ipairs(sectionFrame:GetDescendants()) do
            if object:IsA("GuiObject") and string.lower(object.Name or "") == "sliderframe" then
                table.insert(candidates, object)
                local exactTitleMatch = false
                local hasPartialTitleMatch = false
                for _, child in ipairs(object:GetDescendants()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local text = string.lower(string.gsub(tostring(child.Text or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1"))
                        if text == expectedTitle and expectedTitle ~= "" then
                            exactTitleMatch = true
                            break
                        elseif expectedTitle ~= "" and string.find(text, expectedTitle, 1, true) then
                            hasPartialTitleMatch = true
                        end
                    end
                end
                if exactTitleMatch then return object end
                if hasPartialTitleMatch and not partialMatch then partialMatch = object end
            end
        end
        if partialMatch then return partialMatch end
        if #candidates == 1 then return candidates[1] end
        return nil
    end

    task.defer(function()
        local frame
        -- Lumen may finish parenting/layout on the next task step. Re-resolve the
        -- named slider row briefly so every field attaches to its own slider.
        for attempt = 1, 12 do
            frame = findSliderFrame()
            if not (frame and frame:IsA("GuiObject")) then
                local fallback = control and (control.SliderFrame or control.Frame or control.Instance or control.Container)
                if fallback and typeof(fallback) == "Instance" and fallback:IsA("GuiObject") and string.lower(fallback.Name or "") == "sliderframe" then
                    frame = fallback
                end
            end
            if frame and frame.Parent then
                nativeValueLabel = findValueLabel(frame)
                if nativeValueLabel or attempt >= 3 then break end
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end

        if not (frame and typeof(frame) == "Instance" and frame:IsA("GuiObject") and frame.Parent) then
            warn("[Syllinse] Could not find the Lumen slider row for numeric editor: " .. tostring(sliderOptions.Name))
            return
        end

        local fieldParent = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.Parent or frame
        local rightScale, rightOffset = 1, -3
        local topScale, topOffset = 0, 0
        if nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.Parent then
            local labelPosition = nativeValueLabel.Position
            local labelSize = nativeValueLabel.Size
            local anchor = nativeValueLabel.AnchorPoint
            rightScale = labelPosition.X.Scale + (1 - anchor.X) * labelSize.X.Scale
            rightOffset = labelPosition.X.Offset + (1 - anchor.X) * labelSize.X.Offset
            topScale = labelPosition.Y.Scale - anchor.Y * labelSize.Y.Scale
            topOffset = labelPosition.Y.Offset - anchor.Y * labelSize.Y.Offset
            nativeValueLabel.Visible = false
        end

        -- Keep the numeric editor visually indistinguishable from Lumen's own value
        -- readout: compact, transparent, and using the same native type/color. A subtle
        -- fill/stroke appears only while editing so it remains discoverable without
        -- creating a large pill that clashes with the rest of the UI.
        local suffixText = suffix
        local suffixWidth = suffixText == "" and 0 or math.clamp(#suffixText * 5 + 3, 10, 42)
        local digitCount = #formatNumber(currentValue)
        local editorWidth = math.clamp(digitCount * 6 + 5, 24, 42)
        local nativeTextSize = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.TextSize or 12
        local nativeAbsHeight = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.AbsoluteSize.Y or 0
        local fieldHeight = math.clamp(nativeAbsHeight > 0 and nativeAbsHeight or (nativeTextSize + 2), 13, 17)
        local valueRightOffset = rightOffset - suffixWidth - (suffixWidth > 0 and 2 or 0)

        local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
        box.Name = "SyllinseNumericSliderInput"
        box.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        box.Position = UDim2.new(rightScale, valueRightOffset, topScale, topOffset - 1)
        box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(editorWidth, fieldHeight)
        box.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover
        box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        box.BorderSizePixel = 0
        box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        box.Text = formatNumber(currentValue)
        box.TextColor3 = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.TextColor3 or Theme.PanelValue
        box.TextSize = nativeTextSize
        box.TextTransparency = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.TextTransparency or 0
        box.FontFace = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.FontFace or FONT_REGULAR
        box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        box.TextYAlignment = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.TextYAlignment or Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        box.TextWrapped = false
        box.ZIndex = (nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.ZIndex or frame.ZIndex) + 3
        box.Parent = fieldParent
        local editorStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        editorStroke.Name = "SyllinseNumericSliderFocusStroke"
        editorStroke.Color = Theme.Stroke
        editorStroke.Transparency = 1
        editorStroke.Thickness = 1
        editorStroke.Parent = box
        setManualCorner(box, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.NumericInput)
        numericEditor = box
        box:SetAttribute("SyllinseNumericSliderInput", true)

        if suffixText ~= "" then
            local suffixLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            suffixLabel.Name = "SyllinseNumericSliderSuffix"
            suffixLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            suffixLabel.Position = UDim2.new(rightScale, rightOffset, topScale, topOffset - 1)
            suffixLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(suffixWidth, fieldHeight)
            suffixLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            suffixLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
            suffixLabel.Text = suffixText
            suffixLabel.TextColor3 = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.TextColor3 or Theme.PanelSecondary
            suffixLabel.TextSize = nativeTextSize
            suffixLabel.TextTransparency = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.TextTransparency or 0
            suffixLabel.FontFace = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.FontFace or FONT_REGULAR
            suffixLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            suffixLabel.TextYAlignment = nativeValueLabel and nativeValueLabel.TextYAlignment or Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            suffixLabel.ZIndex = box.ZIndex
            suffixLabel.Parent = fieldParent
        end

        box.Focused:Connect(function()
            box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            editorStroke.Transparency = 0.55
            task.defer(function()
                if box.Parent and box:IsFocused() and #box.Text > 0 then
                    box.SelectionStart = 1
                    box.CursorPosition = #box.Text + 1
                end
            end)
        end)

        box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local typed = tonumber(string.match(box.Text or "", "^%s*([+-]?%d+%.?%d*)"))
            if typed ~= nil then
                local snapped = snapValue(typed)
                if control and type(control.Set) == "function" then
                    local ok = pcall(control.Set, snapped)
                    if not ok then pcall(control.Set, control, snapped) end
                end
                currentValue = tonumber(control and control.Value) or snapped
            end
            box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            editorStroke.Transparency = 1
            syncEditor(currentValue)
        end)

        if nativeValueLabel then
            nativeValueLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
                local parsed = parseNumericPrefix(nativeValueLabel.Text)
                if parsed ~= nil then syncEditor(parsed) end
            end)
        end
        syncEditor(currentValue)
    end)

    return control
end

do
-- ---------- Combat ----------
SyllinseRuntime.AimMain = SyllinseRuntime.pages.Combat:Section({ Name = "Aimbot"; Side = "Left"; Icon = "crosshair" })
SyllinseRuntime.AimTarget = SyllinseRuntime.pages.Combat:Section({ Name = "Targeting"; Side = "Left"; Icon = "target" })

SyllinseRuntime.playerNames = {}
for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    if targetPlayer ~= player then table.insert(SyllinseRuntime.playerNames, targetPlayer.Name) end
end
SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions = SyllinseRuntime.playerNames
SyllinseRuntime.priorityDropdown = SyllinseRuntime.addReptillianDropdown(SyllinseRuntime.AimTarget, {
    Name = "Priority player"; Options = SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions; Value = SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions[1] or ""; Search = true; Flag = "PriorityPlayer";
    Callback = function(value) SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.PriorityPlayer = value end;
})
local priorityPlayerAdded = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(targetPlayer)
    if not gui.Parent then return end
    if targetPlayer ~= player and not table.find(SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions, targetPlayer.Name) then
        table.insert(SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions, targetPlayer.Name)
        SyllinseRuntime.priorityDropdown.UpdateOptions(SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions)
    end
end)
local priorityPlayerRemoving = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(targetPlayer)
    if not gui.Parent then return end
    local index = table.find(SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions, targetPlayer.Name)
    if index then
        table.remove(SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions, index)
        SyllinseRuntime.priorityDropdown.UpdateOptions(SyllinseRuntime.selectedPlayerOptions)
    end
end)
gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
    priorityPlayerAdded:Disconnect()
    priorityPlayerRemoving:Disconnect()
end)

do
-- ---------- Visuals ----------
SyllinseRuntime.VisualPlayers = SyllinseRuntime.pages.Visuals:Section({ Name = "Players"; Side = "Left"; Icon = "users" })

SyllinseRuntime.visualToggle(SyllinseRuntime.VisualPlayers, "Box ESP", "BoxESP", "BoxESP", true)
SyllinseRuntime.visualToggle(SyllinseRuntime.VisualPlayers, "Name ESP", "NameESP", "NameESP", true)
SyllinseRuntime.visualToggle(SyllinseRuntime.VisualPlayers, "Health ESP", "HealthESP", "HealthESP", true)
SyllinseRuntime.visualToggle(SyllinseRuntime.VisualPlayers, "Distance ESP", "DistanceESP", "DistanceESP", false)
SyllinseRuntime.visualToggle(SyllinseRuntime.VisualPlayers, "Tracer ESP", "TracerESP", "TracerESP", false)

    Callback = function(value) SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.VisualRange = value end;

-- ---------- Configs ----------
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigList = SyllinseRuntime.pages.Configs:Section({ Name = "Config manager"; Side = "Left"; Icon = "folder" })
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions = SyllinseRuntime.pages.Configs:Section({ Name = "Actions"; Side = "Right"; Icon = "save" })
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigList:Paragraph({
    Title = "Lumen config storage";
    Body = "Configs use Lumen's native flag serialization. File save/load requires the file APIs available in your client environment.";
})
SyllinseRuntime.configNameInput = SyllinseRuntime.ConfigList:Input({
    Name = "Config name"; Value = "Syllinse"; Placeholder = "Type a config name";
    Callback = function() end;
})
SyllinseRuntime.cleanConfigNames = function(values)
    local clean = {}
    if type(values) == "table" then
        for _, name in ipairs(values) do
            if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
                table.insert(clean, name)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(clean)
    return clean
end

SyllinseRuntime.configNames = SyllinseRuntime.cleanConfigNames(Lumen.ListConfigs())
SyllinseRuntime.selectedConfig = SyllinseRuntime.addReptillianDropdown(SyllinseRuntime.ConfigList, {
    Name = "Saved configs"; Options = SyllinseRuntime.configNames; Value = SyllinseRuntime.configNames[1] or ""; Search = true;
    Callback = function(value)
        if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then SyllinseRuntime.configNameInput.Set(value) end
    end;
})
SyllinseRuntime.refreshConfigs = function(preferredName, silent)
    local ok, options = pcall(function() return Lumen.ListConfigs() end)
    if not ok or type(options) ~= "table" then
        SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Could not list config files in this environment.", "Warning")
        return false
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.configNames = SyllinseRuntime.cleanConfigNames(options)
    SyllinseRuntime.selectedConfig.UpdateOptions(SyllinseRuntime.configNames)
    if type(preferredName) == "string" and table.find(SyllinseRuntime.configNames, preferredName) then
        SyllinseRuntime.selectedConfig.Set(preferredName)
    elseif #SyllinseRuntime.configNames == 0 then
        SyllinseRuntime.selectedConfig.Set("")
    end
    if not silent then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Config list refreshed.", "Info", 1.5) end
    return true
end

SyllinseRuntime.getSelectedConfigName = function()
    local value = SyllinseRuntime.selectedConfig.Value
    if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" and table.find(SyllinseRuntime.configNames, value) then
        return value
    end
    -- Some clients expose read/write APIs but not listfiles; typed names remain usable there.
    if #SyllinseRuntime.configNames == 0 then
        local typed = SyllinseRuntime.configNameInput.Value
        if type(typed) == "string" then
            typed = typed:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if typed ~= "" then return typed end
        end
    end
    return ""
end
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions:Paragraph({
    Title = "Profile actions";
    Body = "Save or load all Lumen flags, including the control values in Combat, Desync, Visuals and Settings.";
})
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions:Label({
    Text = "Autoload config: checking...";
    Color = Theme.PanelText;
})

-- Lumen label rows are builder objects and do not expose a :Set() method.
-- Update the actual rendered TextLabel instead, so this status row cannot abort
-- the remaining UI construction if the library's wrapper API changes.
SyllinseRuntime.setAutoloadStatusText = function(text)
    -- Sections can be reparented outside the original column hosts.
    -- Cache the rendered label and search the actual GUI roots when needed.
    local label = SyllinseRuntime.autoloadStatusLabel
    if label and label.Parent then
        label.Text = text
        return true
    end
    local function updateIn(rootGui)
        if typeof(rootGui) ~= "Instance" then return false end
        for _, descendant in ipairs(rootGui:GetDescendants()) do
            if (descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextButton"))
                and string.find(descendant.Text, "^Autoload config:") then
                SyllinseRuntime.autoloadStatusLabel = descendant
                descendant.Text = text
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    if updateIn(gui) then return true end
    if updateIn(Lumen._Instance) then return true end
    return updateIn(Lumen._Overlay)
end
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Save config";
    Callback = function()
        local name = tostring(SyllinseRuntime.configNameInput.Value or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if name == "" then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Enter a config name first.", "Warning"); return end
        local ok, saved = pcall(function() return Lumen.SaveConfig(name) end)
        if ok and saved then
            SyllinseRuntime.refreshConfigs(name, true)
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Saved '" .. name .. "'.", "Success")
        else
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Save failed. Check file API support.", "Error")
        end
    end;
})
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Load selected config";
    Callback = function()
        local name = SyllinseRuntime.getSelectedConfigName()
        if name == "" then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Choose a saved config first.", "Warning"); return end
        local ok, loaded = pcall(function() return Lumen.LoadConfig(name) end)
        if ok and loaded then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Loaded '" .. name .. "'.", "Success")
        else SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Load failed or the file does not exist.", "Error") end
    end;
})
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Delete selected config";
    Callback = function()
        local name = SyllinseRuntime.getSelectedConfigName()
        if name == "" then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Choose a saved config first.", "Warning"); return end
        local ok, deleted = pcall(function() return Lumen.DeleteConfig(name) end)
        if ok and deleted then SyllinseRuntime.refreshConfigs(nil, true); SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Deleted '" .. name .. "'.", "Success")
        else SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Delete failed or the file does not exist.", "Error") end
    end;
})
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions:Button({ Name = "Refresh config list"; Callback = SyllinseRuntime.refreshConfigs; })

-- The selected config name is stored separately so it can be loaded on the next launch.
SyllinseRuntime.AUTOLOAD_FILE = "Syllinse_Autoload.txt"
getAutoloadName = function()
    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then return nil end
    local okExists, exists = pcall(isfile, SyllinseRuntime.AUTOLOAD_FILE)
    if not okExists or not exists then return nil end
    local okRead, value = pcall(readfile, SyllinseRuntime.AUTOLOAD_FILE)
    if okRead and type(value) == "string" then
        value = value:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if value ~= "" then return value end
    end
    return nil
end
SyllinseRuntime.refreshAutoloadStatus = function()
    local name = getAutoloadName()
    local text = name and ("Autoload config: " .. name) or "Autoload config: None"
    -- Never let a cosmetic status refresh break the UI startup flow.
    local ok, updated = pcall(SyllinseRuntime.setAutoloadStatusText, text)
    if not ok or not updated then
        warn("[Syllinse] Could not refresh the autoload status label")
    end
end
SyllinseRuntime.refreshAutoloadStatus()
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Set selected as autoload";
    Callback = function()
        local name = SyllinseRuntime.getSelectedConfigName()
        if name == "" then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Choose or enter a saved config first.", "Warning"); return end
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Autoload requires file-write support in this client.", "Warning")
            return
        end
        local ok = pcall(writefile, SyllinseRuntime.AUTOLOAD_FILE, name)
        if ok then
            SyllinseRuntime.refreshAutoloadStatus()
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "'" .. name .. "' will load on startup.", "Success")
        else
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Could not save the autoload selection.", "Error")
        end
    end;
})
SyllinseRuntime.ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Clear autoload";
    Callback = function()
        if type(delfile) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Clearing autoload requires file-delete support.", "Warning")
            return
        end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            if isfile(SyllinseRuntime.AUTOLOAD_FILE) then delfile(SyllinseRuntime.AUTOLOAD_FILE) end
        end)
        if ok then
            SyllinseRuntime.refreshAutoloadStatus()
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Autoload cleared.", "Info")
        else SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Could not clear autoload.", "Error") end
    end;
})

end

-- Optional manually supplied image for the Appearance/color section icon.
-- Leaving Image empty preserves Lumen's built-in palette glyph.
do
    local appearanceAsset = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.Appearance.Image
    if appearanceAsset ~= nil and appearanceAsset ~= "" then
        local sectionFrame = SyllinseRuntime.settingsRight.Frame
        local sectionHeader = sectionFrame and sectionFrame:FindFirstChild("Header")
        local sectionIcon = sectionHeader and sectionHeader:FindFirstChild("Icon")
        if sectionIcon and (sectionIcon:IsA("ImageLabel") or sectionIcon:IsA("ImageButton")) then
            sectionIcon.Image = appearanceAsset
            sectionIcon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            sectionIcon.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.zero
            sectionIcon.ImageRectSize = Vector2.zero
            sectionIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
                SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.Appearance.Size,
                SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.Appearance.Size
            )
        end
    end
end
-- This panel intentionally has no header icon or empty icon slot. Keep the title aligned
-- to the normal left padding instead of reserving space for a missing image.
do
    local buttonSectionHeader = SyllinseRuntime.settingsButton.Frame and SyllinseRuntime.settingsButton.Frame:FindFirstChild("Header")
    if buttonSectionHeader then
        local buttonSectionIcon = buttonSectionHeader:FindFirstChild("Icon")
        if buttonSectionIcon and buttonSectionIcon:IsA("ImageLabel") then
            buttonSectionIcon.Image = ""
            buttonSectionIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            buttonSectionIcon.Visible = false
        end
        local buttonSectionLayout = buttonSectionHeader:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
        if buttonSectionLayout then
            buttonSectionLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 0)
        end
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.settingsLeft:Paragraph({
    Title = "Automatic Window Size";
    Body = "The window automatically fits your screen. Lower the scale to shrink the whole window.";
})
SyllinseRuntime.addNumericSlider(SyllinseRuntime.settingsLeft, {
    Name = "UI scale";
    Suffix = "%";
    Value = windowSizePercent;
    Min = 75;
    Max = 100;
    Increment = 1;
    Flag = "SyllinseWindowScale";
    Callback = function(value)
        windowSizePercent = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or windowSizePercent, 75, 100)
        -- Direct percentage mapping: 100% is exactly the authored window size.
        windowSizeMultiplier = windowSizePercent / 100
        updateScale(true)
        -- Existing Lumen popups are rendered in a separate ScreenGui, so refresh
        -- their native geometry and TextSize immediately when the slider changes.
        for _, layer in ipairs({ Lumen._Instance, Lumen._Overlay }) do
            if layer then
                for _, child in ipairs(layer:GetChildren()) do
                    syncDropdownPopupScale(child)
                end
            end
        end
    end;
})

    Name = "Reset UI Position";
    Callback = function()
        -- Clear the custom-position flag; updateScale then restores the correct centered
        -- position for the current PC/mobile viewport and reapplies automatic fitting.
        windowHasCustomPosition = false
        updateScale()
        SyllinseRuntime.notify("Syllinse", "Window position reset to center.", "Success", 1.5)
    end;
})

SyllinseRuntime.terminologyLabelSetting = SyllinseRuntime.settingsLeft:Label({ Text = "Show Menu Function Terminology" })
SyllinseRuntime.addToggle(SyllinseRuntime.terminologyLabelSetting, {
    State = showMenuFunctionTerminology,
    Flag = "SyllinseMenuFunctionTerminology",
    Callback = function(state)
        showMenuFunctionTerminology = state == true
        SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.ShowMenuFunctionTerminology = showMenuFunctionTerminology
        for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
            local label = terminologyLabels[nav.Name]
            -- Keep the tab buttons' original position and hitbox in both modes.
            -- Labels remain independent of the clickable button; their UIScale
            -- mirrors the button's hover tween so the visual animation stays synced.
            if label then
                local targetY = nav.TerminologyLabelY or ((nav.BaseY or 122) + (nav.BaseIconSize or 64) + 25)
                if showMenuFunctionTerminology then
                    label.Visible = true
                    label.TextTransparency = 1
                    label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, targetY + 4)
                    tween(label, 0.16, {
                        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, targetY),
                        TextTransparency = 0,
                    })
                else
                    tween(label, 0.12, {
                        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, targetY + 4),
                        TextTransparency = 1,
                    })
                    task.delay(0.13, function()
                        if label.Parent and not showMenuFunctionTerminology then
                            label.Visible = false
                            label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, targetY + 4)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end,
})

-- A user-rebindable key hides only the Syllinse shell. Lumen's overlay remains enabled
-- so the watermark, keybind list, and notifications stay visible when the menu is hidden.
SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKey = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl
SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyListening = false
SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyLabel = SyllinseRuntime.settingsLeft:Label({ Text = "Toggle UI key" })
SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl = SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyLabel:Keybind({
    Title = "Toggle UI";
    Key = SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKey;
    Type = "Toggle";
    Flag = "SyllinseToggleUIKey";
    Callback = function() end;
})
if SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl and SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Key ~= nil then
    SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKey = SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Key
end
if SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl and type(SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Set) == "function" then
    local originalUiToggleKeySet = SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Set
    SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Set = function(key)
        originalUiToggleKeySet(key)
        SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKey = key
    end
    if SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Flag then
        Lumen.Flags[SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Flag] = Lumen.Flags[SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Flag] or {}
        Lumen.Flags[SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Flag].Set = SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Set
    end
    local keyButton = SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Frame and SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyControl.Frame:FindFirstChild("KeyButton")
    if keyButton then
        keyButton.Activated:Connect(function()
            SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyListening = true
        end)
    end
end

    Name = "Test notification";
    Callback = function() SyllinseRuntime.notify("Settings", "Notification preferences are active.", "Success") end;
})

SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelTypography = nil

SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelTypography = function(color)
    -- Use a clearer hierarchy: section headings are largest, controls are consistent,
    -- and helper text is quieter. Dropdown labels stay capped to avoid crowding fields.
    local controlText = color or Theme.PanelText
    local sectionTitle = Theme.PanelTitle
    local secondaryText = Theme.PanelSecondary
    local iconColor = Theme.PanelIcon
    local valueColor = Theme.PanelValue
    local brighterDefaultText = (SyllinseRuntime.activeAppearancePreset or "Original") == "Original"

    -- Keep each authored TextSize unchanged. The root UIScale enlarges the whole
    -- control tree together, so labels and their layout bounds stay proportional.
    local function setNativeTypographySize(object, minimum, maximum)
        local baseSize = nativeTextBaseSizes[object]
        if baseSize == nil then
            baseSize = object.TextSize
        end
        baseSize = math.clamp(baseSize, minimum, maximum)
        nativeTextBaseSizes[object] = baseSize
        object.TextSize = math.max(1, math.floor(baseSize + 0.5))
    end

    local function hasDropdownAncestor(object, sectionFrame)
        local current, inDropdown = object, false
        while current and current ~= sectionFrame do
            if current:GetAttribute("SyllinseManagedDropdown") then return true, true end
            local ancestorName = string.lower(current.Name or "")
            if ancestorName:find("dropdown", 1, true)
                or ancestorName:find("drop-down", 1, true)
                or ancestorName == "inputframe"
                or ancestorName == "buttonframe" then
                inDropdown = true
            end
            current = current.Parent
        end
        return inDropdown, false
    end

    local function makeMobileRowTappable(control, sectionFrame, physicalHeight)
        if not (touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled) or not control then return end
        Lumen._SyllinseExpandMobileControlRow(control, physicalHeight or 40)
    end

    for _, section in ipairs(Lumen.Sections or {}) do
        local frame = section.Frame
        if frame and frame.Parent then
            local panelRadius = math.max(0, tonumber(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Panel) or 0)
            setManualCorner(frame, panelRadius)

            -- Keep the header aligned with the softer panel radius without changing
            -- any of Lumen's buttons or their original styling.
            -- The parent panel supplies the shared surface and opacity.
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0

            local headerFrame = frame:FindFirstChild("Header")
            if headerFrame then
                SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelDivider(frame, SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparency or 0)
            end

            local headerCorner = headerFrame and headerFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
            if headerCorner then
                headerCorner.TopLeftRadius = UDim.new(0, panelRadius)
                headerCorner.TopRightRadius = UDim.new(0, panelRadius)
                headerCorner.BottomLeftRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
                headerCorner.BottomRightRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
            end

            for _, descendant in ipairs(frame:GetDescendants()) do
                local name = string.lower(descendant.Name or "")
                if name == "labelframe" then
                    local left = descendant:FindFirstChild("LeftContent")
                    local right = descendant:FindFirstChild("RightContent")
                    local caption = left and left:FindFirstChild("Label")
                    local swatch = right and right:FindFirstChild("Button")
                    if caption and swatch and not left:FindFirstChild("Toggle") then
                        local touchFirst = touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
                        local height = touchFirst and SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.ColorSwatch.TouchSize
                            or SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.ColorSwatch.DesktopSize
                        left.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                        left.AnchorPoint, left.Position = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
                        left.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(height + 10), 0, height)
                        right.AnchorPoint, right.Position = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
                        caption.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                        caption.AnchorPoint, caption.Position = Vector2.new(0, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
                        caption.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        caption.TextXAlignment, caption.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                        caption.TextWrapped, caption.TextScaled = false, false
                    end
                end
                local inDropdown, managedDropdown = hasDropdownAncestor(descendant, frame)
                -- Open lists now live inside their fields. Their native checkmark,
                -- selection colors, option fonts and shared arrow styling must not
                -- be overwritten by the general panel pass on theme refresh.
                if managedDropdown then continue end

                if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextBox") or descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
                    local nativeAlpha = descendant:GetAttribute("SyllinseOriginalTextTransparency")
                    if nativeAlpha == nil then
