                        nativeAlpha = tonumber(descendant.TextTransparency) or 0
                        descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseOriginalTextTransparency", nativeAlpha)
                    end
                    descendant.TextTransparency = brighterDefaultText and nativeAlpha < 1
                        and math.min(nativeAlpha, 0.04) or nativeAlpha
                end

                if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextBox") then
                    local targetColor = controlText
                    local isSectionTitle = descendant.Name == "Title"
                        and descendant.Parent
                        and descendant.Parent.Name == "Header"
                    local isSecondary = name:find("body", 1, true)
                        or name:find("description", 1, true)
                        or name:find("sublabel", 1, true)
                        or name:find("placeholder", 1, true)
                    local isValue = name == "amount" or name:find("value", 1, true)

                    if isSectionTitle then
                        targetColor = sectionTitle
                        descendant.FontFace = FONT_SEMIBOLD
                        setNativeTypographySize(descendant, 16, 17)
                        -- Shift only the section title text down by 1 px without moving the icon or divider.
                        -- PaddingTop=2 moves the centered text content down by 1 px and is reused on refresh.
                        local titleYOffset = descendant:FindFirstChild("SyllinseTitleYOffset")
                        if not titleYOffset then
                            titleYOffset = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                            titleYOffset.Name = "SyllinseTitleYOffset"
                            titleYOffset.Parent = descendant
                        end
                        titleYOffset.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2)
                        titleYOffset.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 0)
                        titleYOffset.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 0)
                        titleYOffset.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 0)
                    elseif isSecondary then
                        targetColor = secondaryText
                        descendant.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
                        setNativeTypographySize(descendant, 12, 13)
                    elseif isValue then
                        targetColor = valueColor
                        descendant.FontFace = FONT_MEDIUM
                        if inDropdown and name == "value" then
                            -- Reptillian's closed selected-value label has its own editable
                            -- ClosedTextSize. Do not let the generic panel typography pass
                            -- silently clamp it back to 14 px.
                            local closedDropdownTextSize = math.max(1,
                                tonumber(SYLLINSE_REPTILLIAN_DROPDOWN_STYLE.ClosedTextSize) or 14)
                            setNativeTypographySize(descendant, closedDropdownTextSize, closedDropdownTextSize)
                        else
                            setNativeTypographySize(descendant, 14, 14)
                        end
                    else
                        descendant.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
                        setNativeTypographySize(descendant, 14, 14)
                    end

                    -- Label text above dropdown fields is the cramped case. A 14 px cap
                    -- keeps those labels inside their original row without moving any UI.
                    if inDropdown and not isValue then
                        setNativeTypographySize(descendant, 1, 14)
                    end

                    if inDropdown and (name == "input" or isValue) then
                        targetColor = Theme.PanelValue
                    end
                    descendant.TextColor3 = targetColor
                    if descendant:IsA("TextBox") then
                        descendant.PlaceholderColor3 = secondaryText
                        descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover
                    end
                elseif descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
                    descendant.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
                    -- Color swatches sit inside RightContent. Make the visible swatch
                    -- slightly larger on desktop and mobile while preserving its real click action.
                    if descendant.Name == "Button"
                        and descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent.Name == "RightContent" then
                        local touchFirst = touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
                        local swatchSize = touchFirst and SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.ColorSwatch.TouchSize
                            or SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.ColorSwatch.DesktopSize
                        descendant.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(swatchSize, swatchSize)
                        setManualCorner(descendant, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.ColorSwatch.CornerRadius)
                        if touchFirst then
                            Lumen._SyllinseExpandMobileControlRow(descendant, 42)
                        end
                    end
                    if name == "button" and descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent.Name == "SliderFrame" then
                        descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderTrack
                    end
                    -- Keep the main toggle target comfortably tappable on mobile.
                    if descendant.Name == "Toggle" then
                        makeMobileRowTappable(descendant, frame, 40)
                    elseif descendant.Name == "KeyButton" then
                        makeMobileRowTappable(descendant, frame, 40)
                    elseif descendant.Name == "Click" then
                        makeMobileRowTappable(descendant, frame, 36)
                    end
                    if descendant.Name ~= "Click" then
                        if inDropdown and descendant.Name == "Input" then
                            -- Selected values such as Head and Closest are brighter than helper labels.
                            descendant.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelValue
                            descendant.TextTransparency = 0
                        else
                            descendant.TextColor3 = controlText
                        end
                        setNativeTypographySize(descendant, 14, 14)
                        -- Dropdown font caps are applied to the native baseline above,
                        -- then multiplied by the same physical size factor as the row.
                    else
                        setNativeTypographySize(descendant, 13, 13)
                        descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ButtonFill or (Theme.ThemedControls
                            and Theme.Surface:Lerp(Theme.Accent, Theme.Style.ButtonTint) or Theme.Surface)
                        if Theme.ThemedControls then descendant.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelValue end
                        descendant.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                        descendant.AutoButtonColor = false
                        descendant.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        local buttonCorner = descendant:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
                        if not buttonCorner then
                            buttonCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                            buttonCorner.Parent = descendant
                        end
                        buttonCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.ControlButton)
                        local buttonStroke = descendant:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                        if not buttonStroke then
                            buttonStroke = addStroke(descendant, Theme.Stroke, 0.08, 1)
                        end
                        buttonStroke.Color = Theme.Stroke
                        buttonStroke.Transparency = 0.08
                        if descendant:GetAttribute("SyllinseButtonHoverBound") ~= true then
                            descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseButtonHoverBound", true)
                            descendant.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                                if isPopupInteractionBlocked(descendant) then return end
                                if descendant.Parent then
                                    local fill = Theme.ButtonHoverFill or (Theme.ThemedControls
                                        and Theme.Surface:Lerp(Theme.Accent, Theme.Style.ButtonHoverTint) or Theme.SurfaceHover)
                                    tween(descendant, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = fill })
                                end
                            end)
                            descendant.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                                if descendant.Parent then
                                    local fill = Theme.ButtonFill or (Theme.ThemedControls
                                        and Theme.Surface:Lerp(Theme.Accent, Theme.Style.ButtonTint) or Theme.Surface)
                                    tween(descendant, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = fill })
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                elseif descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") or descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
                    -- Use the supplied image asset only for Lumen's settings/keybind icon.
                    -- The notification bell keeps its original image and behavior.
                    local isSettingsIcon = descendant.Name == "Settings"
                        and descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent.Name == "RightContent"
                    local isNotificationBell = string.find(descendant.Name, "FeatureNotificationBell_", 1, true) == 1
                        and descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent.Name == "RightContent"

                    if isSettingsIcon or isNotificationBell then
                        local touchFirst = touchInputEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
                        local iconConfig = isNotificationBell
                            and SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.NotificationBell
                            or SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.Settings
                        local iconSize = touchFirst and iconConfig.TouchSize or iconConfig.DesktopSize
                        descendant.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(iconSize, iconSize)
                        descendant.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        descendant.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        descendant.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                        descendant.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.zero
                        descendant.ImageRectSize = Vector2.zero
                        descendant.ClipsDescendants = false
                        if descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
                            descendant.Parent.ClipsDescendants = false
                        end

                        if isNotificationBell then
                            -- This remains the original bell asset; edit its ID in SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.
                            descendant.Image = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.NotificationBell.Image
                            local featureFlag = descendant:GetAttribute("FeatureFlag")
                            local enabled = featureFlag and SyllinseRuntime.featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true
                            descendant.ImageColor3 = enabled and Theme.Text or Theme.Muted
                        else
                            -- Keep the supplied image on Lumen's original control. No overlay or extra hitbox.
                            descendant.Image = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.Settings.Image
                            descendant.ImageTransparency = 0

                            local clickTarget = descendant
                            while clickTarget and not clickTarget:IsA("GuiButton") and clickTarget ~= root do
                                clickTarget = clickTarget.Parent
                            end
                            if clickTarget == root then clickTarget = nil end
                            local hoverTarget = clickTarget or descendant
                            if clickTarget then clickTarget.AutoButtonColor = false end

                            if descendant:GetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconEventsBound") ~= true then
                                descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconEventsBound", true)
                                descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconActive", false)
                                descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconHovered", false)

                                local function refresh(icon)
                                    if not icon or not icon.Parent then return end
                                    local active = icon:GetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconActive") == true
                                    local hovered = icon:GetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconHovered") == true
                                    local tint = active and iconConfig.SelectedColor
                                        or (hovered and iconConfig.HoverColor or Theme.Muted)
                                    if Theme.ThemedControls then
                                        tint = active and Theme.Accent or (hovered and Theme.PanelValue or Theme.PanelIcon)
                                    end
                                    local asset = SYLLINSE_MANUAL_ICONS.Settings.Image
                                    if icon.Image ~= asset then icon.Image = asset end
                                    if icon.ImageTransparency ~= 0 then icon.ImageTransparency = 0 end
                                    if icon.ImageColor3 ~= tint then icon.ImageColor3 = tint end
                                    if icon.BackgroundTransparency ~= 1 then icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
                                    if icon:IsA("ImageButton") then
                                        if icon.HoverImage ~= asset then icon.HoverImage = asset end
                                        if icon.PressedImage ~= asset then icon.PressedImage = asset end
                                        if icon.AutoButtonColor then icon.AutoButtonColor = false end
                                    end
                                end

                                SyllinseRuntime.refreshSettingsIconTint = refresh
                                SyllinseRuntime.clearActiveSettingsIconSelection = function()
                                    local activeIcon = SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon
                                    SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon = nil
                                    if activeIcon and activeIcon.Parent then
                                        -- Popup close always returns directly to the normal bell-matched tint.
                                        -- Clear stale hover too, so the icon cannot pass through the hover shade.
                                        activeIcon:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconActive", false)
                                        activeIcon:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconHovered", false)
                                        refresh(activeIcon)
                                    end
                                end

                                hoverTarget.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                                    if isPopupInteractionBlocked(hoverTarget) then return end
                                    descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconHovered", true)
                                    refresh(descendant)
                                end)
                                hoverTarget.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                                    descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconHovered", false)
                                    refresh(descendant)
                                end)

                                local popupVisibilityBeforePress
                                local function isPrimaryPress(input)
                                    return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                                        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                                end

                                local function toggleSettingsIconState()
                                    local wasActive = descendant:GetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconActive") == true
                                    if wasActive then
                                        descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconActive", false)
                                        if SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon == descendant then
                                            SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon = nil
                                        end
                                    else
                                        local previous = SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon
                                        if previous and previous ~= descendant and previous.Parent then
                                            previous:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconActive", false)
                                            refresh(previous)
                                        end
                                        descendant:SetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconActive", true)
                                        SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon = descendant
                                    end
                                    refresh(descendant)

                                    if descendant:GetAttribute("SyllinseSettingsIconActive") == true then
                                        watchLumenPopupOpenedBySettingsIcon(descendant, popupVisibilityBeforePress)
                                    end
                                    popupVisibilityBeforePress = nil
                                end

                                if clickTarget then
                                    -- Snapshot before Lumen's Activated handler opens the popup.
                                    -- The original button and its hitbox remain untouched.
                                    clickTarget.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                                        if isPrimaryPress(input) then
                                            popupVisibilityBeforePress = snapshotLumenPopupVisibility()
                                        end
                                    end)
                                    clickTarget.Activated:Connect(toggleSettingsIconState)
                                else
                                    -- Some Lumen builds render the gear as a bare ImageLabel.
                                    -- Use only the image's own input area; no overlay or extra hitbox.
                                    descendant.Active = true
                                    descendant.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                                        if isPrimaryPress(input) then
                                            popupVisibilityBeforePress = snapshotLumenPopupVisibility()
                                            toggleSettingsIconState()
                                        end
                                    end)
                                end

                                for _, propertyName in ipairs({ "ImageColor3", "ImageTransparency", "Image" }) do
                                    descendant:GetPropertyChangedSignal(propertyName):Connect(function()
                                        refresh(descendant)
                                    end)
                                end
                                if descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
                                    descendant:GetPropertyChangedSignal("HoverImage"):Connect(function() refresh(descendant) end)
                                    descendant:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PressedImage"):Connect(function() refresh(descendant) end)
                                end
                                if clickTarget then
                                    clickTarget:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AutoButtonColor"):Connect(function()
                                        if clickTarget.AutoButtonColor then clickTarget.AutoButtonColor = false end
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                            (SyllinseRuntime.refreshSettingsIconTint or function() end)(descendant)
                        end

                        if touchFirst then
                            Lumen._SyllinseExpandMobileControlRow(descendant, 40)
                        end
                    elseif name == "sliderbar" then
                        descendant.ImageColor3 = Theme.SliderTrack
                    else
                        descendant.ImageColor3 = iconColor
                    end
                elseif descendant:IsA("Frame") then
                    if name == "button" and descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent.Name == "SliderFrame" then
                        descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderTrack
                    elseif name == "overlay" and descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent.Name == "Button"
                        and descendant.Parent.Parent and descendant.Parent.Parent.Name == "SliderFrame" then
                        local gradient = descendant:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                        if gradient then
                            descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                            gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Theme.SliderFillBottom, Theme.SliderFillTop)
                            gradient.Enabled = true
                        else
                            descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderFillTop
                        end
                    elseif name == "circle" then
                        descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderHandle
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Lumen can rebuild native widget visuals during theme changes. Re-apply the
    -- manually configured switch size after all section styling has completed.
    for _, refreshToggleStyle in ipairs(SyllinseRuntime.toggleStyleRefreshers or {}) do
        pcall(refreshToggleStyle)
    end
end

-- Reset the settings icon as soon as Lumen closes its active popup. Do not gate
-- this on popup names or on other visible popups; those checks caused stale highlights.
if Lumen.OpenPopup and type(Lumen.OpenPopup.Close) == "function"
    and not Lumen.OpenPopup._SyllinseSettingsCloseWrappedV91 then
    Lumen.OpenPopup._SyllinseSettingsCloseWrappedV91 = true
    local originalClose = Lumen.OpenPopup.Close
    Lumen.OpenPopup.Close = function(...)
        local closingIcon = SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon
        local results = table.pack(originalClose(...))
        -- Apply the idle tint synchronously after Lumen closes the popup.
        -- The visibility watcher remains as a fallback for popups hidden externally.
        if closingIcon
            and SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon == closingIcon
            and SyllinseRuntime.clearActiveSettingsIconSelection then
            SyllinseRuntime.clearActiveSettingsIconSelection()
        end
        return table.unpack(results, 1, results.n)
    end
end

-- Escape is another native way users close a Lumen popup.
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape
        and SyllinseRuntime.activeSettingsIcon
        and SyllinseRuntime.clearActiveSettingsIconSelection then
        SyllinseRuntime.clearActiveSettingsIconSelection()
    end
end)

-- Keep Lumen popups and overlay text consistent with the Syllinse shell.
-- Use FontFace + explicit weights so letterforms are consistent across all UI layers.
SyllinseRuntime.applyLumenTypeface = function(instance)
    if not instance then return end
    local function styleText(object)
        if not (object:IsA("TextLabel") or object:IsA("TextButton") or object:IsA("TextBox")) then
            return
        end
        local name = string.lower(object.Name or "")
        local parentName = object.Parent and string.lower(object.Parent.Name or "") or ""
        local isHeader = (name == "title" and parentName == "header")
            or name == "toasttitle"
            or name == "notificationtitle"
            or name == "paragraphheader"
            or name == "headertext"
        -- Preserve bold styling on switch labels when this global typography pass runs.
        local isToggleLabel = object:GetAttribute("SyllinseToggleLabel") == true
        if isHeader then
            object.FontFace = FONT_BOLD
        elseif isToggleLabel then
            object.FontFace = getSwitchLabelFontFace()
        elseif object:GetAttribute("SyllinseFieldText") == true then
            object.FontFace = FONT_MEDIUM
        else
            object.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
        end        object.TextScaled = false
        object.AutoLocalize = false
    end
    styleText(instance)
    for _, descendant in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
        styleText(descendant)
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.connectLumenTypeface = function(rootObject)
    if not rootObject then return end
    SyllinseRuntime.applyLumenTypeface(rootObject)
    rootObject.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        task.defer(function()
            if descendant and descendant.Parent then
                SyllinseRuntime.applyLumenTypeface(descendant)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

SyllinseRuntime.connectLumenTypeface(Lumen and Lumen._Instance)
SyllinseRuntime.connectLumenTypeface(Lumen and Lumen._Overlay)

SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance = function()
    if SyllinseRuntime.appearanceBatch then return end
    local main, secondary, accent, text, subtext = Theme.Root, Theme.Surface, Theme.Accent, Theme.Text, Theme.Muted
    Theme.ThemedControls = true
    Theme.SurfaceHover = secondary:Lerp(accent, Theme.Style.ButtonHoverTint or 0.12)
    Theme.PanelText, Theme.PanelTitle, Theme.PanelValue = text, text, text
    Theme.PanelSecondary = subtext
    Theme.HeaderIconTint = Theme.ActiveTabIcon or accent
    Theme.HeaderTitleTint = Theme.HeaderIconTint
    Theme.PanelIcon = subtext:Lerp(text, 0.2)
    Theme.IconActive, Theme.IconIdle = Theme.ActiveTabIcon or accent, Theme.TabIcons or subtext
    Theme.IconHover = Theme.IconIdle:Lerp(Theme.IconActive, 0.65)
    Theme.IconSelected = Theme.IconActive
    Theme.Dim = subtext:Lerp(secondary, 0.4)
    Theme.SliderTrack = secondary:Lerp(subtext, 0.35)
    Theme.SliderHead = accent
    Theme.SliderHandle = accent:Lerp(text, 0.35)
    Theme.SliderFillBottom = secondary:Lerp(accent, 0.7)
    Theme.SliderFillTop = accent
    Theme.ToggleOffTrack = secondary:Lerp(subtext, 0.28)
    Theme.ToggleOffThumb = subtext:Lerp(text, 0.25)
    local legacy = (SyllinseRuntime.activeAppearancePreset or "Original") == "Original" and SyllinseRuntime.defaultVisuals
    if legacy then
        local function sameColor(a, b)
            return math.abs(a.R - b.R) < 0.5 / 255 and math.abs(a.G - b.G) < 0.5 / 255
                and math.abs(a.B - b.B) < 0.5 / 255
        end
        -- Preserve the reference Default hierarchy; explicit custom text colors
        -- retain the direct color-picker behavior used by the other themes.
        if sameColor(text, legacy.Text) then
            Theme.PanelText, Theme.PanelTitle, Theme.PanelValue = legacy.PanelText, legacy.PanelTitle, legacy.PanelValue
        end
        if sameColor(subtext, legacy.Muted) then
            Theme.PanelSecondary, Theme.PanelIcon = legacy.PanelSecondary, legacy.PanelIcon
            Theme.SliderTrack, Theme.ToggleOffTrack = legacy.SliderTrack, legacy.ToggleOffTrack
            Theme.ToggleOffThumb, Theme.Dim = legacy.ToggleOffThumb, legacy.Dim
        end
        if sameColor(accent, legacy.Accent) then
            Theme.SliderHead, Theme.SliderHandle = legacy.SliderHead, legacy.SliderHandle
            Theme.SliderFillBottom, Theme.SliderFillTop = legacy.SliderFillBottom, legacy.SliderFillTop
        end
        if sameColor(Theme.IconActive, SyllinseRuntime.appearanceDefaults.ActiveTabIcon) then
            Theme.HeaderIconTint = Theme.Setup.Presets[1].HeaderIconTint or Theme.IconActive
            Theme.HeaderTitleTint = Theme.Setup.Presets[1].HeaderTitleTint or legacy.HeaderTitleTint
        end
    end
    Theme.Divider = Theme.Stroke
    CLOSED_SURFACE = main
    root.BackgroundColor3, sidebar.BackgroundColor3, header.BackgroundColor3 = main, main, main
    searchFrame.BackgroundColor3 = secondary
    searchBox.TextColor3, searchBox.PlaceholderColor3 = text, subtext
    headerTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.HeaderTitleTint
    if searchLens and searchLens.Parent then searchLens.BackgroundColor3 = secondary end
    searchHandle.BackgroundColor3, searchGlyphStroke.Color = Theme.PanelIcon, Theme.PanelIcon
    avatar.BackgroundColor3, avatarStroke.Color = secondary, Theme.Stroke
    if SyllinseRuntime.applyLauncherAppearance then
        SyllinseRuntime.applyLauncherAppearance()
    else
        if launcher and launcher.Parent then launcher.BackgroundColor3 = main end
        if launcherStroke and launcherStroke.Parent then launcherStroke.Color = Theme.Stroke end
        for _, glow in ipairs({ launcherGlow, launcherGlowCyan, launcherGlowViolet }) do
            if glow and glow.Parent then glow.ImageColor3 = accent end
        end
    end
    shellStroke.Color, sideDivider.BackgroundColor3, footerDivider.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Stroke, Theme.Stroke, Theme.Stroke
    local border = searchFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    if border then border.Color = Theme.Stroke end
    Lumen.Theme.Background, Lumen.Theme.Surface, Lumen.Theme.SurfaceAlt = main, secondary, Theme.SurfaceHover
    Lumen.Theme.Accent, Lumen.Theme.AccentDark = accent, secondary:Lerp(accent, 0.54)
    Lumen.Theme.SliderFillBottom, Lumen.Theme.SliderFillTop = Theme.SliderFillBottom, Theme.SliderFillTop
    Lumen.Theme.Border, Lumen.Theme.SectionBorder = Theme.Stroke, Theme.Stroke
    Lumen.Theme.Text, Lumen.Theme.TextDim = Theme.PanelText, Theme.PanelSecondary
    Lumen.Theme.SyllinsePanelHeader = getPanelHeaderColor()
    Lumen.ApplyTheme()
    SyllinseRuntime.syncReptillianTheme()
    SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)
    SyllinseRuntime.applyFieldStyles()
    if SyllinseRuntime.refreshNativeControlPopups then SyllinseRuntime.refreshNativeControlPopups() end
    for _, hosts in pairs(lumenHosts) do
        if hosts.Shared then SyllinseRuntime.stylePageScrollbar(hosts.Shared) end
    end
    for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
        local selected = nav.Name == activeName
        nav.Button.BackgroundColor3 = main
        local iconColor = selected and Theme.IconActive or (nav.Hovering and Theme.IconHover or Theme.IconIdle)
        if nav.Icon then nav.Icon.ImageColor3 = iconColor end
        local label = terminologyLabels[nav.Name]
        if label then label.TextColor3 = iconColor end
        setFallbackEmphasis(nav.Button, selected, nav.Hovering)
        if nav.Gear then setGearIconColor(nav.Gear, iconColor) end
    end
    updateHeaderIcon(activeName)
    if headerGear then setGearIconColor(headerGear, Theme.HeaderIconTint) end
    SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_TITLE_COLORS.Success = text
    SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_TITLE_COLORS.Info = text
    SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_TITLE_COLORS.Warning = text
    SyllinseRuntime.NOTIFICATION_TITLE_COLORS.Error = text
end
SyllinseRuntime.applyWindowColor = function(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Root = color
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelColor = function(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Surface = color
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.applyTextColor = function(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Text = color
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.applyAccentColor = function(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Accent = color
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.applyStrokeColor = function(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Stroke = color
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.applySubtextColor = function(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Muted = color
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.applyTabIconColor = function(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.TabIcons = color
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.applyActiveTabIconColor = function(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.ActiveTabIcon = color
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparency = 0
SyllinseRuntime.applyModuleTransparency = function(value)
    value = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0, 0, 100)
    SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparency = value / 100
    if SyllinseRuntime.onModuleTransparencyChanged then SyllinseRuntime.onModuleTransparencyChanged(value) end
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
end
SyllinseRuntime.panelDividerStyle = "Filled"
SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelDivider = function(frame, transparency)
    local cap = frame:FindFirstChild("Header")
    if not cap then return end
    local lineStyle = SyllinseRuntime.panelDividerStyle == "Line"
    cap.BackgroundColor3 = getPanelHeaderColor()
    cap.BackgroundTransparency = lineStyle and 1 or transparency
    cap.BorderSizePixel = 0
    for _, child in ipairs(cap:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("UIStroke") then child.Enabled = false end
    end
    local divider = frame:FindFirstChild("SyllinsePanelHeaderDivider")
    if not divider then
        divider = Instance.new("Frame")
        divider.Name = "SyllinsePanelHeaderDivider"
        divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
        divider.Active = false
        divider.Parent = frame
    end
    divider.Visible = lineStyle
    divider.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Stroke
    divider.BackgroundTransparency = Theme.Style.DividerTransparency or 0
    divider.ZIndex = cap.ZIndex + 1
    local function alignDivider()
        if not divider.Parent then return end
        divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 1)
        divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, cap.Position.Y.Scale + cap.Size.Y.Scale, cap.Position.Y.Offset + cap.Size.Y.Offset - 1)
    end
    alignDivider()
    if not frame:GetAttribute("SyllinseDividerLayoutBound") then
        frame:SetAttribute("SyllinseDividerLayoutBound", true)
        frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(alignDivider)
        cap:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(alignDivider)
    end
end

SyllinseRuntime.initializeAppearance = function()
    SyllinseRuntime.defaultVisuals = table.clone(Theme)
    SyllinseRuntime.appearanceDefaults = {
        Root = Theme.Root; Surface = Theme.Surface; Accent = Theme.Accent; Stroke = Theme.Stroke;
        Text = Theme.Text; Subtext = Theme.Muted; ModuleTransparency = 0; PanelDivider = "Filled";
        TabIcons = Theme.TabIcons or Theme.Muted; ActiveTabIcon = Theme.ActiveTabIcon or Theme.Accent;
    }
    for _, preset in ipairs(Theme.Setup.Presets) do
        if preset.Id == "Original" and not preset.Name then preset.Name = "Default" end
    end
    local names, ids = {}, {}
    for _, preset in ipairs(Theme.Setup.Presets) do
        local label = preset.Name or preset.Id
        names[#names + 1], ids[label] = label, preset.Id
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.appearanceDropdown = SyllinseRuntime.addReptillianDropdown(SyllinseRuntime.settingsRight, {
        Name = "Themes"; Options = names; Value = "Default"; Search = true;
        Callback = function(name)
            if not SyllinseRuntime.appearanceSyncing and SyllinseRuntime.selectTheme and ids[name] then
                SyllinseRuntime.selectTheme(ids[name])
            end
        end;
    })
    for _, item in ipairs({
        { "Main Color", "ThemeWindow", Theme.Root, SyllinseRuntime.applyWindowColor },
        { "Secondary Color", "ThemePanels", Theme.Surface, SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelColor },
        { "Accent", "ThemeAccent", Theme.Accent, SyllinseRuntime.applyAccentColor },
        { "Stroke", "ThemeStroke", Theme.Stroke, SyllinseRuntime.applyStrokeColor },
        { "Txt", "ThemeText", Theme.Text, SyllinseRuntime.applyTextColor },
        { "Subtext", "ThemeSubtext", Theme.Muted, SyllinseRuntime.applySubtextColor },
        { "Tab Icons", "ThemeTabIcons", Theme.TabIcons or Theme.Muted, SyllinseRuntime.applyTabIconColor },
        { "Active Tab Icon", "ThemeActiveTabIcon", Theme.ActiveTabIcon or Theme.Accent, SyllinseRuntime.applyActiveTabIconColor },
    }) do
        local callback = item[4]
        SyllinseRuntime.settingsRight:Label({ Text = item[1] }):Colorpicker({
            Title = item[1]; Color = item[3]; Flag = item[2]; Callback = function(color) callback(color) end;
        })
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparencyControl = SyllinseRuntime.addNumericSlider(SyllinseRuntime.settingsRight, {
        Name = "Module Transparency"; Suffix = "%"; Min = 0; Max = 100; Value = 0; Increment = 1;
        Flag = "ThemeModuleTransparency"; Callback = SyllinseRuntime.applyModuleTransparency;
    })
    SyllinseRuntime.panelDividerControl = SyllinseRuntime.addReptillianDropdown(SyllinseRuntime.settingsRight, {
        Name = "Panel Divider"; Options = { "Filled", "Line" }; Value = "Filled"; Flag = "ThemePanelDivider";
        Callback = function(value)
            SyllinseRuntime.panelDividerStyle = value == "Line" and "Line" or "Filled"
            SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
        end;
    })
    SyllinseRuntime.settingsRight:Button({ Name = "Restore Default Theme"; Callback = function()
        if SyllinseRuntime.selectTheme then SyllinseRuntime.selectTheme("Original") end
        SyllinseRuntime.panelDividerControl.Set("Filled")
    end; })
    local customSection = SyllinseRuntime.settingsCustomThemes
    SyllinseRuntime.customThemesDropdown = SyllinseRuntime.addReptillianDropdown(customSection, {
        Name = "Saved Themes"; Options = { "None" }; Value = "None"; Search = true;
        Callback = function(name)
            if not SyllinseRuntime.customThemeSyncing and SyllinseRuntime.selectCustomTheme then
                SyllinseRuntime.selectCustomTheme(name)
            end
        end;
    })
    SyllinseRuntime.themeNameInput = customSection:Input({
        Name = "Theme name"; Value = ""; Placeholder = "e.g. Midnight blue"; Callback = function() end;
    })
    customSection:Button({ Name = "Save Theme & Background"; Callback = function()
        if SyllinseRuntime.saveCustomTheme then SyllinseRuntime.saveCustomTheme() end
    end; })
    customSection:Button({ Name = "Delete Selected Theme"; Callback = function()
        if SyllinseRuntime.deleteCustomTheme then SyllinseRuntime.deleteCustomTheme() end
    end; })
end
SyllinseRuntime.initializeAppearance()
SyllinseRuntime.initializeAppearance = nil


-- Native star particles: no media files, conversion jobs or texture uploads.
SyllinseRuntime.createStarfield = function(parent, shell, screenGui, options)
    options = options or {}
    local snowflakes = options.Snowflakes == true
    local layer = Instance.new("Frame")
    layer.Name = snowflakes and "SyllinseSnowflakes" or "SyllinseWhiteStars"
    layer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    layer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    layer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    layer.ClipsDescendants = true
    layer.Active = false
    layer.Visible = false
    layer.ZIndex = 5
    layer.Parent = parent
    local state = { Layer = layer, Enabled = false, Opacity = 1, Stars = {}, Sparkles = {}, Particles = {} }
    local random = Random.new(snowflakes and 251225 or 17311)
    local elapsed, render = 0, nil
    local scaleModifier = shell:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
    local connections = {}
    local profiles, profileScale = {}, nil
    local function makePart(owner, width, height)
        local part = Instance.new("Frame")
        part.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        part.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        part.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)
        part.BackgroundColor3 = options.Color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        part.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        part.BorderSizePixel = 0
        part.Active = false
        part.ZIndex = 0
        part.Parent = owner
        return part
    end
    -- Precompute a small antialiased circle mask. Moving it blends neighboring
    -- pixel coverage instead of relying on GuiObject subpixel positioning.
    local function circleProfile(diameter, scale, sparkle)
        local radius = diameter * scale * 0.5
        local flakeProfile = snowflakes and not sparkle
        local segments = {}
        local strokeRadius = math.max(0.38 * scale, 0.3)
        if flakeProfile then
            local tip = math.max(0.1, radius - strokeRadius)
            local function segment(x1, y1, x2, y2)
                local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
                segments[#segments + 1] = { X = x1, Y = y1, DX = dx, DY = dy, LengthSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy }
            end
            for arm = 0, 5 do
                local angle = arm * math.pi / 3 - math.pi / 2
                local x, y = math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle)
                segment(0, 0, x * tip, y * tip)
                local bx, by = x * tip * 0.52, y * tip * 0.52
                for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                    local branch = angle + side * math.pi / 3
                    segment(bx, by, bx + math.cos(branch) * tip * 0.3, by + math.sin(branch) * tip * 0.3)
                end
            end
        end
        local function covered(x, y)
            if not flakeProfile then return x * x + y * y <= radius * radius end
            for _, line in ipairs(segments) do
                local dx, dy = x - line.X, y - line.Y
                local t = math.clamp((dx * line.DX + dy * line.DY) / line.LengthSquared, 0, 1)
                local ex, ey = dx - line.DX * t, dy - line.DY * t
                if ex * ex + ey * ey <= strokeRadius * strokeRadius then return true end
            end
            return false
        end
        local pad = math.max(0, math.ceil(radius + 0.5) - 1)
        local baseSide, side = pad * 2 + 1, pad * 2 + 2
        local mask = {}
        for y = -pad, pad do
            for x = -pad, pad do
                local coverage = 0
                for sy = 0, 7 do
                    for sx = 0, 7 do
                        local dx, dy = x - 0.5 + (sx + 0.5) / 8, y - 0.5 + (sy + 0.5) / 8
                        if covered(dx, dy) then coverage += 1 end
                    end
                end
                mask[(y + pad) * baseSide + x + pad + 1] = coverage / 64
            end
        end
        local function sample(x, y)
            if x < -pad or x > pad or y < -pad or y > pad then return 0 end
            return mask[(y + pad) * baseSide + x + pad + 1]
        end
        local profile = { Side = side, Pad = pad, Cells = {} }
        for y = 0, side - 1 do
            for x = 0, side - 1 do
                local ix, iy = x - pad, y - pad
                local cell = {
                    X = x, Y = y,
                    A = sample(ix, iy), B = sample(ix - 1, iy),
                    C = sample(ix, iy - 1), D = sample(ix - 1, iy - 1),
                }
                if not flakeProfile or cell.A + cell.B + cell.C + cell.D > 0 then
                    profile.Cells[#profile.Cells + 1] = cell
                end
            end
        end
        return profile
    end
    local function setProfile(star, profile)
        for _, pixel in ipairs(star.ActivePixels) do pixel.Node.Visible = false end
        table.clear(star.ActivePixels)
        for _, cell in ipairs(profile.Cells) do
            local key = cell.Y * 64 + cell.X
            local pixel = star.Pixels[key]
            if not pixel then
                local node = makePart(star.Point, 1, 1)
                node.Name = star.Sparkle and "SparkleCoveragePixel" or (snowflakes and "SnowflakeCoveragePixel" or "StarCoveragePixel")
                pixel = { Node = node }
                star.Pixels[key] = pixel
            end
            pixel.Node.Position = UDim2.fromScale(cell.X / profile.Side, cell.Y / profile.Side)
            pixel.Node.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 / profile.Side, 1 / profile.Side)
            pixel.Node.Visible = true
            pixel.Weights = cell
            star.ActivePixels[#star.ActivePixels + 1] = pixel
        end
        star.Profile = profile
    end
    local function populate()
        if #state.Stars > 0 then return end
        for index = 1, snowflakes and 22 or 54 do
            local size = snowflakes and (index % 7 == 0 and 10 or (index % 3 == 0 and 8 or 6))
                or (index % 11 == 0 and 4 or (index % 5 == 0 and 3 or (index % 2 == 0 and 2 or 1)))
            local point = makePart(layer, size, size)
            point.Name = snowflakes and "Snowflake" or "StarDot"
            local particle = {
                Point = point,
                X = random:NextNumber(), Y = random:NextNumber(),
                Drift = random:NextNumber(0.0008, snowflakes and 0.003 or 0.0028),
                Rise = snowflakes and -random:NextNumber(0.012, 0.025) or random:NextNumber(0.003, 0.008),
                Brightness = random:NextNumber(snowflakes and 0.3 or 0.4, snowflakes and 0.7 or 0.95),
                Radius = size * 0.5, Diameter = size,
                Pixels = {}, ActivePixels = {},
            }
            state.Stars[#state.Stars + 1] = particle
            state.Particles[#state.Particles + 1] = particle
        end
        if options.Sparkles then
            for index = 1, 4 do
                local size = index % 2 == 0 and 2 or 1
                local point = makePart(layer, size, size)
                point.Name = "WinterSparkle"
                local sparkle = {
                    Point = point, Sparkle = true,
                    X = random:NextNumber(0.06, 0.94), Y = random:NextNumber(0.06, 0.94),
                    Drift = 0, Rise = 0, Brightness = 0.6,
                    Radius = size * 0.5, Diameter = size,
                    Period = random:NextNumber(7, 12), Phase = random:NextNumber(),
                    Pixels = {}, ActivePixels = {},
                }
                state.Sparkles[#state.Sparkles + 1] = sparkle
                state.Particles[#state.Particles + 1] = sparkle
            end
        end
    end
    local function draw()
        local scale = math.max(scaleModifier and scaleModifier.Scale or 1, 0.001)
        local width, height = layer.AbsoluteSize.X / scale, layer.AbsoluteSize.Y / scale
        if width <= 0 or height <= 0 then return end
        if profileScale ~= scale then
            profileScale = scale
            table.clear(profiles)
        end
        local absoluteX, absoluteY = layer.AbsolutePosition.X, layer.AbsolutePosition.Y
        local absoluteWidth, absoluteHeight = layer.AbsoluteSize.X, layer.AbsoluteSize.Y
        local radius = math.clamp(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Window, 0, math.min(width, height) * 0.5)
        local halfWidth, halfHeight = width * 0.5, height * 0.5
        for _, star in ipairs(state.Particles) do
            local x, y = (star.X + elapsed * star.Drift) % 1, (star.Y - elapsed * star.Rise) % 1
            star.RenderX, star.RenderY = x, y
            local key = star.Sparkle and ("sparkle:" .. star.Diameter) or star.Diameter
            local profile = profiles[key]
            if not profile then
                profile = circleProfile(star.Diameter, scale, star.Sparkle)
                profiles[key] = profile
            end
            if star.Profile ~= profile then setProfile(star, profile) end
            local px, py = absoluteX + x * absoluteWidth - 0.5, absoluteY + y * absoluteHeight - 0.5
            local baseX, baseY = math.floor(px), math.floor(py)
            local fx, fy = px - baseX, py - baseY
            local originX, originY = baseX - profile.Pad, baseY - profile.Pad
            if star.PixelX ~= originX or star.PixelY ~= originY or star.DrawLeft ~= absoluteX or star.DrawTop ~= absoluteY
                or star.DrawWidth ~= absoluteWidth or star.DrawHeight ~= absoluteHeight then
                star.Point.Position = UDim2.fromScale((originX - absoluteX) / absoluteWidth, (originY - absoluteY) / absoluteHeight)
            end
            if star.DrawSide ~= profile.Side or star.DrawWidth ~= absoluteWidth or star.DrawHeight ~= absoluteHeight then
                star.Point.Size = UDim2.fromScale(profile.Side / absoluteWidth, profile.Side / absoluteHeight)
            end
            star.PixelX, star.PixelY = originX, originY
            star.DrawLeft, star.DrawTop = absoluteX, absoluteY
            star.DrawWidth, star.DrawHeight, star.DrawSide = absoluteWidth, absoluteHeight, profile.Side
            -- Distance inside the rounded shell, including each dot's full radius.
            -- Fade before the boundary so wrapping never pops across an edge.
            local qx = math.abs(x * width - halfWidth) - (halfWidth - radius)
            local qy = math.abs(y * height - halfHeight) - (halfHeight - radius)
            local dx, dy = math.max(qx, 0), math.max(qy, 0)
            local clearance = radius - math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) - math.min(math.max(qx, qy), 0)
            local fade = math.clamp((clearance - star.Radius - 3 / scale) / 12, 0, 1)
            fade = fade * fade * (3 - 2 * fade)
            local alpha = star.Brightness * state.Opacity * fade
            if star.Sparkle then
                -- One slow, smooth glint followed by a longer quiet interval.
                local phase = (elapsed / star.Period + star.Phase) % 1
                alpha *= phase < 0.25 and math.sin(math.pi * phase / 0.25) ^ 2 or 0
            end
            star.Alpha = alpha
            star.Point.Visible = alpha > 0.001
            if star.Point.Visible then
                local wa, wb, wc, wd = (1 - fx) * (1 - fy), fx * (1 - fy), (1 - fx) * fy, fx * fy
                for _, pixel in ipairs(star.ActivePixels) do
                    local weights = pixel.Weights
                    local coverage = weights.A * wa + weights.B * wb + weights.C * wc + weights.D * wd
                    -- Blend light energy so crossing pixels does not dim the dot
                    -- or pull its apparent center toward the brighter pixel.
                    local opacity = math.clamp(coverage, 0, 1) ^ (1 / 2.2) * alpha
                    if not pixel.Alpha or math.abs(pixel.Alpha - opacity) > 0.00000001 then
                        pixel.Node.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - opacity
                        pixel.Alpha = opacity
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function sync()
        local visible = not state.Destroyed and state.Enabled and state.Opacity > 0
            and shell.Visible and screenGui.Enabled and parent.Visible and layer.Parent ~= nil
        if visible then
            populate()
            layer.Visible = true
            draw()
            if not render then
                render = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
                    local step = math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)
                    elapsed += step
                    draw()
                    if options.Mist then options.Mist:Step(step) end
                end)
            end
        else
            layer.Visible = false
            if render then render:Disconnect(); render = nil end
        end
    end
    function state:SetEnabled(enabled)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Enabled = enabled == true
        if options.Mist then options.Mist:SetEnabled(self.Enabled) end
        sync()
    end
    function state:SetOpacity(opacity)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Opacity = math.clamp(tonumber(opacity) or 1, 0, 1)
        if options.Mist then options.Mist:SetOpacity(self.Opacity) end
        sync()
    end
    for _, pair in ipairs({ { shell, "Visible" }, { screenGui, "Enabled" }, { parent, "Visible" } }) do
        connections[#connections + 1] = pair[1]:GetPropertyChangedSignal(pair[2]):Connect(sync)
    end
    layer.Destroying:Connect(function()
        state.Destroyed = true
        if render then render:Disconnect(); render = nil end
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(state.Stars)
        table.clear(state.Sparkles)
        table.clear(state.Particles)
        if options.Mist then options.Mist:Destroy() end
    end)
    return state
end

-- Full-window haze stays still; only its brightness changes on the snowfall clock.
-- Native rounded surfaces avoid image-loading failures and texture seams.
SyllinseRuntime.createWinterMist = function(parent, shell, screenGui)
    local layer = Instance.new("Frame")
    layer.Name = "SyllinseWinterMist"
    layer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    layer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    layer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    layer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    layer.ClipsDescendants = true
    layer.Active = false
    layer.Visible = false
    layer.ZIndex = 3
    layer.Parent = parent
    local state = { Layer = layer, Enabled = false, Opacity = 1, Clouds = {}, Elapsed = 0 }
    local connections = {}
    local scaleModifier = shell:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
    local function populate()
        if #state.Clouds > 0 then return end
        for index, shape in ipairs({
            { Alpha = 0.08, Speed = 0.23, Rotation = 90,
                Mask = { { 0, 0.35 }, { 0.35, 0.12 }, { 0.70, 0.02 }, { 1, 0.20 } } },
            { Alpha = 0.035, Speed = 0.19, Rotation = -18,
                Mask = { { 0, 1 }, { 0.18, 0.78 }, { 0.47, 0 }, { 0.80, 0.76 }, { 1, 1 } } },
            { Alpha = 0.025, Speed = 0.16, Rotation = 28,
                Mask = { { 0, 1 }, { 0.28, 0.80 }, { 0.72, 0.15 }, { 1, 1 } } },
        }) do
            local surface = Instance.new("Frame")
            surface.Name = "WinterHazeGlow"
            surface.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            surface.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            surface.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(164, 189, 213)
            surface.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            surface.BorderSizePixel = 0
            surface.Active = false
            surface.ZIndex = 0
            surface.Parent = layer
            local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            gradient.Name = "WinterHazeGradient"
            gradient.Rotation = shape.Rotation
            gradient.Offset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            local points = {}
            for _, point in ipairs(shape.Mask) do points[#points + 1] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(point[1], point[2]) end
            gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(points)
            gradient.Parent = surface
            state.Clouds[index] = { Surface = surface, Gradient = gradient, Shape = shape, Phase = (index - 1) * 2.1 }
        end
    end
    local function draw()
        local scale = math.max(scaleModifier and scaleModifier.Scale or 1, 0.001)
        local width = shell.Size.X.Scale == 0 and shell.Size.X.Offset or shell.AbsoluteSize.X / scale
        local height = shell.Size.Y.Scale == 0 and shell.Size.Y.Offset or shell.AbsoluteSize.Y / scale
        local radius = math.clamp(SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Window, 0, math.min(width, height) * 0.5)
        if state.Radius ~= radius then
            state.Radius = radius
            for _, cloud in ipairs(state.Clouds) do setManualCorner(cloud.Surface, radius) end
        end
        for _, cloud in ipairs(state.Clouds) do
            local glow = 0.9 + 0.1 * math.sin(state.Elapsed * cloud.Shape.Speed + cloud.Phase)
            local alpha = cloud.Shape.Alpha * glow * state.Opacity
            cloud.Alpha = alpha
            if not cloud.DrawnAlpha or math.abs(cloud.DrawnAlpha - alpha) >= 0.00001 then
                cloud.Surface.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - alpha
                cloud.DrawnAlpha = alpha
            end
        end
    end
    local function sync()
        if state.Destroyed then return end
        local visible = state.Enabled and state.Opacity > 0 and shell.Visible and screenGui.Enabled
            and parent.Visible and layer.Parent ~= nil
        layer.Visible = visible
        if visible then populate(); draw() end
    end
    function state:SetEnabled(enabled)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Enabled = enabled == true
        sync()
    end
    function state:SetOpacity(opacity)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Opacity = math.clamp(tonumber(opacity) or 1, 0, 1)
        sync()
    end
    function state:Step(delta)
        if self.Destroyed or not layer.Visible then return end
        self.Elapsed += math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)
        draw()
    end
    function state:Destroy()
        if not self.Destroyed then layer:Destroy() end
    end
    for _, pair in ipairs({ { shell, "Visible" }, { shell, "Size" }, { screenGui, "Enabled" }, { parent, "Visible" } }) do
        connections[#connections + 1] = pair[1]:GetPropertyChangedSignal(pair[2]):Connect(sync)
    end
    if scaleModifier then connections[#connections + 1] = scaleModifier:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"):Connect(sync) end
    layer.Destroying:Connect(function()
        state.Destroyed = true
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(state.Clouds)
    end)
    return state
end

-- The moving highlight belongs to the shell's own outer border.
SyllinseRuntime.createShellSheen = function(parent, shell, screenGui)
    local layer = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    layer.Name = "SyllinseShellSheen"
    layer.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    layer.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    layer.Thickness = 1
    layer.Transparency = 0.2
    layer.Enabled = false
    pcall(function() layer.BorderStrokePosition = Enum.BorderStrokePosition.Outer end)
    pcall(function() layer.ZIndex = 2 end)
    layer.Parent = shell
    local state = { Layer = layer, Stroke = layer, Enabled = false, Gradients = {} }
    local elapsed, pending, render = 0, 0, nil
    local connections = {}
    local function populate()
        if #state.Gradients > 0 then return end
        local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        gradient.Rotation = -30 -- 60-degree highlight, moving right to left.
        gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
        gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.65),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.65),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        })
        gradient.Parent = layer
        state.Gradients[1] = gradient
    end
    local function draw()
        -- Keep the slow travel speed and shorten the offscreen wait between passes.
        local offset = 1.1 - ((elapsed * (3.6 / 8)) % 2.2)
        state.Gradients[1].Offset = Vector2.new(offset, 0)
    end
    local function sync()
        local visible = not state.Destroyed and state.Enabled and shell.Visible and screenGui.Enabled
            and parent.Visible and parent.Parent ~= nil and layer.Parent ~= nil
        layer.Enabled = visible
        if visible then
            populate()
            draw()
            if not render then
                render = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
                    pending += math.min(delta, 0.1)
                    if pending < 1 / 30 then return end
                    elapsed += pending
                    pending = 0
                    draw()
                end)
            end
        else
            if render then render:Disconnect(); render = nil end
            pending = 0
        end
    end
    function state:SetEnabled(enabled, color)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Enabled = enabled == true
        layer.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        sync()
    end
    for _, pair in ipairs({ { shell, "Visible" }, { screenGui, "Enabled" }, { parent, "Visible" }, { parent, "Parent" } }) do
        connections[#connections + 1] = pair[1]:GetPropertyChangedSignal(pair[2]):Connect(sync)
    end
    connections[#connections + 1] = parent.Destroying:Connect(function() layer:Destroy() end)
    layer.Destroying:Connect(function()
        state.Destroyed = true
        if render then render:Disconnect(); render = nil end
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(state.Gradients)
    end)
    return state
end

-- A continuous rounded outline with a faint outer glow and rotating icy highlights.
-- Native strokes follow the shell itself; they never cover the window's contents.
SyllinseRuntime.createChristmasShine = function(parent, shell, screenGui)
    local state = { Enabled = false, Strokes = {}, Gradients = {}, Elapsed = 0, Speed = 14, Rotation = -90 }
    local connections, render = {}, nil
    local tint = Color3.fromRGB(203, 225, 244)
    for index, style in ipairs({ { 6, 0.99 }, { 3, 0.97 }, { 1.1, 0.68 } }) do
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Name = "SyllinseChristmasOutline"
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        stroke.Thickness, stroke.Transparency = style[1], style[2]
        stroke.Color = tint
        stroke.Enabled = false
        pcall(function() stroke.BorderStrokePosition = Enum.BorderStrokePosition.Outer end)
        pcall(function() stroke.ZIndex = index + 1 end)
        stroke.Parent = shell
        state.Strokes[#state.Strokes + 1] = stroke
    end
    state.Layer = state.Strokes[#state.Strokes]
    local function populate()
        if #state.Gradients > 0 then return end
        for _, stroke in ipairs(state.Strokes) do
            local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(183, 205, 224))
            gradient.Offset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            -- Keep a brightness floor along the whole outline while the highlight turns.
            gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.65),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.18, 0.50),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.42, 0.18),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, 0.05),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.58, 0.18),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.82, 0.50),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.65),
            })
            gradient.Parent = stroke
            state.Gradients[#state.Gradients + 1] = gradient
        end
    end
    local function draw()
        state.Rotation = -90 + (state.Elapsed * state.Speed) % 360
        for _, gradient in ipairs(state.Gradients) do gradient.Rotation = state.Rotation end
    end
    local function sync()
        if state.Destroyed then return end
        local visible = state.Enabled and shell.Visible and screenGui.Enabled and parent.Visible
            and parent.Parent ~= nil and shell.Parent ~= nil
        for _, stroke in ipairs(state.Strokes) do stroke.Enabled, stroke.Color = visible, tint end
        if visible then
            populate()
            draw()
            if not render then
                render = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
                    state.Elapsed += math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)
                    draw()
                end)
            end
        elseif render then
            render:Disconnect()
            render = nil
        end
    end
    function state:SetEnabled(enabled, color)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Enabled = enabled == true
        tint = color or tint
        sync()
    end
    function state:Destroy(skip)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Destroyed = true
        if render then render:Disconnect(); render = nil end
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        for _, stroke in ipairs(self.Strokes) do if stroke ~= skip then stroke:Destroy() end end
        table.clear(self.Strokes)
        table.clear(self.Gradients)
    end
    for _, pair in ipairs({ { shell, "Visible" }, { shell, "Parent" }, { screenGui, "Enabled" }, { parent, "Visible" }, { parent, "Parent" } }) do
        connections[#connections + 1] = pair[1]:GetPropertyChangedSignal(pair[2]):Connect(sync)
    end
    for _, owner in ipairs({ parent, shell, screenGui }) do
        connections[#connections + 1] = owner.Destroying:Connect(function() state:Destroy() end)
    end
    state.Layer.Destroying:Connect(function() state:Destroy(state.Layer) end)
    return state
end

-- Built-in theme backdrops share the existing media viewport.
SyllinseRuntime.initializeThemes = function()
    local backgroundStorage = { Root = "Syllinse/Backgrounds"; }
    function backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        assert(type(isfolder) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function", "This executor cannot create background folders")
        for _, folder in ipairs({ "Syllinse", "Syllinse/Backgrounds", "Syllinse/Backgrounds/Cache", "Syllinse/Backgrounds/Saved", "Syllinse/Backgrounds/Prepared" }) do
            if not isfolder(folder) then makefolder(folder) end
        end
    end
    function backgroundStorage.MoveFile(source, destination)
        local prefix = backgroundStorage.Root .. "/"
        assert(destination:sub(1, #prefix) == prefix and not destination:find("/../", 1, true), "Invalid background destination")
        if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" or type(writefile) ~= "function" then return false end
        local ok, moved = pcall(function()
            if not isfile(source) then return isfile(destination) end
            local bytes = readfile(source)
            if isfile(destination) then
                if readfile(destination) ~= bytes then return false end
            else
                writefile(destination, bytes)
                assert(readfile(destination) == bytes, "Could not verify moved background file")
            end
            if type(delfile) == "function" then delfile(source) end
            return true
        end)
        return ok and moved
    end
    function backgroundStorage.Cache(filename)
        assert(filename:match("^[%w_.%-]+$") and not filename:find("..", 1, true), "Invalid background cache filename")
        backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        local path = backgroundStorage.Root .. "/Cache/" .. filename
        if type(isfile) == "function" and not isfile(path) and isfile(filename) then
            backgroundStorage.MoveFile(filename, path)
        end
        return path
    end
    function backgroundStorage.MigrateFolder(source, destination, id)
        source = source:gsub("\\", "/"):gsub("/+", "/")
        if type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder(source) or type(listfiles) ~= "function" then return end
        backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        if not isfolder(destination) then makefolder(destination) end
        for i, path in ipairs(listfiles(source)) do
            local normalized = path:gsub("\\", "/"):gsub("/+", "/")
            local parent, name = normalized:match("^(.*)/([^/]+)$")
            if parent and (parent == source or parent:sub(-#source - 1) == "/" .. source)
                and not normalized:find("/../", 1, true) and normalized:sub(1, 3) ~= "../" then
                local target
                local format = name:match("^source%.(png)$") or name:match("^source%.(jpg)$")
                local frame, extension = name:match("^frame%-(%d+)%.(%a+)$")
                if format then target = id .. "-source." .. format
                elseif frame and (extension == "png" or extension == "jpg") then target = id .. "-frame-" .. frame .. "." .. extension
                elseif name == "manifest.json" or name == "original-sheet.png" or name:match("^original%.%a+$") then target = name end
                if target then backgroundStorage.MoveFile(path, destination .. "/" .. target) end
            end
            if i % 4 == 0 then task.wait() end
        end
        if type(delfolder) == "function" and #listfiles(source) == 0 then pcall(delfolder, source) end
    end
    function backgroundStorage.Saved(id)
        assert(id:match("^[%w_-]+$") and #id <= 64, "Invalid saved background")
        backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        local folder = backgroundStorage.Root .. "/Saved/" .. id
        backgroundStorage.MigrateFolder("SyllinseBackgrounds/" .. id, folder, id)
        return folder
    end
    function backgroundStorage.Prepared(name)
        assert(name:match("^[%w_-]+$") and #name <= 64, "Invalid prepared background")
        backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        local folder = backgroundStorage.Root .. "/Prepared/" .. name
        backgroundStorage.MigrateFolder("SyllinseHQ/" .. name, folder, name)
        return folder
    end
    function backgroundStorage.MigrateOldFiles()
        backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        if type(listfiles) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then return end
        local listed, paths = pcall(listfiles, "")
        if not listed then listed, paths = pcall(listfiles, ".") end
        if listed then
            for i, path in ipairs(paths) do
                local name = path:gsub("\\", "/"):match("([^/]+)$")
                if name and name:match("^[%w_.%-]+$") and not name:find("..", 1, true) and isfile(path) then
                    local owned = name:match("^SyllinseThemePhoto%-%w[%w_%-]*%.[a-z]+$")
                        or name:match("^SyllinseClean%-v2%-%w[%w_%-]*%.png$")
                        or name:match("^SyllinseSource%-v1%-%w[%w_%-]*%.[a-z]+$")
                    if owned then backgroundStorage.MoveFile(path, backgroundStorage.Root .. "/Cache/" .. name) end
                end
                if i % 4 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end
        for _, pair in ipairs({ { "SyllinseBackgrounds", "Saved" }, { "SyllinseHQ", "Prepared" } }) do
            if isfolder(pair[1]) then
                for _, path in ipairs(listfiles(pair[1])) do
                    local normalized = path:gsub("\\", "/"):gsub("/+", "/")
                    local parent, id = normalized:match("^(.*)/([^/]+)$")
                    if parent and (parent == pair[1] or parent:sub(-#pair[1] - 1) == "/" .. pair[1])
                        and id:match("^[%w_-]+$") and #id <= 64 and isfolder(path) then
                        backgroundStorage.MigrateFolder(path, backgroundStorage.Root .. "/" .. pair[2] .. "/" .. id, id)
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
                if type(delfolder) == "function" and #listfiles(pair[1]) == 0 then pcall(delfolder, pair[1]) end
            end
        end
    end

    local defaults = table.clone(Theme)
    local presets, idsByName = {}, {}
    for _, preset in ipairs(Theme.Setup.Presets) do
        local id = preset.Id
        local label = preset.Name or id
        assert(type(id) == "string" and id:match("^[%w_-]+$") and not presets[id], "Theme Id must be unique and use letters, numbers, underscores or dashes")
        assert(type(label) == "string" and label ~= "" and not idsByName[label], "Theme Name must be unique and non-empty")
        presets[id] = preset
        idsByName[label] = id
    end
    assert(presets.Original, "Keep the Original theme Id for reset and older configs")
    local section = SyllinseRuntime.pages.Settings:Section({ Name = "Backgrounds"; Side = "Left"; Icon = "image" })
    local description = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    description.Name = "BackgroundDescription"
    description.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    description.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    description.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    description.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
    description.TextSize = 13
    description.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelText
    description.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    description.TextWrapped = true
    description.Text = "Name your background, paste a direct media URL, then import. Saved backgrounds work offline."
    description.LayoutOrder = 0
    description.Parent = section.Content

    local backdrop = Instance.new("Frame")
    backdrop.Name = "SyllinseThemeBackdrop"
    backdrop.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    backdrop.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    backdrop.BorderSizePixel = 0
    backdrop.ZIndex = 0
    backdrop.Visible = false
    backdrop.Parent = root
    setManualCorner(backdrop, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Window)
    local shellSheen = SyllinseRuntime.createShellSheen(backdrop, root, gui)
    local christmasShine = SyllinseRuntime.createChristmasShine(backdrop, root, gui)
    local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    gradient.Rotation = 125
    gradient.Parent = backdrop
    local mediaViewport = Instance.new("Frame")
    mediaViewport.Name = "SyllinseMediaViewport"
    mediaViewport.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    mediaViewport.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    mediaViewport.ClipsDescendants = true
    mediaViewport.ZIndex = 0
    mediaViewport.Parent = backdrop

    local photo = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    photo.Name = "SyllinseThemePhoto"
    photo.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    photo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    photo.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    photo.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Default
    photo.Active = false
    photo.Visible = false
    photo.ZIndex = 0
    photo.Parent = mediaViewport
    setManualCorner(photo, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Window)
    local starfield = SyllinseRuntime.createStarfield(mediaViewport, root, gui)
    local winterMist = SyllinseRuntime.createWinterMist(mediaViewport, root, gui)
    local snowfall = SyllinseRuntime.createStarfield(mediaViewport, root, gui, {
        Snowflakes = true; Sparkles = true; Mist = winterMist; Color = Color3.fromRGB(230, 242, 255);
    })

    local function notifyBackground(message, kind)
        -- Responses to a user action remain visible even when feature toasts are disabled.
        if type(SyllinseRuntime.showNotification) == "function" then
            return SyllinseRuntime.showNotification({ Title = "Backgrounds"; Content = message; Type = kind or "Info"; Duration = 5; })
        end
        return SyllinseRuntime.notify("Backgrounds", message, kind or "Info")
    end

    local currentBackgroundNotice
    local function makeBackgroundLoadingNotice(title)
        if currentBackgroundNotice then currentBackgroundNotice.Dismiss() end
        local toast = SyllinseRuntime.showNotification({
            Title = "Background Status"; Content = "Loading..."; Type = "Info"; Persistent = true; ProgressTweenTime = 2; DrainFromFull = true;
        })
        toast.Update("Loading...", nil, true)
        local connection, dismissed
        local function dismiss()
            if dismissed then return end
            dismissed = true
            if connection then connection:Disconnect() end
            toast.Dismiss()
        end
        connection = gui.Destroying:Connect(dismiss)
        local notice = { Dismiss = dismiss; Update = function(text, fraction, indeterminate)
            if not dismissed then toast.Update("Loading...", fraction, indeterminate) end
        end; Loaded = function()
            if dismissed then return end
            toast.Update("Loaded...", nil, false)
            task.delay(0.8, dismiss)
        end; }
        currentBackgroundNotice = notice
        return notice
    end

    local activeBackgroundLoad
    local backgroundStageRates = {}
    local function beginBackgroundLoad(title, note, silent)
        if activeBackgroundLoad then activeBackgroundLoad:Cancel() end
        local now = os.clock()
        local load = { Started = now; PhaseStarted = now; Label = "Checking media"; Completed = 0; Note = note; }
        local toast = silent and { Update = function() end; Dismiss = function() end; } or makeBackgroundLoadingNotice(title)
        local function recordStage()
            local duration = os.clock() - load.PhaseStarted
            if duration < 0.25 then return end
            local units = load.Completed > 0 and load.Completed or load.Total or 1
            local rate = duration / units
            local previous = backgroundStageRates[load.Label]
            backgroundStageRates[load.Label] = previous and (previous * 0.6 + rate * 0.4) or rate
        end
        local function completeCallback(success, message)
            local callback = load.OnComplete
            load.OnComplete = nil
            if callback then callback(success, message) end
        end
        function load:Update(force)
            if self.Done then return end
            local time = os.clock()
            if not force and self.LastUpdate and time - self.LastUpdate < 0.5 then return end
            self.LastUpdate = time
            local remaining
            if self.EstimateEnds then
                remaining = self.EstimateEnds - time
            elseif self.Total and backgroundStageRates[self.Label] then
                remaining = backgroundStageRates[self.Label] * self.Total - (time - self.PhaseStarted)
            end
            self.RemainingSeconds = remaining and math.max(0, remaining) or nil
            -- Activity sweeps right-to-left for the whole request; only readiness starts the final drain.
            toast.Update("", nil, true)
        end
        function load:Stage(label, total, detail)
            assert(not self.Cancelled, "Background load cancelled")
            if self.Done then return end
            recordStage()
            self.Label = tostring(label)
            self.Detail = detail
            self.Total = type(total) == "number" and total > 0 and total < math.huge and total or nil
            self.Completed, self.PhaseStarted = 0, os.clock()
            self.EstimateEnds = nil
            self:Update(true)
        end
        function load:Step(completed)
            assert(not self.Cancelled, "Background load cancelled")
            if self.Done or type(completed) ~= "number" or completed ~= completed then return end
            local firstProgress = self.Completed == 0 and completed > 0
            local nextCompleted = math.clamp(completed, 0, self.Total or completed)
            if self.Total and nextCompleted > self.Completed then
                local time = os.clock()
                self.EstimateEnds = time + (time - self.PhaseStarted) / nextCompleted * (self.Total - nextCompleted)
            end
            self.Completed = nextCompleted
            self:Update(firstProgress)
        end
        function load:Cancel()
            if self.Done then return end
            self.Done, self.Cancelled = true, true
            toast.Dismiss()
            completeCallback(false, "Cancelled")
        end
        function load:Finish(success, message)
            if self.Done then return end
            if success then recordStage() end
            self.Done = true
            local text = message or (success and (self.SuccessMessage or "Background ready") or "Background could not load")
            if success then
