                and type(meta.Fps) == "number" and meta.Fps > 0
                and type(meta.FrameEnds) == "table" and #meta.FrameEnds == meta.FrameCount
                and meta.FrameEnds[meta.FrameCount] == meta.Duration and type(meta.Chunks) == "table",
                "Invalid source sprite manifest")
            local prior = 0
            for _, ending in ipairs(meta.FrameEnds) do
                assert(type(ending) == "number" and ending > prior, "Invalid source frame timing")
                prior = ending
            end
            local chunkCount = math.ceil(meta.PageCount / meta.PagesPerChunk)
            assert(#meta.Chunks <= chunkCount and (meta.State ~= "ready" or #meta.Chunks == chunkCount),
                "Invalid source sprite batch count")
            for index, chunk in ipairs(meta.Chunks) do
                local first = (index - 1) * meta.PagesPerChunk + 1
                assert(type(chunk) == "table" and chunk.FirstPage == first and type(chunk.Sizes) == "table"
                    and #chunk.Sizes == math.min(meta.PagesPerChunk, meta.PageCount - first + 1)
                    and batchDigest(meta, chunk.Url), "Invalid source sprite batch")
                for _, size in ipairs(chunk.Sizes) do
                    assert(type(size) == "number" and size % 1 == 0 and size >= 57 and size <= 4 * 1024 * 1024,
                        "Invalid source sheet size")
                end
            end
            return meta
        end

        local function cachePath(url)
            return backgroundStorage.Cache("SyllinseSource-v1-" .. mediaUrlHash(api .. "|" .. url) .. ".json")
        end

        local function poll(id)
            local data = response({ Url = jobsUrl .. "/" .. id; Method = "GET"; Timeout = 10; })
            assert(data and data.Id == id, "Source API returned a different job")
            return data.Chunks and validate(data) or data
        end

        local function allPagesCached(meta)
            for _, chunk in ipairs(meta.Chunks) do
                local digest = batchDigest(meta, chunk.Url)
                for i = 1, #chunk.Sizes do
                    if not isfile(pagePath(meta, digest, i)) and not isfile(pagePath(meta, digest, i, true)) then
                        return false
                    end
                end
            end
            return true
        end

        local function cache(meta, url)
            if not meta.Cached and #meta.Chunks == math.ceil(meta.PageCount / meta.PagesPerChunk) then
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    writefile(cachePath(url), http:JSONEncode({ Url = url; Api = api; Meta = meta; }))
                end)
                meta.Cached = ok
            end
        end

        return {
            Read = function(url)
                if api == "" or type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then return nil end
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    local path = cachePath(url)
                    if not isfile(path) then return nil end
                    local entry = http:JSONDecode(readfile(path))
                    assert(entry.Url == url and entry.Api == api, "Source cache URL changed")
                    local meta = validate(entry.Meta)
                    assert(meta.Version == 1 and type(meta.Id) == "string" and #meta.Id == 64
                        and meta.Id:match("^[a-f0-9]+$") and #meta.Chunks == math.ceil(meta.PageCount / meta.PagesPerChunk),
                        "Incomplete source cache")
                    if meta.Chunks[1].Url:sub(1, 1) == "/" and not allPagesCached(meta) then
                        meta = poll(meta.Id)
                        assert(meta.State == "ready", "The server cache is rebuilding")
                    end
                    meta.SourceUrl, meta.Cached = url, true
                    return meta
                end)
                return ok and result or nil
            end;
            Convert = function(url, bytes, load)
                if api == "" then return nil end
                if load then load:Stage("Uploading and converting animation", #bytes, string.format("%.1f MB", #bytes / 1048576)) end
                assert(api:match("^https://[^%s]+$"), "Use the HTTPS API base for SourceApiUrl")
                local options = { Url = jobsUrl; Method = "POST"; Timeout = 235; Body = bytes;
                    Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/octet-stream" }; }
                local meta = response(options)
                assert(not load or not load.Cancelled, "Background load cancelled")
                if not meta then
                    task.wait(1)
                    assert(not load or not load.Cancelled, "Background load cancelled")
                    meta = response(options)
                end
                assert(not load or not load.Cancelled, "Background load cancelled")
                assert(meta, "Source converter is busy")
                local deadline = os.clock() + 110
                while not meta.Chunks or not meta.Chunks[1] do
                    assert(not load or not load.Cancelled, "Background load cancelled")
                    assert(os.clock() < deadline, "The first source sprite batch took too long")
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    meta = poll(meta.Id)
                end
                validate(meta)
                cache(meta, url)
                meta.SourceUrl = url
                return meta
            end;
            Files = function(meta, index, alive, previousPaths)
                local deadline = os.clock() + 60
                while not meta.Chunks[index] and alive() do
                    assert(os.clock() < deadline, "Source sprites stopped arriving")
                    local update = poll(meta.Id)
                    if update.Chunks and #update.Chunks > #meta.Chunks then
                        meta.Chunks, meta.State = update.Chunks, update.State
                        cache(meta, meta.SourceUrl)
                    end
                    if not meta.Chunks[index] then task.wait(0.3) end
                end
                if not alive() then return nil end

                local chunk = meta.Chunks[index]
                local paths, missing, total = {}, {}, 0
                -- Include the published batch identity so files from interrupted jobs cannot mix.
                local digest = batchDigest(meta, chunk.Url)
                assert(digest, "Invalid source sprite batch identity")
                for i, size in ipairs(chunk.Sizes) do
                    local path = pagePath(meta, digest, i)
                    local alternate = pagePath(meta, digest, i, true)
                    if previousPaths then
                        -- A different filename avoids reusing the URI that failed to load.
                        if previousPaths[i] == path then path = alternate end
                    elseif type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(alternate) then
                        path = alternate
                    end
                    paths[i] = path
                    missing[i] = previousPaths ~= nil or type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(path)
                    total += size
                end
                if table.find(missing, true) then
                    local body
                    local url = chunk.Url:sub(1, 1) == "/" and api .. chunk.Url or chunk.Url
                    for attempt = 1, 2 do
                        local ok, reply = pcall(send, { Url = url; Method = "GET"; Timeout = 12; })
                        if not alive() then return nil end
                        if ok and type(reply) == "table" and tonumber(reply.StatusCode or reply.Status) == 200
                            and type(reply.Body) == "string" and #reply.Body == total then
                            body = reply.Body
                            break
                        end
                        if attempt == 1 then task.wait(0.35) end
                        if not alive() then return nil end
                    end
                    assert(body, "Could not download a source sprite batch")
                    local cursor = 1
                    for i, size in ipairs(chunk.Sizes) do
                        if missing[i] then
                            local page = body:sub(cursor, cursor + size - 1)
                            assert(mediaKind(page) == (meta.ImageFormat or "png"), "Invalid source sprite sheet")
                            writefile(paths[i], page)
                        end
                        cursor += size
                    end
                end
                return paths
            end;
        }
    end)()

    local function localFramePath(folder, index, format)
        return string.format("%s/%s-frame-%04d.%s", folder, folder:match("([^/]+)$"), index, format or "png")
    end

    local function localBackground(name, expectedUrl)
        assert(type(readfile) == "function", "Prepared backgrounds require access to the executor workspace")
        local folder = backgroundStorage.Prepared(name)
        local found, json = pcall(readfile, folder .. "/manifest.json")
        assert(found and type(json) == "string", "Prepared background was not found in the executor workspace")
        assert(#json <= 64 * 1024, "Invalid prepared background manifest")
        local meta = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(json)
        assert(type(meta) == "table" and meta.Version == 1 and type(meta.FrameEnds) == "table"
            and #meta.FrameEnds > 0 and #meta.FrameEnds <= 1800, "Invalid prepared background manifest")
        assert(not expectedUrl or meta.Url == expectedUrl, "Background cache URL changed")
        local duration = 0
        for _, ending in ipairs(meta.FrameEnds) do
            assert(type(ending) == "number" and ending > duration and ending <= 60.001, "Invalid prepared frame timing")
            duration = ending
        end
        local firstPath = localFramePath(folder, 1)
        local bytes = readfile(firstPath)
        assert(mediaKind(bytes) == "png", "Prepared backgrounds require PNG frames")
        local width, height = photoDimensions(bytes)
        assert(width <= 970 and height <= 670, "Prepared frames must fit within 970 x 670")
        local count = #meta.FrameEnds
        if count == 1 then
            local register = type(getcustomasset) == "function" and getcustomasset or getsynasset
            assert(type(register) == "function", "This client cannot load local images")
            local asset = register(firstPath)
            assert(type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "", "Could not load the prepared image")
            return { Kind = "photo"; Asset = asset; SavedFile = firstPath; }
        end
        if type(isfile) == "function" then
            for i = 2, count do
                assert(isfile(localFramePath(folder, i)), "Prepared background is missing frame " .. i)
            end
        end
        return { Kind = "animation"; Asset = {
            LocalFolder = folder; PageCount = count; PagesPerChunk = 4;
            Columns = 1; Rows = 1; CellWidth = width; CellHeight = height;
            FrameCount = count; FrameEnds = meta.FrameEnds; Duration = duration; Fps = count / duration;
        }; }
    end

    local function captureGifBackground(name, url, bytes, load)
        if not Theme.Setup.ProcessBackgroundPixels then return nil end
        if #bytes > 8 * 1024 * 1024 or type(isfolder) ~= "function" or type(makefolder) ~= "function" then return nil end
        local timing = gifTimeline(bytes)
        local width, height = timing.Width, timing.Height
        if width > 512 or height > 512 or #timing.FrameEnds > 24 or timing.Duration > 60 then return nil end
        local outWidth, outHeight = proportionalSize(width, height, 970, 670)
        if load then load:Stage("Preparing GIF frames", #timing.FrameEnds) end
        local gif = backgroundGifCodec(bytes)
        local folder = backgroundStorage.Prepared(name)
        if not isfolder(folder) then makefolder(folder) end
        local pixels = buffer.create(width * height * 4)
        local function yieldWork()
            task.wait()
            assert(gui.Parent, "Background was closed")
        end
        for index = 1, #timing.FrameEnds do
            local frame = gif.read_matrix()
            for y = 1, height do
                local row = frame[y]
                for x = 1, width do
                    local color = row[x]
                    local pixel = color >= 0 and bit32.bor(0xff000000, bit32.rshift(bit32.byteswap(color), 8)) or 0
                    buffer.writeu32(pixels, ((y - 1) * width + x - 1) * 4, pixel)
                end
            end
            local cleaned = cleanFramePixels(pixels, width, height, outWidth, outHeight, yieldWork)
            local png = backgroundPngCodec.encode(cleaned, { width = outWidth; height = outHeight; })
            writefile(localFramePath(folder, index), buffer.tostring(png))
            if load then load:Step(index) end
            if index < #timing.FrameEnds then assert(gif.next_image(), "GIF ended early") end
            yieldWork()
        end
        local manifest = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({ Version = 1; Url = url; FrameEnds = timing.FrameEnds; })
        writefile(folder .. "/manifest.json", manifest)
        return localBackground(name, url)
    end

    local function readSavedBackground(id)
        assert(id:match("^[%w_-]+$") and #id <= 64, "Invalid saved background")
        local folder = backgroundStorage.Saved(id)
        local json = readfile(folder .. "/manifest.json")
        assert(#json <= 512 * 1024, "Saved background manifest is too large")
        local meta = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(json)
        assert(type(meta) == "table" and meta.Version == 1, "Invalid saved background")
        if meta.Kind == "video" then
            assert(type(meta.Asset) == "string" and meta.Asset:match("^rbxassetid://[1-9]%d*$"), "Invalid saved video")
            return { Kind = "video"; Asset = meta.Asset; }
        end
        assert(meta.Format == "png" or meta.Format == "jpg", "Invalid saved image format")
        if meta.Kind == "photo" then
            local path = folder .. "/" .. id .. "-source." .. meta.Format
            local bytes = readfile(path)
            assert(mediaKind(bytes) == meta.Format, "The saved image is damaged")
            local width, height = photoDimensions(bytes)
            local register = type(getcustomasset) == "function" and getcustomasset or getsynasset
            local asset = register(path)
            local ok, cleaned = pcall(cleanBackgroundAsset, id, asset, bytes,
                width, height, 1, 1, function() return gui.Parent ~= nil end)
            if ok and cleaned then asset = cleaned end
            return { Kind = "photo"; Asset = asset; SavedFile = path; }
        end
        assert(meta.Kind == "animation", "Invalid saved background type")
        for _, key in ipairs({ "CellWidth", "CellHeight", "Columns", "Rows", "FrameCount", "PageCount" }) do
            local number = meta[key]
            assert(type(number) == "number" and number % 1 == 0 and number >= 1, "Invalid saved sprite dimensions")
        end
        assert(meta.CellWidth * meta.Columns <= 4096 and meta.CellHeight * meta.Rows <= 4096
            and meta.FrameCount <= 3600 and meta.PageCount == math.ceil(meta.FrameCount / (meta.Columns * meta.Rows))
            and type(meta.Fps) == "number" and meta.Fps > 0 and meta.Fps <= 120, "Invalid saved animation")
        if meta.FrameEnds then
            assert(type(meta.FrameEnds) == "table" and #meta.FrameEnds == meta.FrameCount, "Invalid saved frame timing")
            local previous = 0
            for _, ending in ipairs(meta.FrameEnds) do
                assert(type(ending) == "number" and ending > previous and ending <= 60.001, "Invalid saved frame timing")
                previous = ending
            end
            meta.Duration = previous
        else
            meta.Duration = meta.FrameCount / meta.Fps
        end
        for page = 1, meta.PageCount do
            if type(isfile) == "function" then
                assert(isfile(localFramePath(folder, page, meta.Format)), "Saved background is missing page " .. page)
            end
        end
        meta.LocalFolder, meta.LocalPageFormat, meta.PagesPerChunk = folder, meta.Format, 4
        return { Kind = "animation"; Asset = meta; }
    end

    local function saveBackgroundFiles(url, result, id, alive, load)
        alive = alive or function() return gui.Parent ~= nil end
        assert(alive(), "Import cancelled")
        assert(type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function"
            and type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function", "This executor cannot save backgrounds")
        backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        local folder = backgroundStorage.Saved(id)
        makefolder(folder)
        local meta = { Version = 1; Kind = result.Kind; }
        local category = result.Kind == "photo" and "Images" or result.Kind == "video" and "Videos" or "Animations"
        local mediaUrl, sheetUrl = splitMediaLinks(url)
        local sourceBytes
        if mediaUrl:match("^https://") then
            sourceBytes = result.SourceUrl == mediaUrl and result.SourceBytes or nil
            if not sourceBytes then
                local kind, asset, bytes = loadMedia("Saved-" .. id, mediaUrl, false)
                sourceBytes = kind == "animation" and asset or bytes
            end
            assert(alive(), "Import cancelled")
            local format = mediaKind(sourceBytes)
            if format == "animation" then
                format = sourceBytes:sub(1, 3) == "GIF" and "gif" or sourceBytes:sub(5, 8) == "ftyp" and "mp4" or "webm"
                if format ~= "gif" then category = "Videos" end
            end
            if result.Kind ~= "photo" then writefile(folder .. "/original." .. format, sourceBytes) end
            if sheetUrl then
                local _, _, sheet = loadMedia("SavedSheet-" .. id, sheetUrl, true)
                writefile(folder .. "/original-sheet.png", sheet)
            end
        end
        if result.Kind == "video" then
            meta.Asset = result.Asset
        elseif result.Kind == "photo" then
            local bytes = result.SavedFile and readfile(result.SavedFile) or sourceBytes
            assert(bytes, "Could not find the downloaded image")
            meta.Format = mediaKind(bytes)
            assert(meta.Format == "png" or meta.Format == "jpg", "Invalid downloaded image")
            writefile(folder .. "/" .. id .. "-source." .. meta.Format, bytes)
        else
            local spec = result.Asset
            for _, key in ipairs({ "CellWidth", "CellHeight", "Columns", "Rows", "FrameCount", "Fps", "FrameEnds" }) do
                meta[key] = spec[key]
            end
            meta.PageCount = spec.PageCount or #spec.Pages
            if load then load:Stage("Saving animation sheets", meta.PageCount) end
            local downloaded, total = {}, 0
            for page = 1, meta.PageCount do
                assert(alive(), "Import cancelled")
                local path
                if spec.LocalFolder then
                    path = localFramePath(spec.LocalFolder, page, spec.LocalPageFormat)
                elseif spec.SourceUrl then
                    local chunk = math.floor((page - 1) / spec.PagesPerChunk) + 1
                    if not downloaded[chunk] then downloaded[chunk] = sourceQuality.Files(spec, chunk, alive) end
                    assert(alive(), "Import cancelled")
                    path = downloaded[chunk][(page - 1) % spec.PagesPerChunk + 1]
                else
                    path = spec.LocalFiles and spec.LocalFiles[page]
                end
                assert(path, "The sprite sheet does not have a downloadable local copy")
                local bytes = readfile(path)
                local format = mediaKind(bytes)
                assert(format == "png" or format == "jpg", "A downloaded sprite sheet is damaged")
                meta.Format = meta.Format or format
                assert(meta.Format == format, "Sprite sheets use different image formats")
                total += #bytes
                assert(total <= 512 * 1024 * 1024, "The downloaded animation exceeds 512 MiB")
                writefile(localFramePath(folder, page, format), bytes)
                if load then load:Step(page) end
                if page % 4 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end
        assert(alive(), "Import cancelled")
        writefile(folder .. "/manifest.json", game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(meta))
        readSavedBackground(id)
        return category
    end

    local function resolveMedia(name, url, stillOnly, load, fresh)
        local mediaUrl, sheetUrl = splitMediaLinks(url)
        local savedId = mediaUrl:match("^saved://([%w_-]+)$")
        if savedId then
            local result = readSavedBackground(savedId)
            assert(not stillOnly or result.Kind == "photo", "The Photo fallback must be a still image")
            return result
        end
        local localName = mediaUrl:match("^hq://([%w_-]+)$")
        if localName then
            local result = localBackground(localName)
            assert(not stillOnly or result.Kind == "photo", "The Photo fallback must be a still image")
            return result
        end
        if mediaUrl:match("^rbxassetid://") then
            assert(not stillOnly, "The Photo fallback must be a JPEG or PNG")
            return { Kind = "video"; Asset = mediaUrl; }
        end
        if sheetUrl then
            assert(not stillOnly, "Photo fallback must be a single still-image URL")
            return { Kind = "animation"; Asset = sourceSheet(name, mediaUrl, sheetUrl); }
        end
        local captureName = "Auto-v2-" .. mediaUrlHash(mediaUrl)
        if fresh then captureName ..= "-" .. name end
        local looksLikeGif = mediaUrl:lower():match("%.gif$") or mediaUrl:lower():match("%.gif%?")
        if not stillOnly and not fresh then
            if type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(backgroundStorage.Prepared(captureName) .. "/manifest.json") then
                local ok, captured = pcall(localBackground, captureName, mediaUrl)
                if ok then return captured end
            end
            local cached = sourceQuality.Read(mediaUrl)
            if cached and not looksLikeGif then return { Kind = "animation"; Asset = cached; } end
        end
        if load then load:Stage("Downloading media") end
        local kind, asset, bytes, path = loadMedia(name, mediaUrl, stillOnly, fresh)
        assert(not load or not load.Cancelled, "Background load cancelled")
        local sourceBytes = kind == "animation" and asset or bytes
        if kind == "animation" then
            if asset:sub(1, 3) == "GIF" then
                local ok, captured = pcall(captureGifBackground, captureName, mediaUrl, asset, load)
                if ok and captured then
                    captured.SourceUrl, captured.SourceBytes = mediaUrl, sourceBytes
                    return captured
                end
            end
            asset = assert((not fresh and sourceQuality.Read(mediaUrl)) or sourceQuality.Convert(mediaUrl, asset, load), "Set SourceApiUrl to the HTTPS address from Altare")
        else
            if load then load:Stage("Preparing image") end
            local width, height = photoDimensions(bytes)
            local key = fresh and name .. "-" .. mediaUrlHash(mediaUrl) or mediaUrlHash(mediaUrl)
            local ok, cleaned = pcall(cleanBackgroundAsset, key, asset, bytes,
                width, height, 1, 1, function() return gui.Parent ~= nil end)
            if ok and cleaned then asset = cleaned end
        end
        return { Kind = kind; Asset = asset; SourceUrl = mediaUrl; SourceBytes = sourceBytes; SavedFile = kind == "photo" and path or nil; }
    end

    local function uploadedAnimation(spec)
        assert(type(spec) == "table" and type(spec.Pages) == "table", "Sprite needs a Pages list of Roblox image IDs")
        local columns, rows = spec.Columns or 4, spec.Rows or 4
        local width, height = spec.CellWidth or 256, spec.CellHeight or 160
        local fps, count = spec.Fps or 12, spec.FrameCount
        assert(type(columns) == "number" and columns % 1 == 0 and columns >= 1
            and type(rows) == "number" and rows % 1 == 0 and rows >= 1
            and type(width) == "number" and width % 1 == 0 and width >= 1
            and type(height) == "number" and height % 1 == 0 and height >= 1
            and columns * width <= 4096 and rows * height <= 4096,
            "Invalid Sprite grid or cell size")
        assert(type(fps) == "number" and fps >= 1 and fps <= 30
            and type(count) == "number" and count % 1 == 0 and count >= 1
            and #spec.Pages == math.ceil(count / (columns * rows)),
            "Invalid Sprite frame count, page count or FPS")

        local pages = {}
        for i, asset in ipairs(spec.Pages) do
            local id = tostring(asset)
            if id:match("^%d+$") then id = "rbxassetid://" .. id end
            assert(id:match("^rbxassetid://[1-9]%d*$"), "Sprite pages must be Roblox image asset IDs")
            pages[i] = id
        end
        return {
            Pages = pages; FrameCount = count; Fps = fps;
            Columns = columns; Rows = rows; CellWidth = width; CellHeight = height;
        }
    end

    local function syncVideoPlayback()
        if not nativeVideo then return end
        if root.Visible and gui.Enabled then
            nativeVideo.Player:Play()
        else
            nativeVideo.Player:Pause()
        end
    end
    local videoVisibility = root:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(syncVideoPlayback)
    gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(syncVideoPlayback)

    local function playVideo(asset, interactive, load)
        if nativeVideo and nativeVideo.Asset == asset then
            nativeVideo.Display.VideoTransparency = backgroundTransparency(0)
            if load then load:Finish(true) end
            return
        end

        cancelPendingLoop()
        local token = playbackToken
        local function reportFailure(message)
            warn("[Syllinse themes] " .. message)
            if load then load:Finish(false, message)
            elseif interactive then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Themes", message, "Error") end
        end

        local display
        local ok, player = pcall(function()
            display = Instance.new("VideoDisplay")
            display.Name = "SyllinseThemeVideo"
            display.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            display.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            display.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
            display.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Default
            display.Visible = false
            display.ZIndex = 1
            setManualCorner(display, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Window)
            local source = Instance.new("VideoPlayer", display)
            source.VideoContent = Content.fromUri(asset)
            source.Looping = true
            source.Volume = 0
            local wire = Instance.new("Wire", display)
            wire.SourceInstance = source
            wire.TargetInstance = display
            display.Parent = mediaViewport
            return source
        end)
        if not ok then
            if display then display:Destroy() end
            reportFailure("Could not create the Roblox video player")
            return
        end

        pendingVideo = display
        if load then load:Stage("Buffering video") end
        local function fail()
            if playbackToken ~= token or pendingVideo ~= display then return end
            cancelPendingLoop()
            reportFailure("Could not load the Roblox video. Check the asset ID and access.")
        end
        task.delay(25, fail)
        task.spawn(function()
            local loaded, status = pcall(player.LoadAsync, player)
            if not gui.Parent or playbackToken ~= token or pendingVideo ~= display then return end
            if not loaded or status ~= Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then fail(); return end

            pendingVideo = nil
            stopAnimation()
            nativeVideo = { Asset = asset; Display = display; Player = player; }
            display.VideoTransparency = backgroundTransparency(0)
            display.Visible = true
            photo.Visible = false
            syncVideoPlayback()
            if load then load:Finish(true)
            elseif interactive then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Themes", "Video background loaded.", "Success") end
        end)
    end

    local function awaitSpritePages(batch, first, alive)
        local statuses, preloadError = {}, nil
        local requestThread = task.spawn(function()
            local succeeded, failed = 0, false
            local ok = pcall(function()
                ContentProvider:PreloadAsync(batch, function(assetId, status)
                    statuses[assetId] = status.Name
                    if status.Name == "Success" then
                        succeeded += 1
                        for _, image in ipairs(batch) do
                            if image.Image == assetId then fetchedSpritePages[image] = image.Image end
                        end
                    else
                        failed = true
                    end
                end)
            end)
            if not ok then preloadError = "preload request failed" end
            if ok and succeeded > 0 and not failed then
                for _, image in ipairs(batch) do fetchedSpritePages[image] = image.Image end
            end
        end)
        local function releaseRequest()
            if type(requestThread) == "thread" and type(task.cancel) == "function" then pcall(task.cancel, requestThread) end
        end

        local remaining = 8
        while alive() do
            local waiting
            for i, image in ipairs(batch) do
                if not spriteReadyForSetup(image) then waiting = i; break end
            end
            if not waiting then releaseRequest(); return end
            if remaining <= 0 then
                releaseRequest()
                local status = statuses[batch[waiting].Image] or preloadError or "no fetch status"
                error(string.format("Sprite sheet %d did not become ready (%s)", first + waiting - 1, status), 0)
            end
            local visible = root.Visible and gui.Enabled and mediaViewport.Visible ~= false
            local before = os.clock()
            task.wait(visible and 0.05 or 0.2)
            remaining -= os.clock() - before
        end
        releaseRequest()
    end

    local function playAnimation(result, interactive, load)
        if result == animation then
            if pendingResult then
                cancelPendingLoop()
            end
            if activePool.VisibleImage then
                activePool.VisibleImage.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency(0)
            end
            if load then load:Finish(true) end
            return
        end
        if result == pendingResult then return end
        cancelPendingLoop()
        local token = playbackToken
        pendingResult = result

        local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.Name = "SyllinseReadyLoop"
        holder.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.ZIndex = 1
        holder.Parent = mediaViewport
        local pool = { Frame = holder; Images = {}; CellSizes = {}; }
        pendingPools[pool] = true
        local function newImage(asset)
            local image = sprite:Clone()
            if asset then image.Image = asset end
            image.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            image.ImageTransparency = 1
            image.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(result.CellWidth, result.CellHeight)
            image.Visible = true
            image.Parent = holder
            return image
        end
        for index, asset in ipairs(result.Pages or {}) do
            pool.Images[index] = newImage(not result.LocalPages and asset or nil)
        end

        local function abandon(message)
            if token ~= playbackToken then return end
            playbackToken = playbackToken + 1
            pendingResult = nil
            pendingPools[pool] = nil
            holder:Destroy()
            warn("[Syllinse themes] " .. selected .. ": " .. message)
            if load then load:Finish(false, "The animated background could not load")
            elseif interactive then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Themes", "The animated background could not load.", "Error") end
        end
        local function alive()
            return gui.Parent and holder.Parent and (activePool == pool or token == playbackToken)
        end

        local function preloadPages(first, last, sourcePaths, sourceAssets, useOriginal)
            local batch = {}
            local register = type(getcustomasset) == "function" and getcustomasset or getsynasset
            for i = first, last do
                local image = pool.Images[i]
                if sourcePaths then
                    local path = sourcePaths[i - first + 1]
                    local asset = sourceAssets[path] or register(path)
                    assert(type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "", "Could not register source sprite sheet " .. i)
                    sourceAssets[path] = asset
                    if not alive() then return end
                    if image == pool.VisibleImage and image then
                        pool.RetiredImage = image
                    elseif image then
                        image:Destroy()
                    end
                    local cleanedSize
                    if not useOriginal and result.SourceUrl and result.PageCount <= 12 and type(readfile) == "function" then
                        local ok, cleaned, width, height = pcall(function()
                            return cleanBackgroundAsset(mediaUrlHash(path), asset, readfile(path),
                                result.CellWidth, result.CellHeight, result.Columns, result.Rows, alive)
                        end)
                        if ok and cleaned then
                            asset, cleanedSize = cleaned, Vector2.new(width, height)
                        end
                        if not alive() then return end
                    end
                    image = newImage(asset)
                    if cleanedSize then image.ImageRectSize = cleanedSize end
                    pool.CellSizes[i] = cleanedSize
                    pool.Images[i] = image
                elseif result.LocalPages then
                    local asset = register(result.Pages[i])
                    assert(type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "", "Could not register a sprite sheet")
                    image.Image = asset
                end
                stageSprite(image)
                batch[#batch + 1] = image
            end
            awaitSpritePages(batch, first, alive)
        end

        local function preloadStaticPages(first, last)
            local ok, failure = pcall(preloadPages, first, last)
            if ok or not alive() then return end
            warn("[Syllinse themes] Retrying sprite sheets: " .. tostring(failure))
            for i = first, last do
                if not pool.Images[i].IsLoaded then
                    local image = pool.Images[i]
                    pool.Images[i] = newImage(image.Image)
                    image:Destroy()
                end
            end
            preloadPages(first, last)
        end

        local sourceFiles, sourceLoaded, sourceAssets, resident = {}, {}, {}, {}
        local chunkCount = (result.SourceUrl or result.LocalFolder) and math.ceil(result.PageCount / result.PagesPerChunk)
        pool.Streamed = chunkCount ~= nil
        local window
        if chunkCount then
            -- Bound the number of decoded textures while keeping as much of the loop resident as possible.
            local chunkBytes = result.CellWidth * result.CellHeight * result.Columns * result.Rows * 4 * result.PagesPerChunk
            window = math.min(chunkCount, math.clamp(math.floor(48 * 1024^2 / chunkBytes), 2, 6))
        end
        local function loadChunk(index)
            resident[index] = true
            sourceLoaded[index] = nil
            local paths = sourceFiles[index]
            local first = (index - 1) * result.PagesPerChunk + 1
            local last = first + #paths - 1
            local ok, failure = pcall(preloadPages, first, last, paths, sourceAssets)
            if not alive() then return end
            if not ok then
                warn(string.format("[Syllinse themes] Retrying background batch %d/%d: %s", index, chunkCount, tostring(failure)))
                if result.SourceUrl then
                    local refreshed, retryPaths = pcall(sourceQuality.Files, result, index, alive, paths)
                    if not alive() then return end
                    if refreshed then
                        paths = retryPaths
                        sourceFiles[index] = paths
                    else
                        warn("[Syllinse themes] Could not refresh the converter batch; retrying its cached files: " .. tostring(retryPaths))
                    end
                end
                for _, path in ipairs(paths) do sourceAssets[path] = nil end
                preloadPages(first, last, paths, sourceAssets, true)
            end
            sourceLoaded[index] = true
        end

        local function streamSource()
            local cells = result.Columns * result.Rows
            local retryAt = {}
            while alive() do
                local current = math.floor(frameIndex / (cells * result.PagesPerChunk)) + 1
                local wanted, nextChunk = {}, nil
                local now = os.clock()
                local neededImage = pool.NeededPage and pool.Images[pool.NeededPage]
                if pool.NeededPage and (not neededImage or not neededImage.IsLoaded) then
                    local needed = math.ceil(pool.NeededPage / result.PagesPerChunk)
                    wanted[needed] = true
                    if now >= (retryAt[needed] or 0) then nextChunk = needed end
                end
                for distance = 0, window - 1 do
                    local index = (current + distance - 1) % chunkCount + 1
                    wanted[index] = true
                    if not sourceLoaded[index] and not nextChunk and now >= (retryAt[index] or 0) then nextChunk = index end
                end
                for index in pairs(resident) do
                    if not wanted[index] then
                        local first = (index - 1) * result.PagesPerChunk + 1
                        for i = first, math.min(first + result.PagesPerChunk - 1, result.PageCount) do
                            if pool.Images[i] then pool.Images[i]:Destroy() end
                            pool.Images[i] = nil
                            pool.CellSizes[i] = nil
                        end
                        sourceLoaded[index] = nil
                        resident[index] = nil
                    end
                end
                if nextChunk then
                    local ok, failure = pcall(loadChunk, nextChunk)
                    if not ok and alive() then
                        retryAt[nextChunk] = os.clock() + 2
                        warn("[Syllinse themes] Background batch will retry: " .. tostring(failure))
                    elseif ok then
                        retryAt[nextChunk] = nil
                    end
                else
                    task.wait(0.12)
                end
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            if not gui.Parent or token ~= playbackToken then return end
            local initial = math.min(2, #pool.Images)
            local ok, failure
            if chunkCount then
                ok, failure = pcall(function()
                    if load then load:Stage("Downloading animation batches", chunkCount) end
                    -- Keep downloads and disk writes off the playback path, including at the loop boundary.
                    for i = 1, chunkCount do
                        local paths
                        if result.LocalFolder then
                            paths = {}
                            local first = (i - 1) * result.PagesPerChunk + 1
                            for page = first, math.min(first + result.PagesPerChunk - 1, result.PageCount) do
                                paths[#paths + 1] = localFramePath(result.LocalFolder, page, result.LocalPageFormat)
                            end
                        else
                            paths = sourceQuality.Files(result, i, alive)
                        end
                        if not paths or not alive() then return end
                        sourceFiles[i] = paths
                        if load then load:Step(i) end
                        if i % 4 == 0 then task.wait() end
                    end
                    if load then load:Stage("Preparing playback batches", window) end
                    for i = 1, window do
                        if not alive() then return end
                        loadChunk(i)
                        if load then load:Step(i) end
                    end
                end)
            else
                if load then load:Stage("Preparing animation sheets") end
                ok, failure = pcall(preloadStaticPages, 1, initial)
            end
            if not gui.Parent or token ~= playbackToken then return end
            if not ok then abandon(tostring(failure)); return end
            if not spriteReadyForSetup(pool.Images[1]) then
                abandon("The first sprite sheet failed to preload")
                return
            end

            pendingResult = nil
            pendingPools[pool] = nil
            local oldPool = activePool
            animation, activePool = result, pool
            elapsed, frameIndex = 0, -1
            showSpriteFrame(0)
            if nativeVideo then
                nativeVideo.Display:Destroy()
                nativeVideo = nil
            end
            photo.Visible = false
            if oldPool then oldPool.Frame:Destroy() end
            if load then load:Finish(true) end
            print(string.format("[Syllinse themes] Loop ready. %dx%d at %.2f FPS, %d frames.",
                result.CellWidth, result.CellHeight, result.Fps, result.FrameCount))
            if type(result.SourceDuration) == "number" and type(result.Duration) == "number"
                and result.SourceDuration > result.Duration + 0.05 then
                print(string.format("[Syllinse themes] Playing the first %.1f seconds of a %.1f-second clip.",
                    result.Duration, result.SourceDuration))
            end

            if chunkCount then
                task.defer(streamSource)
                return
            end
            for first = initial + 1, #pool.Images, 4 do
                if not gui.Parent or activePool ~= pool then return end
                local loaded, message = pcall(preloadStaticPages, first, math.min(first + 3, #pool.Images))
                if not loaded then
                    if activePool == pool then warn("[Syllinse themes] " .. tostring(message)) end
                    return
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local mediaCache, pending = {}, {}
    local spritePlayback = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
        if not animation or not root.Visible or not gui.Enabled then return end
        local duration = animation.Duration or animation.FrameCount / animation.Fps
        local preparedTime = activePool.PreparedTime
        local nextTime = preparedTime or (elapsed + delta) % duration
        local frame = animation.FrameEnds and sourceFrameAt(animation, nextTime)
            or math.min(animation.FrameCount - 1, math.floor(nextTime * animation.Fps))
        if showSpriteFrame(frame) then
            elapsed = preparedTime and (nextTime + delta) % duration or nextTime
            activePool.PreparedTime = nil
            activePool.MissingTime = nil
        elseif activePool.Streamed and activePool.NeededPage then
            activePool.MissingTime = (activePool.MissingTime or 0) + delta
            if activePool.MissingTime >= 0.5 then
                -- Keep the last good frame visible while advancing past missing frames.
                elapsed = (nextTime + (preparedTime and delta or 0)) % duration
                activePool.PreparedTime = nil
                local prepared = activePool.PreparedImage
                if prepared and prepared.Parent then stageSprite(prepared) end
                activePool.PreparedImage = nil
            end
        elseif activePool.PreparedImage then
            activePool.PreparedTime = nextTime
        end
    end)
    gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        spritePlayback:Disconnect()
        videoVisibility:Disconnect()
        playbackToken = playbackToken + 1
    end)

    local function extraColors(name)
        local preset = presets[name]
        if name == "Original" then
            for key, value in pairs(defaults) do Theme[key] = value end
        end
        local style = table.clone(Theme.Setup.Style)
        for key in pairs(style) do
            if preset[key] ~= nil then style[key] = preset[key] end
        end
        Theme.Style = style
        Theme.ButtonFill = preset.ButtonFill
        Theme.ButtonHoverFill = preset.ButtonHoverFill
        if name == "Original" then
            Theme.PanelIcon = preset.PanelIcon or defaults.PanelIcon
        else
            local text = preset.Text or defaults.Text
            local surface = preset.Surface or defaults.Surface
            local accent = preset.Accent or defaults.Accent
            Theme.ThemedControls = true
            Theme.SurfaceHover = surface:Lerp(accent, Theme.Style.ButtonHoverTint)
            Theme.Stroke = preset.Border or surface:Lerp(accent, Theme.Style.BorderBlend)
            Theme.SliderTrack = surface:Lerp(accent, 0.24)
            Theme.ToggleOffTrack = surface:Lerp(accent, 0.25)
            Theme.ToggleOffThumb = surface:Lerp(text, 0.54)
            Theme.SliderHead = surface:Lerp(accent, 0.58)
            Theme.SliderHandle = accent:Lerp(text, 0.28)
            Theme.Divider = preset.Divider or Theme.Stroke
            Theme.PanelTitle = text
            Theme.HeaderIconTint = text
            Theme.PanelIcon = preset.PanelIcon or accent:Lerp(text, 0.26)
            Theme.PanelValue = text:Lerp(surface, 0.12)
            Theme.PanelSecondary = text:Lerp(surface, 0.4)
            Theme.Muted = text:Lerp(surface, 0.35)
            Theme.IconIdle = text:Lerp(surface, 0.32)
            Theme.IconHover = text:Lerp(surface, 0.12)
            Theme.Dim = text:Lerp(surface, 0.6)
        end
        Lumen.Theme.SurfaceAlt = Theme.SurfaceHover
        Lumen.Theme.Border = Theme.Stroke
        Lumen.Theme.SectionBorder = Theme.Stroke
        Lumen.Theme.AccentDark = Theme.Muted
    end
    -- Keep the sidebar and footer separators without a top-bar divider.
    local function syncShellBorders()
        sideDivider.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider
        local style = Theme.Style
        sideDivider.BackgroundTransparency = style.DividerTransparency
        sideDivider.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SIDEBAR_WIDTH - style.DividerThickness, 1)
        sideDivider.Size = UDim2.new(0, style.DividerThickness, sideDivider.Size.Y.Scale, sideDivider.Size.Y.Offset)
        footerDivider.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider
        footerDivider.BackgroundTransparency = style.DividerTransparency
        footerDivider.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, style.DividerThickness)
        shellStroke.Color = Theme.Stroke
        shellStroke.Transparency = style.OutlineTransparency
        shellStroke.Thickness = style.OutlineThickness
        local searchBorder = searchFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        if searchBorder then
            searchBorder.Color = Theme.Stroke
            searchBorder.Transparency = 0.08
        end
    end
    local function syncShellBackground()
        -- One window-sized layer supplies the color at every point of the shell.
        -- Header/sidebar fills must not cover that layer with separate flat rectangles.
        header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        footerBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        footerTopFill.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        backdrop.Visible = true
        shellSheen:SetEnabled(presets[selected].ShellSheen == true)
        christmasShine:SetEnabled(presets[selected].ChristmasShine == true, Theme.ActiveTabIcon or Theme.Accent)
        local endColor = presets[selected].GradientEnd or Theme.Root:Lerp(Theme.Accent, Theme.Style.GradientStrength)
        gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Theme.Root, endColor)
        gradient.Rotation = Theme.Style.GradientAngle
        syncShellBorders()
    end
    local primaryStill = false
    local customBackground, backgroundRequest = nil, 0
    local currentMediaKeys = {}

    local function showPhoto(result)
        photo.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency(0)
        photo.Image = result.Asset
        photo.Visible = not animation and not nativeVideo
    end

    local function refreshMedia(interactive, loadProgress)
        if not loadProgress and activeBackgroundLoad and not activeBackgroundLoad.Done then loadProgress = activeBackgroundLoad end
        if pendingResult or pendingVideo then cancelPendingLoop() end
        primaryStill = false
        currentMediaKeys = {}
        local name = selected
        local preset = presets[name]
        local url = preset.Media or preset.Video or preset.Photo
        starfield:SetOpacity(backgroundOpacity / 100)
        starfield:SetEnabled(preset.Starfield == true)
        snowfall:SetOpacity(backgroundOpacity / 100)
        snowfall:SetEnabled(preset.Snowfall == true)
        if (preset.Starfield or preset.Snowfall) and not customBackground then
            if activeBackgroundLoad then activeBackgroundLoad:Cancel() end
            stopAnimation()
            photo.Image, photo.Visible = "", false
            return
        end
        if (backgroundSelectionNone and not customBackground) or (not url and not preset.Sprite and not customBackground) then
            if activeBackgroundLoad then activeBackgroundLoad:Cancel() end
            stopAnimation()
            photo.Image, photo.Visible = "", false
            return
        end

        local function load(urlToLoad, fallback)
            local key = name .. "|" .. tostring(urlToLoad) .. (fallback and "|fallback" or "|primary")
            currentMediaKeys[key] = true
            local function show(result, load)
                load = load or (not fallback and loadProgress or nil)
                if result.Kind == "animation" then
                    playAnimation(result.Asset, false, load)
                elseif result.Kind == "video" then
                    playVideo(result.Asset, false, load)
                elseif not fallback or not primaryStill then
                    if not fallback then
                        primaryStill = true
                        stopAnimation()
                    end
                    showPhoto(result)
                    if load then load:Finish(true) end
                end
            end

            if mediaCache[key] then
                show(mediaCache[key])
            elseif not pending[key] then
                pending[key] = true
                local load = not fallback and beginBackgroundLoad() or nil
                task.spawn(function()
                    local ok, result = pcall(resolveMedia, name, urlToLoad, fallback, load)
                    pending[key] = nil
                    if ok then mediaCache[key] = result end
                    if not gui.Parent or not currentMediaKeys[key] then
                        if load then load:Cancel() end
                        return
                    end
                    if ok then
                        show(result, load)
                    else
                        if load then load:Finish(false, "Could not load background") end
                        warn("[Syllinse themes] " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(result))
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        if customBackground then
            if customBackground.Kind == "photo" then
                stopAnimation()
                showPhoto(customBackground)
                if loadProgress then loadProgress:Finish(true) end
            else
                if customBackground.Kind == "video" then
                    playVideo(customBackground.Asset, interactive, loadProgress)
                else
                    playAnimation(customBackground.Asset, interactive, loadProgress)
                end
            end
        elseif preset.Sprite then
            if preset.Photo then load(preset.Photo, true) end
            local ok, result = pcall(uploadedAnimation, preset.Sprite)
            if ok then
                playAnimation(result, false, beginBackgroundLoad())
            else
                warn("[Syllinse themes] " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(result))
            end
        else
            if preset.Photo and preset.Photo ~= url then load(preset.Photo, true) end
            load(url, false)
        end
    end

    local function refreshSurfaces()
        SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
        if SyllinseRuntime.refreshThemeBackdrop then SyllinseRuntime.refreshThemeBackdrop() end
    end
    local function markSelected(name)
        selected = name
        SyllinseRuntime.activeAppearancePreset = name
        if SyllinseRuntime.clearCustomThemeSelection then SyllinseRuntime.clearCustomThemeSelection() end
        local flag = Lumen.Flags.SyllinseThemePreset
        if flag then flag.Value = name end
        if SyllinseRuntime.appearanceDropdown then
            SyllinseRuntime.appearanceSyncing = true
            SyllinseRuntime.appearanceDropdown.Set(presets[name].Name or name)
            SyllinseRuntime.appearanceSyncing = false
        end
    end
    local function choose(name)
        name = presets[name] and name or idsByName[name]
        if not name or not presets[name] then return end
        markSelected(name)
        extraColors(name)
        local preset, initial = presets[name], SyllinseRuntime.appearanceDefaults
        local surface, text = preset.Surface or initial.Surface, preset.Text or initial.Text
        SyllinseRuntime.appearanceBatch = true
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeWindow", preset.Root or initial.Root)
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemePanels", surface)
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeAccent", preset.Accent or initial.Accent)
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeStroke", preset.Border or surface:Lerp(preset.Accent or initial.Accent, 0.2))
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeText", text)
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeSubtext", preset.Subtext or (name == "Original" and initial.Subtext or text:Lerp(surface, 0.42)))
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeTabIcons", preset.TabIcons or (name == "Original" and initial.TabIcons or preset.Subtext or text:Lerp(surface, 0.42)))
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeActiveTabIcon", preset.ActiveTabIcon or preset.Accent or initial.ActiveTabIcon)
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeModuleTransparency", preset.ModuleTransparency or initial.ModuleTransparency)
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemePanelDivider", preset.PanelDivider or initial.PanelDivider)
        SyllinseRuntime.appearanceBatch = false
        refreshSurfaces()
        if (preset.Starfield or preset.Snowfall) and SyllinseRuntime.useThemeBackground then SyllinseRuntime.useThemeBackground() end
    end
    -- Restore metadata without overwriting a config's independently saved color flags.
    Lumen.RegisterFlag("SyllinseThemePreset", {
        Value = "Original";
        Set = function(name)
            name = presets[name] and name or idsByName[name] or "Original"
            markSelected(name)
            extraColors(name)
            task.defer(function()
                if not gui.Parent then return end
                local fields = {
                    { "ThemeWindow", SyllinseRuntime.applyWindowColor },
                    { "ThemePanels", SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelColor },
                    { "ThemeText", SyllinseRuntime.applyTextColor },
                    { "ThemeAccent", SyllinseRuntime.applyAccentColor },
                    { "ThemeStroke", SyllinseRuntime.applyStrokeColor },
                    { "ThemeSubtext", SyllinseRuntime.applySubtextColor },
                    { "ThemeTabIcons", SyllinseRuntime.applyTabIconColor },
                    { "ThemeActiveTabIcon", SyllinseRuntime.applyActiveTabIconColor },
                }
                SyllinseRuntime.appearanceBatch = true
                for _, field in ipairs(fields) do
                    local flag = Lumen.Flags[field[1]]
                    if flag and typeof(flag.Value) == "Color3" then field[2](flag.Value) end
                end
                local opacity = Lumen.Flags.ThemeModuleTransparency
                if opacity then SyllinseRuntime.applyModuleTransparency(opacity.Value) end
                SyllinseRuntime.appearanceBatch = false
                refreshSurfaces()
            end)
        end;
    })
    SyllinseRuntime.selectTheme = function(name)
        local id = presets[name] and name or idsByName[name]
        if id then choose(id) end
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.restoreThemeAppearance = function(snapshot)
        local name = presets[snapshot.Preset] and snapshot.Preset or "Original"
        markSelected(name)
        extraColors(name)
        SyllinseRuntime.appearanceBatch = true
        for flag, rgb in pairs(snapshot.Colors) do
            Lumen.SetFlag(flag, Color3.new(rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3]))
        end
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemeModuleTransparency", snapshot.ModuleTransparency)
        Lumen.SetFlag("ThemePanelDivider", snapshot.PanelDivider)
        SyllinseRuntime.appearanceBatch = false
        refreshSurfaces()
    end
    local urlInput, syncMediaSelection
    local function currentInputText(input)
        if not input then return "" end
        if type(input.GetText) == "function" then return input.GetText() end
        return type(input.Value) == "string" and input.Value or ""
    end

    local function writeUrlInput(value)
        if not urlInput then return end
        urlInput.Set(value)
    end

    local function setBackgroundUrl(value, interactive, loadProgress, fresh)
        local url = type(value) == "string" and value:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
        if url ~= "" and not pcall(splitMediaLinks, url) then
            if interactive then SyllinseRuntime.notify("Themes", "Use a media URL, hq://name, or a Roblox video asset ID.", "Error") end
            return
        end

        backgroundRequest = backgroundRequest + 1
        local token = backgroundRequest
        if activeBackgroundLoad and activeBackgroundLoad ~= loadProgress then activeBackgroundLoad:Cancel() end
        local load = url ~= "" and (loadProgress or beginBackgroundLoad(nil, nil, url:match("^saved://") ~= nil)) or nil
        local function apply(result)
            backgroundSelectionNone = url == ""
            customBackground = result
            Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value = url
            if syncMediaSelection then syncMediaSelection(url) end
            refreshMedia(interactive, load)
            SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)
        end
        if url == "" then
            apply(nil)
            return
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            local name = fresh and "Preview-" .. game:GetService("HttpService"):GenerateGUID(false):gsub("%-", "") or "CustomBackground"
            local ok, result = pcall(resolveMedia, name, url, false, load, fresh)
            if not gui.Parent or backgroundRequest ~= token then load:Cancel(); return end
            if ok then
                apply(result)
            else
                warn("[Syllinse themes] Background URL: " .. tostring(result))
                load:Finish(false, "Could not load the new URL. Previous background kept. " .. tostring(result):sub(1, 150))
            end
        end)
    end

    Lumen.RegisterFlag("SyllinseBackgroundUrl", {
        Value = "";
        Set = function(value) setBackgroundUrl(value, false) end;
    })
    local libraryPath = "Syllinse/Backgrounds/index.json"
    if type(isfile) == "function" and (isfile("SyllinseBackgrounds/index.json") or isfile("SyllinseBackgrounds/index.json.bak")) then
        backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        backgroundStorage.MoveFile("SyllinseBackgrounds/index.json", libraryPath)
        backgroundStorage.MoveFile("SyllinseBackgrounds/index.json.bak", libraryPath .. ".bak")
    end
    local http = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local function readLibraryEntries()
        if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return {}, {} end
        for _, path in ipairs({ libraryPath, libraryPath .. ".bak" }) do
            local ok, saved = pcall(function()
                local bytes = readfile(path)
                assert(#bytes <= 512 * 1024)
                local data = http:JSONDecode(bytes)
                assert(type(data) == "table" and data.Version == 1 and type(data.Entries) == "table" and #data.Entries <= 100)
                return data
            end)
            if ok then
                local entries, seen = {}, { None = true }
                for _, entry in ipairs(saved.Entries) do
                    if type(entry) == "table" and entry.Name == "None" then entry.Name = "None (saved)" end
                    if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.Id) == "string" and entry.Id:match("^[%w_-]+$")
                        and #entry.Id <= 64 and type(entry.Name) == "string" and #entry.Name > 0 and #entry.Name <= 80
                        and not seen[entry.Name] then
                        entries[#entries + 1] = entry
                        seen[entry.Name] = true
                    end
                end
                return entries, saved
            end
        end
        return {}, {}
    end
    local entries, librarySettings = readLibraryEntries()
    local savedDropdown, updatingLibrary = nil, true
    local savingBackground, importGeneration, settingsGeneration = false, 0, 0
    local importLoad
    local importPrevious
    local importRollback
    local libraryReady = false
    -- Background history must not override the selected appearance preset on startup.
    -- Explicit config flags still restore custom module transparency afterwards.
    local panelContrast = 100 - math.clamp(SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparency or 0, 0, 1) * 100
    local savedByName = {}
    local function libraryDocument(nextEntries)
        return { Version = 1; Entries = nextEntries or entries; Selected = librarySettings.Selected;
            Opacity = backgroundOpacity; PanelContrast = panelContrast; }
    end
    local function persistLibrary(nextEntries)
        assert(type(writefile) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function",
            "This executor cannot save backgrounds")
        backgroundStorage.Ensure()
        local bytes = http:JSONEncode(libraryDocument(nextEntries))
        writefile(libraryPath, bytes)
        pcall(writefile, libraryPath .. ".bak", bytes)
    end
    local function saveSettingsSoon()
        if not libraryReady then return end
        settingsGeneration += 1
        local token = settingsGeneration
        task.delay(0.5, function()
            if gui.Parent and token == settingsGeneration then
                local ok, err = pcall(persistLibrary)
                if not ok then warn("[Syllinse backgrounds] Could not save settings: " .. tostring(err)) end
            end
        end)
    end
    local function refreshLibrary()
        local names = {}
        table.clear(savedByName)
        for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
            names[#names + 1] = entry.Name
            savedByName[entry.Name] = entry
        end
        table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
        table.insert(names, 1, "None")
        if savedDropdown then
            updatingLibrary = true
            local previous = savedDropdown.Value
            savedDropdown.UpdateOptions(names)
            savedDropdown.Set(savedByName[previous] and previous or "None")
            updatingLibrary = false
        end
        local service = SyllinseRuntime.buttonMediaService
        if service then for _, callback in ipairs(service.Listeners) do task.defer(callback) end end
        return names
    end
    local function discardImportFiles(id)
        if not id or type(listfiles) ~= "function" or type(delfile) ~= "function" then return end
        local folder = backgroundStorage.Saved(id)
        pcall(function()
            for _, path in ipairs(listfiles(folder)) do
                local normalized = path:gsub("\\", "/"):gsub("/+", "/")
                local parent = normalized:match("^(.*)/[^/]+$")
                if parent and (parent == folder or parent:sub(-#folder - 1) == "/" .. folder)
                    and not normalized:find("/../", 1, true) and normalized:sub(1, 3) ~= "../" then
                    delfile(path)
                end
            end
            if type(delfolder) == "function" then pcall(delfolder, folder) end
        end)
    end

    local function backgroundInput(options)
        local existing = {}
        for _, child in ipairs(section.Content:GetChildren()) do existing[child] = true end
        local control = section:Input(options)
        for _, child in ipairs(section.Content:GetChildren()) do
            if not existing[child] then
                local box = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextBox", true)
                if box then
                    control.GetText = function() return box.Text end
                    return control
                end
            end
        end
        error("Could not bind background input: " .. options.Name)
    end
    local nameInput = backgroundInput({ Name = "Background name"; Value = ""; Placeholder = "e.g. Midnight mountains"; Callback = function() end; })
    urlInput = backgroundInput({ Name = "Media URL / asset ID"; Value = ""; Placeholder = "https://... or rbxassetid://..."; Callback = function() end; })
    savedDropdown = SyllinseRuntime.addReptillianDropdown(section, {
        Name = "Saved backgrounds"; Options = refreshLibrary(); Value = "None"; Search = true;
        Callback = function(value)
            if updatingLibrary then return end
            if savingBackground then
                notifyBackground("Cancel the current import before choosing another background.", "Warning")
                if syncMediaSelection then syncMediaSelection(Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value or "") end
                return
            end
            local entry = savedByName[value]
            setBackgroundUrl(entry and "saved://" .. entry.Id or "", true)
        end;
    })
    local opacityControl = SyllinseRuntime.addNumericSlider(section, {
        Name = "Background opacity"; Suffix = "%"; Value = math.clamp(tonumber(librarySettings.Opacity) or 65, 0, 100);
        Min = 0; Max = 100; Increment = 1; Flag = "SyllinseBackgroundOpacity";
        Callback = function(value)
            backgroundOpacity = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 65, 0, 100)
            mediaViewport.Visible = backgroundEnabled and backgroundOpacity > 0
            starfield:SetOpacity(backgroundOpacity / 100)
            photo.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency(0)
            if activePool and activePool.VisibleImage then activePool.VisibleImage.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency(0) end
            if nativeVideo then nativeVideo.Display.VideoTransparency = backgroundTransparency(0) end
            if Lumen.Flags.SyllinseMediaOpacity then Lumen.Flags.SyllinseMediaOpacity.Value = backgroundOpacity / 100 end
            saveSettingsSoon()
        end;
    })
    Lumen.RegisterFlag("SyllinseMediaOpacity", {
        Value = backgroundOpacity / 100;
        Set = function(value) opacityControl.Set(math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0.65, 0, 1) * 100) end;
    })
    Lumen.RegisterFlag("SyllinseBackgroundEnabled", {
        Value = true;
        Set = function(value)
            backgroundEnabled = value == true
            mediaViewport.Visible = backgroundEnabled and backgroundOpacity > 0
        end;
    })
    SyllinseRuntime.onModuleTransparencyChanged = function(value)
        panelContrast = 100 - value
        if Lumen.Flags.SyllinsePanelContrast then Lumen.Flags.SyllinsePanelContrast.Value = panelContrast end
        saveSettingsSoon()
    end
    Lumen.RegisterFlag("SyllinsePanelContrast", {
        Value = panelContrast;
        Set = function(value) SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparencyControl.Set(100 - math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 62, 0, 100)) end;
    })
    if SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparencyControl then SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparencyControl.Set(100 - panelContrast) end
    updatingLibrary = false
    syncMediaSelection = function(url)
        local name, id = "None", ""
        for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
            if url == "saved://" .. entry.Id then
                name, id = entry.Name, entry.Id
                break
            end
        end
        updatingLibrary = true
        savedDropdown.Set(name)
        updatingLibrary = false
        librarySettings.Selected = id
        saveSettingsSoon()
    end

    SyllinseRuntime.refreshThemeBackdrop = function()
        starfield:SetEnabled(presets[selected].Starfield == true)
        snowfall:SetEnabled(presets[selected].Snowfall == true)
        if not customBackground then refreshMedia(false) end
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.useThemeBackground = function()
        -- Native stars/snow stay above imported media without replacing it.
        Lumen.SetFlag("SyllinseBackgroundEnabled", true)
        Lumen.SetFlag("SyllinseBackgroundOpacity", 100)
        if customBackground then
            starfield:SetEnabled(presets[selected].Starfield == true)
            snowfall:SetEnabled(presets[selected].Snowfall == true)
        else
            setBackgroundUrl("", false)
        end
    end

    SyllinseRuntime.checkThemeBackground = function(background)
        if savingBackground then return false, "Wait for the current background import to finish." end
        if background.Url ~= "" and not pcall(splitMediaLinks, background.Url) then
            return false, "This theme's background URL is invalid. Select a background and save the theme again."
        end
        local id = background.Url:match("^saved://([%w_-]+)$")
        if id then
            local found = false
            for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do if entry.Id == id then found = true; break end end
            if not found then return false, "This theme's saved background is missing. Import that background again first." end
        end
        return true
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.captureThemeBackground = function()
        if savingBackground or (activeBackgroundLoad and not activeBackgroundLoad.Done) or pendingResult or pendingVideo then
            return nil, "Wait until the background has finished loading before saving the theme."
        end
        return { Url = Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value or ""; Enabled = backgroundEnabled; Opacity = backgroundOpacity; }
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.restoreThemeBackground = function(background)
        local ready, reason = SyllinseRuntime.checkThemeBackground(background)
        if not ready then error(reason) end
        Lumen.SetFlag("SyllinseBackgroundOpacity", background.Opacity)
        Lumen.SetFlag("SyllinseBackgroundEnabled", background.Enabled)
        if background.Url ~= Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value or (activeBackgroundLoad and not activeBackgroundLoad.Done) then
            setBackgroundUrl(background.Url, false)
        end
    end

    -- Button media reuses prepared files from the shared background library.
    -- These operations never select or replace the shell background.
    SyllinseRuntime.buttonMediaService = { Listeners = {} }
    local buttonMediaService = SyllinseRuntime.buttonMediaService
    function buttonMediaService.List()
        local result = {}
        for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do result[#result + 1] = table.clone(entry) end
        return result
    end
    function buttonMediaService.Resolve(url)
        assert(type(url) == "string" and url:match("^saved://[%w_-]+$"), "Select an imported background")
        return readSavedBackground(url:sub(9))
    end
    function buttonMediaService.Page(asset, page, alive)
        assert(alive(), "Button load cancelled")
        local path
        if asset.LocalFolder then
            path = localFramePath(asset.LocalFolder, page, asset.LocalPageFormat)
        elseif asset.SourceUrl then
            local chunk = math.ceil(page / asset.PagesPerChunk)
            local paths = sourceQuality.Files(asset, chunk, alive)
            path = paths[(page - 1) % asset.PagesPerChunk + 1]
        elseif asset.LocalPages then
            path = asset.Pages[page]
        else
            return assert(asset.Pages[page], "Missing animation page")
        end
        assert(alive(), "Button load cancelled")
        local register = type(getcustomasset) == "function" and getcustomasset or getsynasset
        assert(type(register) == "function", "This executor cannot load local media")
        return register(path)
    end


    section:Button({ Name = "Import, download & save"; Callback = function()
        if savingBackground then
            notifyBackground("An import is already running. Use Cancel import before entering another background.", "Warning"); return
        end
        local value = currentInputText(urlInput):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local title = currentInputText(nameInput):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local nameLength = utf8.len(title)
        if not nameLength or nameLength < 1 or nameLength > 48 or title:lower() == "none" then
            notifyBackground("Enter a name of 1–48 characters other than None.", "Warning"); return
        end
        if value == "" or not pcall(splitMediaLinks, value) then
            notifyBackground("Enter a direct media URL or Roblox asset ID.", "Warning"); return
        end
        local existing = savedByName[title]
        if not existing then
            for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do if entry.Name:lower() == title:lower() then existing = entry; break end end
        end
        if existing then
            notifyBackground("The background name '" .. title .. "' is already used. Rename it before importing.", "Warning"); return
        end
        if #entries >= 100 then notifyBackground("The background library has reached 100 entries.", "Warning"); return end
        importGeneration += 1
        backgroundRequest += 1
        local token, requestAtStart = importGeneration, backgroundRequest
        savingBackground = true
        local previous = { Url = Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value; Result = customBackground; None = backgroundSelectionNone; }
        local previousEntries = table.clone(entries)
        local id, committed
        importPrevious = previous
        local load = beginBackgroundLoad("Importing: " .. title)
        load.SuccessMessage = "Background '" .. title .. "' is ready."
        importLoad = load
        local appliedUrl
        local function restorePrevious()
            cancelPendingLoop()
            customBackground, backgroundSelectionNone = previous.Result, previous.None
            Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value = previous.Url
            if committed then
                librarySettings.Selected = previous.Url and previous.Url:match("^saved://([%w_-]+)$") or ""
                local restored, failure = pcall(persistLibrary, previousEntries)
                entries = previousEntries
                refreshLibrary()
                if restored then discardImportFiles(id)
                else notifyBackground("The previous background was restored, but its library could not be saved: " .. tostring(failure):sub(1, 100), "Error") end
            end
            syncMediaSelection(previous.Url or "")
            refreshMedia(false)
            SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)
        end
        importRollback = restorePrevious
        load.OnComplete = function(success)
            if token ~= importGeneration then return end
            savingBackground, importLoad, importPrevious, importRollback = false, nil, nil, nil
            if success then
                if currentInputText(nameInput):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") == title then nameInput.Set("") end
                if currentInputText(urlInput):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") == value then writeUrlInput("") end
            end
            if not success and not load.Cancelled and appliedUrl and
                (Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value == appliedUrl or backgroundRequest == requestAtStart + 1) then
                restorePrevious()
            end
        end
        local function alive() return gui.Parent ~= nil and token == importGeneration and not load.Cancelled end
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, message = pcall(function()
                assert(alive(), "Import cancelled")
                id = http:GenerateGUID(false):gsub("%-", "")
                local result = resolveMedia("Import-" .. id, value, false, load, true)
                assert(alive(), "Import cancelled")
                local category = saveBackgroundFiles(value, result, id, alive, load)
                assert(alive(), "Import cancelled")
                local nextEntries = table.clone(entries)
                nextEntries[#nextEntries + 1] = { Id = id; Name = title; Category = category; Url = value; }
                -- A completed import becomes selected only if another request has not superseded it.
                local previousSelection = librarySettings.Selected
                if backgroundRequest == requestAtStart then librarySettings.Selected = id end
                local saved, failure = pcall(persistLibrary, nextEntries)
                if not saved then librarySettings.Selected = previousSelection; error(failure, 0) end
                entries, committed = nextEntries, true
                refreshLibrary()
                if backgroundRequest == requestAtStart then
                    appliedUrl = "saved://" .. id
                    load:Stage("Preparing playback")
                    setBackgroundUrl(appliedUrl, false, load)
                else
                    load:Finish(true, "Background saved. Your newer selection was kept.")
                end
            end)
            if not committed then discardImportFiles(id) end
            if token ~= importGeneration then return end
            if not ok then load:Finish(false, "Could not import background: " .. tostring(message):sub(1, 160)) end
        end)
    end })
    section:Button({ Name = "Cancel import"; Callback = function()
        if not savingBackground then return end
        local rollback = importRollback
        importGeneration += 1
        backgroundRequest += 1
        savingBackground = false
        if importLoad then importLoad:Cancel(); importLoad = nil end
        importPrevious, importRollback = nil, nil
        if rollback then rollback() end
