        syncMediaSelection(Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value or "")
        notifyBackground("Import cancelled.", "Info")
    end })
    section:Button({ Name = "Delete selected background"; Callback = function()
        if savingBackground then notifyBackground("Cancel the current import before deleting a background.", "Warning"); return end
        local entry = savedByName[savedDropdown.Value]
        if not entry then notifyBackground("Select a saved background first.", "Warning"); return end
        if type(delfile) ~= "function" or type(listfiles) ~= "function" then
            notifyBackground("This executor cannot delete background files.", "Error"); return
        end
        local ok, failure = pcall(function()
            local nextEntries = {}
            for _, saved in ipairs(entries) do if saved.Id ~= entry.Id then nextEntries[#nextEntries + 1] = saved end end
            local previousSelection = librarySettings.Selected
            if librarySettings.Selected == entry.Id then librarySettings.Selected = "" end
            local saved, failure = pcall(persistLibrary, nextEntries)
            if not saved then librarySettings.Selected = previousSelection; error(failure, 0) end
            entries = nextEntries
            if Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value == "saved://" .. entry.Id then setBackgroundUrl("", false) end
            refreshLibrary()
            discardImportFiles(entry.Id)
        end)
        notifyBackground(ok and "Selected background deleted." or "Could not finish deleting: " .. tostring(failure):sub(1, 140), ok and "Success" or "Error")
    end })
    section:Button({ Name = "Refresh background list"; Callback = function()
        if savingBackground then notifyBackground("Cancel the current import before refreshing the list.", "Warning"); return end
        entries = readLibraryEntries()
        refreshLibrary()
        syncMediaSelection(Lumen.Flags.SyllinseBackgroundUrl.Value or "")
        notifyBackground("Background list refreshed.", "Success")
    end })


    -- Appearance edits also recolor the footer and the subtle native backdrop.
    local originalTypography = SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelTypography
    SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelTypography = function(...)
        originalTypography(...)
        local panelTransparency = SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparency or (1 - panelContrast / 100)
        for _, card in ipairs(Lumen.Sections or {}) do
            if card.Frame then
                card.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = panelTransparency
                SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelDivider(card.Frame, panelTransparency)
            end
        end
        footerBar.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
        footerTopFill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
        footerDivider.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider
        description.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelText
        syncShellBackground()
    end
    libraryReady = true
    extraColors("Original")
    SyllinseRuntime.refreshAppearance()
    refreshMedia()
    local restoredSelection = librarySettings.Selected
    for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
        if entry.Id == restoredSelection then setBackgroundUrl("saved://" .. entry.Id, false); break end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, failure = pcall(backgroundStorage.MigrateOldFiles)
        if not ok then warn("[Syllinse backgrounds] Old cache migration: " .. tostring(failure)) end
    end)

end
SyllinseRuntime.initializeThemes()
SyllinseRuntime.initializeThemes = nil

-- Independent, bounded playback for the small open/close button.
SyllinseRuntime.createButtonMediaPlayer = function(result, alive)
    local provider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
    local state = { Pages = {}; Loading = {}; RetryAt = {}; Time = 0; Opacity = 1; Enabled = false; }
    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.Name = "SyllinseButtonMedia"
    holder.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    holder.ClipsDescendants = true
    holder.Visible = false
    holder.ZIndex = 200
    holder.Parent = launcher
    setManualCorner(holder, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Launcher)
    state.Frame = holder
    local destroyed, connection, video = false, nil, nil
    local function living() return not destroyed and holder.Parent ~= nil and alive() end
    local function image(asset)
        local label = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        label.Name = "ButtonMediaPage"
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.BorderSizePixel = 0
        label.Image = asset
        label.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        label.ImageTransparency = 0.96
        label.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Default
        label.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-4, -4)
        label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
        label.ZIndex = 200
        label.Parent = holder
        setManualCorner(label, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Launcher)
        return label
    end
    local function fetch(label)
        local done, success, message = false, false, nil
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, failure = pcall(function()
                provider:PreloadAsync({ label }, function(_, status)
                    if status == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then success = true end
                end)
            end)
            message, done = not ok and failure or nil, true
        end)
        local timeout = os.clock() + 20
        while living() and not done and not label.IsLoaded and os.clock() < timeout do task.wait(0.05) end
        assert(living(), "Button load cancelled")
        assert(label.IsLoaded or (done and success), tostring(message or "Button image did not load"))
    end
    function state:SetOpacity(opacity)
        self.Opacity = math.clamp(opacity, 0, 1)
        if self.Current then self.Current.ImageTransparency = 1 - self.Opacity end
        if self.Display then self.Display.VideoTransparency = 1 - self.Opacity end
    end
    function state:Destroy()
        if destroyed then return end
        destroyed = true
        if connection then connection:Disconnect(); connection = nil end
        if video then pcall(function() video:Pause() end) end
        holder:Destroy()
    end
    local step
    function state:SetEnabled(enabled)
        enabled = enabled == true
        if self.Enabled == enabled then return end
        self.Enabled = enabled
        if video then
            if self.Enabled then video:Play() else video:Pause() end
        elseif step then
            if self.Enabled and not connection then connection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(step)
            elseif not self.Enabled and connection then connection:Disconnect(); connection = nil end
        end
    end
    local ok, failure = pcall(function()
        if result.Kind == "video" then
            local display = Instance.new("VideoDisplay")
            display.Name = "ButtonVideo"
            display.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            display.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            display.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
            display.ZIndex = 200
            display.VideoTransparency = 1
            display.Parent = holder
            setManualCorner(display, SYLLINSE_MANUAL_STYLE.Corners.Launcher)
            video = Instance.new("VideoPlayer")
            video.VideoContent = Content.fromUri(result.Asset)
            video.Looping, video.Volume = true, 0
            video.Parent = display
            local wire = Instance.new("Wire")
            wire.SourceInstance, wire.TargetInstance = video, display
            wire.Parent = display
            local loaded, status, done
            task.spawn(function() loaded, status = pcall(video.LoadAsync, video);done = true end)
            local timeout = os.clock() + 25
            while living() and not done and os.clock() < timeout do task.wait(0.05) end
            assert(living(), "Button load cancelled")
            assert(loaded and status == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success, "The Roblox video could not load. Check its asset access")
            state.Display = display
        elseif result.Kind == "photo" then
            local label = image(result.Asset)
            fetch(label)
            label.Position, label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            state.Current = label
        else
            assert(result.Kind == "animation", "Unsupported button media")
            local asset = result.Asset
            local cells = asset.Columns * asset.Rows
            local pages = asset.PageCount or #asset.Pages
            local function loadPage(page)
                if state.Pages[page] then return state.Pages[page] end
                local uri = SyllinseRuntime.buttonMediaService.Page(asset, page, living)
                assert(living(), "Button load cancelled")
                local label = image(uri)
                label.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(asset.CellWidth, asset.CellHeight)
                local loaded, reason = pcall(fetch, label)
                if not loaded then label:Destroy(); error(reason, 0) end
                state.Pages[page] = label
                return label
            end
            local function request(page)
                if state.Pages[page] or state.Loading[page] or os.clock() < (state.RetryAt[page] or 0) then return end
                state.Loading[page] = true
                task.spawn(function()
                    local loaded, reason = pcall(loadPage, page)
                    state.Loading[page] = nil
                    if not loaded and living() then
                        state.RetryAt[page] = os.clock() + 2
                        warn("[Syllinse button] Retrying animation page: " .. tostring(reason))
                    end
                end)
            end
            local function frameAt(time)
                if not asset.FrameEnds then return math.min(asset.FrameCount - 1, math.floor(time * asset.Fps)) end
                local low, high = 1, #asset.FrameEnds
                while low < high do
                    local middle = math.floor((low + high) / 2)
                    if time < asset.FrameEnds[middle] then high = middle else low = middle + 1 end
                end
                return low - 1
            end
            local function draw(frame)
                local page, cell = math.floor(frame / cells) + 1, frame % cells
                local label = state.Pages[page]
                if not label then request(page); return false end
                -- Crop within a single frame; never stretch the source proportions.
                local width, height = asset.CellWidth, asset.CellHeight
                local side = math.min(width, height)
                label.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(side, side)
                label.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new((cell % asset.Columns) * width + math.floor((width - side) / 2),
                    math.floor(cell / asset.Columns) * height + math.floor((height - side) / 2))
                if state.Current ~= label then
                    local outgoing = state.Current
                    label.Position, label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    if outgoing and state.Prepared ~= label then
                        label.ImageTransparency, label.ZIndex = 0.98, 199
                        state.Prepared = label
                        return false
                    end
                    state.Prepared = nil
                    label.ImageTransparency = 1 - state.Opacity
                    label.ZIndex = 200
                    state.Current = label
                    if outgoing then
                        outgoing.ImageTransparency = 1
                        outgoing.Position, outgoing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(-4, -4), UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
                    end
                end
                local nextPage = page % pages + 1
                request(nextPage)
                for index, old in pairs(state.Pages) do
                    if index ~= page and index ~= nextPage then old:Destroy(); state.Pages[index] = nil end
                end
                return true
            end
            loadPage(1)
            draw(0)
            step = function(delta)
                if not living() or not state.Enabled then return end
                local duration = asset.Duration or asset.FrameCount / asset.Fps
                local time = state.PendingTime or (state.Time + delta) % duration
                if draw(frameAt(time)) then state.Time, state.PendingTime = time, nil else state.PendingTime = time end
            end
        end
    end)
    if not ok then state:Destroy(); error(failure, 0) end
    return state
end


SyllinseRuntime.initializeButtonAppearance = function()
    local http = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local api = assert(SyllinseRuntime.buttonMediaService)
    local section = SyllinseRuntime.settingsButton
    local path = "Syllinse/Button/settings.json"
    -- Existing settings can still reference a preset from the former dropdown.
    local presets = {}
    for _, preset in ipairs(Theme.Setup.Presets) do
        presets[preset.Id] = preset
    end
    local state = { Preset = "Follow", Fill = Theme.Root, Stroke = Theme.Stroke, Media = "" }
    local controls, byName, byUrl = {}, {}, {}
    local syncing, restoring, applying = false, true, false
    local generation, saveGeneration, active, loading = 0, 0, nil, false
    local function notify(message, kind)
        SyllinseRuntime.notify("Open/Close Button", message, kind or "Info", 4)
    end
    local function validNumber(n, min, max) return type(n) == "number" and n == n and n >= min and n <= max end
    local function color(rgb)
        assert(type(rgb) == "table" and #rgb == 3, "Invalid button color")
        for _, n in ipairs(rgb) do assert(validNumber(n, 0, 1), "Invalid button color") end
        return Color3.new(rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3])
    end
    local function validate(data)
        assert(type(data) == "table" and data.Version == 1, "Invalid button appearance")
        assert(data.Preset == "Follow" or data.Preset == "Custom" or presets[data.Preset], "Unknown button theme")
        color(data.Color); color(data.Background)
        assert(type(data.Media) == "string" and #data.Media <= 72 and (data.Media == "" or data.Media:match("^saved://[%w_-]+$")), "Invalid button background")
        assert(validNumber(data.Opacity, 0, 100), "Invalid button opacity")
        return data
    end
    local function snapshot()
        -- Keep the original save keys so existing button settings still load.
        return { Version = 1, Preset = state.Preset, Color = { state.Stroke.R, state.Stroke.G, state.Stroke.B },
            Background = { state.Fill.R, state.Fill.G, state.Fill.B }, Media = state.Media,
            Opacity = SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.LauncherOpacity or 100 }
    end
    local function record()
        local bytes = http:JSONEncode(snapshot())
        if Lumen.Flags.SyllinseButtonAppearance then Lumen.Flags.SyllinseButtonAppearance.Value = bytes end
        if restoring then return end
        saveGeneration += 1
        local token = saveGeneration
        task.delay(0.5, function()
            if not gui.Parent or token ~= saveGeneration or type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
            local ok, failure = pcall(function()
                assert(type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function", "This executor cannot save button settings")
                for _, folder in ipairs({ "Syllinse", "Syllinse/Button" }) do if not isfolder(folder) then makefolder(folder) end end
                writefile(path .. ".pending", bytes)
                validate(http:JSONDecode(readfile(path .. ".pending")))
                writefile(path, bytes)
                assert(readfile(path) == bytes, "Button settings could not be verified")
                pcall(writefile, path .. ".bak", bytes)
                if type(delfile) == "function" then pcall(delfile, path .. ".pending") end
            end)
            if not ok then warn("[Syllinse button] Could not save appearance: " .. tostring(failure)) end
        end)
    end
    local function palette()
        local preset = presets[state.Preset]
        if state.Preset == "Follow" then return Theme.Root, Theme.Stroke, Theme.Accent end
        if preset then return preset.Root, preset.Border, preset.Accent end
        return state.Fill, state.Stroke, Theme.Accent
    end
    local function syncMedia()
        if not active then return end
        local opacity = (SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.LauncherOpacity or 100) / 100
        active:SetOpacity(opacity)
        active.Frame.Visible = opacity > 0
        active:SetEnabled(launcher.Visible and gui.Enabled and opacity > 0)
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.syncLauncherMediaOpacity = function() syncMedia(); record() end
    SyllinseRuntime.applyLauncherAppearance = function()
        if applying then return end
        applying = true
        local fill, stroke, accent = palette()
        launcher:SetAttribute("ButtonBackgroundColor", fill)
        launcher:SetAttribute("ButtonAccentColor", accent)
        launcher.BackgroundColor3 = launcher:GetAttribute("ButtonHovered")
            and fill:Lerp(accent, 0.07) or fill
        launcherStroke.Color = stroke
        for _, glow in ipairs({ launcherGlow, launcherGlowCyan, launcherGlowViolet }) do glow.ImageColor3 = accent end
        syncing = true
        if controls.Color then controls.Color.Set(fill) end
        if controls.Stroke then controls.Stroke.Set(stroke) end
        syncing, applying = false, false
        syncMedia()
    end
    local function refreshList()
        local names = { "None" }
        table.clear(byName); table.clear(byUrl)
        for _, entry in ipairs(api.List()) do
            local uri = "saved://" .. entry.Id
            byName[entry.Name], byUrl[uri] = uri, entry.Name
            names[#names + 1] = entry.Name
        end
        table.sort(names, function(a, b) if a == b then return false end;if a == "None" then return true end;if b == "None" then return false end;return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
        if controls.Media then
            syncing = true
            controls.Media.UpdateOptions(names)
            controls.Media.Set(byUrl[state.Media] or "None")
            syncing = false
        end
        return names
    end
    local function selectMedia(url)
        generation += 1
        local token = generation
        if url == state.Media then
            loading = false
            if controls.Media then
                syncing = true
                controls.Media.Set(byUrl[state.Media] or "None")
                syncing = false
            end
            record()
            return
        end
        loading = true
        local committed = false
        local function alive() return gui.Parent ~= nil and (committed or token == generation) end
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, candidate = pcall(function()
                assert(alive(), "Button load cancelled")
                if url ~= "" then return SyllinseRuntime.createButtonMediaPlayer(api.Resolve(url), alive) end
            end)
            if not alive() then
                if ok and candidate then candidate:Destroy() end
                return
            end
            loading = false
            if not ok then
                refreshList()
                notify("Could not load button background. " .. tostring(candidate):sub(1, 160), "Error")
                return
            end
            local previous = active
            committed = true
            active, state.Media = candidate, url
            syncMedia()
            if previous then
                previous:SetEnabled(false)
                if active then task.delay(0.12, function() previous:Destroy() end) else previous:Destroy() end
            end
            refreshList()
            record()
        end)
    end
    syncing = true
    controls.Media = SyllinseRuntime.addReptillianDropdown(section, {
        Name = "Button Background", Options = refreshList(), Value = "None", Search = true,
        Callback = function(name) if not syncing then selectMedia(byName[name] or "") end end,
    })
    controls.Color = section:Label({ Text = "Button Color" }):Colorpicker({ Title = "Button Color", Color = Theme.Root,
        Callback = function(value)
            if syncing then return end
            local _, stroke = palette()
            state.Preset, state.Fill, state.Stroke = "Custom", value, stroke
            SyllinseRuntime.applyLauncherAppearance();record()
        end })
    controls.Stroke = section:Label({ Text = "Stroke Color" }):Colorpicker({ Title = "Button Stroke Color", Color = Theme.Stroke,
        Callback = function(value)
            if syncing then return end
            local fill = palette()
            state.Preset, state.Fill, state.Stroke = "Custom", fill, value
            SyllinseRuntime.applyLauncherAppearance();record()
        end })
    local opacityControl = SyllinseRuntime.addNumericSlider(section, {
        Name = "Button Opacity",
        Suffix = "%",
        Value = 100,
        Min = 0,
        Max = 100,
        Increment = 1,
        Flag = "SyllinseLauncherOpacity",
        Callback = SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings.ApplyLauncherOpacity,
    })
    local function restore(data)
        validate(data)
        restoring = true
        state.Preset, state.Fill, state.Stroke = data.Preset, color(data.Background), color(data.Color)
        SyllinseRuntime.applyLauncherAppearance()
        opacityControl.Set(data.Opacity)
        restoring = false
        selectMedia(data.Media)
    end
    Lumen.RegisterFlag("SyllinseButtonAppearance", { Value = http:JSONEncode(snapshot()), Set = function(bytes)
        local ok, failure = pcall(function() assert(type(bytes) == "string" and #bytes <= 16384, "Invalid button appearance");restore(http:JSONDecode(bytes)) end)
        if not ok then notify("Button appearance could not be restored: " .. tostring(failure):sub(1, 120), "Error") end
    end })
    api.Listeners[#api.Listeners + 1] = function()
        refreshList()
        if state.Media ~= "" and not byUrl[state.Media] then
            selectMedia("")
            notify("The saved button background was removed from the library.", "Warning")
        end
    end
    launcher:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(syncMedia)
    gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(syncMedia)
    gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        generation += 1
        if active then active:Destroy() end
    end)
    syncing = false
    SyllinseRuntime.applyLauncherAppearance()
    if type(readfile) == "function" then
        for _, file in ipairs({ path, path .. ".bak" }) do
            local ok, data = pcall(function() local bytes = readfile(file);assert(#bytes <= 16384);return validate(http:JSONDecode(bytes)) end)
            if ok then restore(data);break end
        end
    end
    restoring = false
    if not loading then record() end
end
SyllinseRuntime.initializeButtonAppearance()
SyllinseRuntime.initializeButtonAppearance = nil


-- User themes reuse the background library's saved media and keep their own palette snapshots.
SyllinseRuntime.initializeCustomThemes = function()
    local http = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local path = "Syllinse/Themes/index.json"
    local presetIds, reservedNames = {}, { none = true }
    for _, preset in ipairs(Theme.Setup.Presets) do
        presetIds[preset.Id] = true
        reservedNames[(preset.Name or preset.Id):lower()] = true
    end
    local colorFields = {
        { "ThemeWindow", "Root" }, { "ThemePanels", "Surface" },
        { "ThemeAccent", "Accent" }, { "ThemeStroke", "Stroke" },
        { "ThemeText", "Text" }, { "ThemeSubtext", "Muted" },
        { "ThemeTabIcons", "TabIcons" }, { "ThemeActiveTabIcon", "ActiveTabIcon" },
    }
    local function notify(message, kind)
        if SyllinseRuntime.showNotification then
            SyllinseRuntime.showNotification({ Title = "Custom Themes"; Content = message; Type = kind or "Info"; Duration = 4; })
        else
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Custom Themes", message, kind or "Info", 4)
        end
    end
    local function validNumber(value, minimum, maximum)
        return type(value) == "number" and value == value and value >= minimum and value <= maximum
    end
    local function themeName(value, allowBuiltinName)
        if type(value) ~= "string" then return nil end
        local name = value:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local length = utf8.len(name)
        if not length or length < 1 or length > 48 or name:find("[%z\1-\31\127]") then return nil end
        local key = name:lower()
        if key == "none" or (reservedNames[key] and not allowBuiltinName) then return nil end
        return name
    end
    local function validTheme(entry)
        if type(entry) ~= "table" then return false end
        local name = themeName(entry.Name, true)
        if not name or name ~= entry.Name then return false end
        if not presetIds[entry.Preset] then return false end
        if type(entry.Colors) ~= "table" then return false end
        for _, field in ipairs(colorFields) do
            local rgb = entry.Colors[field[1]]
            if type(rgb) ~= "table" or #rgb ~= 3 then return false end
            for index = 1, 3 do if not validNumber(rgb[index], 0, 1) then return false end end
        end
        -- Only recognized color flags can be applied from disk.
        local count = 0
        for _ in pairs(entry.Colors) do count += 1 end
        if count ~= #colorFields then return false end
        if not validNumber(entry.ModuleTransparency, 0, 100) then return false end
        if entry.PanelDivider ~= "Filled" and entry.PanelDivider ~= "Line" then return false end
        local background = entry.Background
        return type(background) == "table" and type(background.Url) == "string" and #background.Url <= 8192
            and type(background.Enabled) == "boolean" and validNumber(background.Opacity, 0, 100)
    end
    local function decode(bytes)
        assert(type(bytes) == "string" and #bytes <= 512 * 1024, "Theme library is too large")
        local document = http:JSONDecode(bytes)
        assert(type(document) == "table" and document.Version == 1 and type(document.Entries) == "table"
            and #document.Entries <= 100, "Invalid theme library")
        local seen = {}
        for key in pairs(document.Entries) do
            assert(type(key) == "number" and key % 1 == 0 and key >= 1 and key <= #document.Entries, "Invalid theme entries")
        end
        for _, entry in ipairs(document.Entries) do
            assert(validTheme(entry) and not seen[entry.Name:lower()], "Invalid saved theme")
            seen[entry.Name:lower()] = true
        end
        return document.Entries
    end
    local function readEntries()
        if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return {} end
        local found = false
        for _, candidate in ipairs({ path, path .. ".bak" }) do
            local present = type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(candidate)
            if type(isfile) == "function" and not present then continue end
            local ok, bytes = pcall(readfile, candidate)
            if ok then
                found = true
                local parsed, entries = pcall(decode, bytes)
                if parsed then return entries end
            elseif present then
                found = true
            end
        end
        return {}, found and "The saved theme library could not be read. Its files have been kept." or nil
    end
    local entries, storageError = readEntries()
    local byName = {}
    local dropdown = SyllinseRuntime.customThemesDropdown
    local saving = false
    local returnAppearance, activeName, applying
    local function setSelection(name)
        SyllinseRuntime.customThemeSyncing = true
        dropdown.Set(name)
        SyllinseRuntime.customThemeSyncing = false
    end
    local function refresh(selected)
        local names = {}
        table.clear(byName)
        for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
            names[#names + 1] = entry.Name
            byName[entry.Name] = entry
        end
        table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
        table.insert(names, 1, "None")
        SyllinseRuntime.customThemeSyncing = true
        dropdown.UpdateOptions(names)
        dropdown.Set(byName[selected] and selected or "None")
        SyllinseRuntime.customThemeSyncing = false
    end
    local function persist(nextEntries)
        assert(not storageError, storageError)
        assert(type(readfile) == "function" and type(writefile) == "function"
            and type(isfolder) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function", "This executor cannot save custom themes")
        for _, folder in ipairs({ "Syllinse", "Syllinse/Themes" }) do
            if not isfolder(folder) then makefolder(folder) end
        end
        local bytes = http:JSONEncode({ Version = 1; Entries = nextEntries; })
        assert(#bytes <= 512 * 1024, "The custom theme library is full")
        writefile(path .. ".pending", bytes)
        assert(readfile(path .. ".pending") == bytes, "Could not verify the saved theme")
        decode(bytes)
        local previousOk, previous = pcall(readfile, path)
        if previousOk and pcall(decode, previous) then writefile(path .. ".bak", previous) end
        writefile(path, bytes)
        assert(readfile(path) == bytes, "Could not finish saving the theme")
        pcall(writefile, path .. ".bak", bytes)
        if type(delfile) == "function" then pcall(delfile, path .. ".pending") end
    end
    local function captureAppearance(background)
        local snapshot = {
            Preset = SyllinseRuntime.activeAppearancePreset or "Original"; Colors = {};
            ModuleTransparency = math.clamp((SyllinseRuntime.moduleTransparency or 0) * 100, 0, 100);
            PanelDivider = SyllinseRuntime.panelDividerStyle; Background = background;
        }
        for _, field in ipairs(colorFields) do
            local color = Theme[field[2]]
            snapshot.Colors[field[1]] = { color.R, color.G, color.B }
        end
        return snapshot
    end
    local function applyAppearance(snapshot)
        local ready, reason = SyllinseRuntime.checkThemeBackground(snapshot.Background)
        if not ready then return false, reason end
        applying = true
        local ok, failure = pcall(function()
            SyllinseRuntime.restoreThemeAppearance(snapshot)
            SyllinseRuntime.restoreThemeBackground(snapshot.Background)
        end)
        applying = false
        SyllinseRuntime.appearanceBatch = false
        return ok, failure
    end
    local function restorePrevious()
        if returnAppearance then
            local background = SyllinseRuntime.captureThemeBackground()
            local current = background and captureAppearance(background) or byName[activeName]
            local ok, failure = applyAppearance(returnAppearance)
            if not ok then
                if current then applyAppearance(current) end
                setSelection(activeName or "None")
                notify("Could not restore the previous appearance: " .. tostring(failure):sub(1, 140), "Error")
                return false
            end
        end
        returnAppearance, activeName = nil, nil
        setSelection("None")
        return true
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.clearCustomThemeSelection = function()
        -- An explicit built-in theme starts a new baseline; internal restores retain it.
        if applying then return end
        returnAppearance, activeName = nil, nil
        setSelection("None")
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.selectCustomTheme = function(name)
        if saving then setSelection(activeName or "None"); return end
        if name == "None" then restorePrevious(); return end
        local entry = byName[name]
        if not entry then setSelection(activeName or "None"); return end
        local ready, reason = SyllinseRuntime.checkThemeBackground(entry.Background)
        if not ready then
            setSelection(activeName or "None")
            notify(reason, "Warning")
            return
        end
        local background, warning = SyllinseRuntime.captureThemeBackground()
        if not returnAppearance and not background then
            setSelection(activeName or "None")
            notify(warning, "Warning")
            return
        end
        local current = background and captureAppearance(background) or byName[activeName]
        local ok, failure = applyAppearance(entry)
        if ok then
            returnAppearance = returnAppearance or current
            activeName = entry.Name
            setSelection(entry.Name)
        else
            if current then applyAppearance(current) end
            setSelection(activeName or "None")
            notify("Could not apply the theme: " .. tostring(failure):sub(1, 140), "Error")
        end
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.saveCustomTheme = function()
        if saving then notify("A theme is already being saved.", "Info"); return end
        local input = SyllinseRuntime.themeNameInput
        local value = type(input.GetText) == "function" and input.GetText() or input.Value
        local name = themeName(value)
        if not name then
            notify("Enter a theme name of 1–48 characters. None and built-in theme names are reserved.", "Warning")
            return
        end
        for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
            if entry.Name:lower() == name:lower() then
                notify("The theme name '" .. name .. "' is already used. Choose a different name.", "Warning")
                return
            end
        end
        if #entries >= 100 then notify("The custom theme library has reached 100 themes.", "Warning"); return end
        local background, reason = SyllinseRuntime.captureThemeBackground()
        if not background then notify(reason, "Warning"); return end
        local ready, warning = SyllinseRuntime.checkThemeBackground(background)
        if not ready then notify(warning, "Warning"); return end
        local entry = captureAppearance(background)
        entry.Name = name
        if not validTheme(entry) then notify("The current appearance could not be saved.", "Error"); return end
        local nextEntries = table.clone(entries)
        nextEntries[#nextEntries + 1] = entry
        saving = true
        local ok, failure = pcall(persist, nextEntries)
        saving = false
        if not ok then notify("Could not save the theme: " .. tostring(failure):sub(1, 140), "Error"); return end
        entries = nextEntries
        returnAppearance = returnAppearance or entry
        activeName = name
        refresh(name)
        input.Set("")
        notify("Saved '" .. name .. "' with its background.", "Success")
    end
    SyllinseRuntime.deleteCustomTheme = function()
        if saving then notify("A theme is already being saved.", "Info"); return end
        local entry = byName[activeName]
        if not entry then notify("Select a saved theme to delete.", "Warning"); return end
        if returnAppearance then
            local ready, reason = SyllinseRuntime.checkThemeBackground(returnAppearance.Background)
            if not ready then notify(reason, "Warning"); return end
        end
        local nextEntries = {}
        for _, item in ipairs(entries) do
            if item ~= entry then nextEntries[#nextEntries + 1] = item end
        end
        saving = true
        local ok, failure = pcall(persist, nextEntries)
        if ok and not restorePrevious() then
            local rolledBack, rollbackFailure = pcall(persist, entries)
            saving = false
            if not rolledBack then
                entries = nextEntries
                returnAppearance, activeName = nil, nil
                refresh("None")
                notify("The theme was deleted, but its previous appearance could not be restored: "
                    .. tostring(rollbackFailure):sub(1, 100), "Error")
            end
            return
        end
        saving = false
        if not ok then notify("Could not delete the theme: " .. tostring(failure):sub(1, 140), "Error"); return end
        entries = nextEntries
        refresh("None")
        notify("Deleted '" .. entry.Name .. "'.", "Success")
    end
    refresh("None")
    if storageError then notify(storageError, "Warning") end
end
SyllinseRuntime.initializeCustomThemes()
SyllinseRuntime.initializeCustomThemes = nil

-- Apply theme links once more after every page/section/control has been constructed.
-- Lumen registers many native widget surfaces lazily as each control is created.
Lumen.ApplyTheme()

-- Now every native section exists; restore base layout values and apply the current
-- native geometry scale so text is rendered at its real TextSize, not enlarged by UIScale.
refreshNativeResponsiveLayout()

SyllinseRuntime.applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)
SyllinseRuntime.applyFieldStyles()

-- Live feature search across Lumen's real searchable control registry. Category prefixes
-- navigate to the matching Syllinse tab; all other terms filter Lumen rows in place.
SyllinseRuntime.startsWith = function(text, prefix)
    return text:sub(1, #prefix) == prefix
end
SyllinseRuntime.filterLumenControls = function(query)
    query = string.lower(query or "")
    local sectionMatches = {}
    local pageMatches = {}

    if query == "" then
        for _, item in ipairs(Lumen.Searchable or {}) do
            if item.Frame and item.Frame.Parent then item.Frame.Visible = true end
        end
        for _, section in ipairs(Lumen.Sections or {}) do
            if section.Frame and section.Frame.Parent then section.Frame.Visible = true end
        end
        return
    end

    for _, section in ipairs(Lumen.Sections or {}) do
        local matched = string.find(string.lower(tostring(section.Name or "")), query, 1, true) ~= nil
        sectionMatches[section] = matched
        if section.Frame and section.Frame.Parent then section.Frame.Visible = matched end
        if matched and section.PageFrame then
            local tabName = section.PageFrame:GetAttribute("SyllinseTabName")
            if tabName then pageMatches[tabName] = true end
        end
    end

    for _, item in ipairs(Lumen.Searchable or {}) do
        local frame = item.Frame
        if frame and frame.Parent then
            local section = item.Section
            local text = string.lower(tostring(item.Text or ""))
            local match = string.find(text, query, 1, true) ~= nil or (section and sectionMatches[section] == true)
            frame.Visible = match
            if match and section and section.Frame and section.Frame.Parent then
                section.Frame.Visible = true
            end
            if match and section and section.PageFrame then
                local tabName = section.PageFrame:GetAttribute("SyllinseTabName")
                if tabName then pageMatches[tabName] = true end
            end
        end
    end

    local currentHasMatch = pageMatches[activeName] == true
    if not currentHasMatch then
        for _, item in ipairs(navItems) do
            if pageMatches[item.Name] then
                setActiveTab(item.Name)
                break
            end
        end
    end
end

searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    local query = string.lower((searchBox.Text or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or "")
    if query == "" then
        currentSearchMode = "none"
        SyllinseRuntime.filterLumenControls("")
        return
    end

    local tabPrefix
    for _, item in ipairs(navItems) do
        local name = string.lower(item.Name)
        if SyllinseRuntime.startsWith(name, query) then
            tabPrefix = item.Name
            break
        end
    end
    if tabPrefix then
        currentSearchMode = "tab"
        SyllinseRuntime.filterLumenControls("")
        if activeName ~= tabPrefix then setActiveTab(tabPrefix) end
    else
        currentSearchMode = "controls"
        SyllinseRuntime.filterLumenControls(query)
    end
end)

-- Export the native library, page/section handles and settings so feature code can
-- attach to the same native Lumen controls without using a second widget library.
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment = _G
if type(getgenv) == "function" then
    -- Keep temporary variables inside this callback so they do not consume
    -- registers in the already-large top-level script chunk.
    pcall(function()
        local environment = getgenv()
        if type(environment) == "table" then
            SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment = environment
        end
    end)
elseif type(shared) == "table" then
    SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment = shared
end
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.Lumen = Lumen
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseLumenWindow = LumenWindow
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseLumenPages = SyllinseRuntime.pages
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseTabs = lumenPages
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseFeatureSettings = SyllinseRuntime.featureSettings
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseRoot = root
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseScreenGui = gui
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseNotify = SyllinseRuntime.notify
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseSetFeature = function(flag, value)
    if type(flag) ~= "string" then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function() Lumen.SetFlag(flag, value) end)
    return ok
end
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseSetMobileActionButtons = SyllinseRuntime.setMobileActionButtonsVisible
SyllinseRuntime.SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseSetMobileKeybindButton = SyllinseRuntime.setMobileActionButtonVisibility

-- Correct initial tab visibility after all native sections have been mounted.
for name, page in pairs(lumenPages) do
    if page.Frame then page.Frame.Visible = false end
end
setActiveTab("Combat");
-- Load the chosen profile after all controls/flags are registered.
-- A do-block gives startup-only locals their own scope without ambiguous call syntax.
do
    local startupAutoload = getAutoloadName()
    if startupAutoload then
        local ok, loaded = pcall(function() return Lumen.LoadConfig(startupAutoload) end)
        if ok and loaded then
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Autoloaded '" .. startupAutoload .. "'.", "Success", 2)
        else
            SyllinseRuntime.notify("Configs", "Autoload failed for '" .. startupAutoload .. "'.", "Warning", 2)
        end
    end
end
SyllinseRuntime.notify("Syllinse", "Lumen controls loaded.", "Success", 2)

-- Keep the backdrop outside the CanvasGroup so it renders directly at window resolution.
-- Both layers still move and scale together under the root.
for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
    if child ~= motionBody and child.Name ~= "SyllinseThemeBackdrop" and child:IsA("GuiObject") then
        child.Parent = motionBody
    end
end
motionBody.GroupTransparency = 0
refreshLauncherVisibility()

-- Open the initial shell with the Syllinse launcher-summon animation.
setWindowOpen(true)

-- Toggle the Syllinse main window with the user-configured key. Keep Lumen's
-- backend/overlay ScreenGuis enabled so watermark, keybind list, and toasts stay visible.
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    -- The next input after clicking the key selector is the new key; don't toggle the UI
    -- while Lumen is capturing that input for rebinding.
    if SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyListening then
        SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKeyListening = false
        return
    end
    if gameProcessed then return end

    local matchesToggleKey = input.KeyCode == SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKey or input.UserInputType == SyllinseRuntime.uiToggleKey
    if matchesToggleKey then
        setWindowOpen(not windowIsOpen)
    end
end)

if playerGui:GetAttribute("SyllinseReadyLogShown") ~= true then
    playerGui:SetAttribute("SyllinseReadyLogShown", true)
    print("[Syllinse] Lumen integration ready")
end
